function campaignCycleStage(stage=meta.stage||1){return ((Math.max(1,stage)-1)%15)+1}

function campaignStageProfile(stage=meta.stage||1){
  const c=Math.min(10,campaignCycleStage(stage));
  const table={
    1:{fodder:1.08,special:.72,speed:1.00,bossHp:1.00,bossDmg:1.00},
    2:{fodder:1.05,special:.80,speed:1.00,bossHp:1.01,bossDmg:1.00},
    3:{fodder:1.00,special:.90,speed:1.01,bossHp:1.03,bossDmg:1.01},
    4:{fodder:.96,special:1.00,speed:1.02,bossHp:1.06,bossDmg:1.02},
    5:{fodder:.91,special:1.14,speed:1.03,bossHp:1.17,bossDmg:1.07},
    6:{fodder:.94,special:1.08,speed:1.035,bossHp:1.07,bossDmg:1.03},
    7:{fodder:.90,special:1.18,speed:1.045,bossHp:1.10,bossDmg:1.04},
    8:{fodder:.86,special:1.30,speed:1.055,bossHp:1.13,bossDmg:1.055},
    9:{fodder:.82,special:1.43,speed:1.065,bossHp:1.17,bossDmg:1.075},
    10:{fodder:.78,special:1.58,speed:1.075,bossHp:1.31,bossDmg:1.14}
  };
  return table[c]||table[1];
}
function stageThreatScale(stage=meta.stage||1){let n=Math.max(0,stage-1),c=campaignCycleStage(stage),milestone=c===10?.10:c===5?.05:0;return 1+Math.min(.44,n*.038)+Math.min(.16,Math.floor(n/5)*.018)+milestone}

function memoryRank(kind){return Math.max(0,meta.memoryTraits?.[kind]||0)}
function memoryActiveRank(kind){return meta.activeMemory===kind?memoryRank(kind):0}
function memoryDamageMul(kind){
  const active=meta.activeMemory,r=memoryRank(active);if(!r)return 1;
  if(active==='arm'&&(kind==='arm'||kind==='artillery'))return 1+r*.03;
  if(active==='mouth'&&kind==='acid')return 1+r*.03;
  if(active==='tail'&&kind==='tail')return 1+r*.03;
  return 1;
}
function memoryActiveCdMult(){let r=memoryActiveRank('speed');return r?Math.max(.88,1-r*.02):1}
function applyMemoryTrait(){
  const active=meta.activeMemory,r=memoryRank(active);if(!r)return;
  if(active==='core'){player.dmgMul*=1+r*.02;player.armor=Math.min(.52,(player.armor||0)+r*.01)}
  else if(active==='speed')player.speed*=1+r*.02;
}
function awardMemoryTrait(kind){
  if(!MEMORY_TRAITS[kind])kind='core';
  const before=memoryRank(kind),after=Math.min(5,before+1);meta.memoryTraits[kind]=after;
  if(meta.activeMemory==='none')meta.activeMemory=kind;
  discover('memory:'+kind);saveMeta();renderMemoryArchive();
  if(after>before)flash('MEMORY EXTRACTED · '+MEMORY_TRAITS[kind].name+' Lv.'+after,1250);
}
function renderMemoryArchive(){
  const wrap=$('memoryGrid'),sum=$('memorySummary');if(!wrap||!sum)return;
  const total=Object.values(meta.memoryTraits||{}).reduce((a,b)=>a+(b||0),0);
  sum.textContent='NEMESIS DEFEATS '+(meta.nemesisDefeats||0)+' · MEMORY RANKS '+total+' / 25 · ONE MEMORY MAY BE EQUIPPED';
  wrap.innerHTML='';
  for(const [kind,m] of Object.entries(MEMORY_TRAITS)){
    const rank=memoryRank(kind),b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';
    b.className='memoryCard'+(rank?'':' locked')+(meta.activeMemory===kind?' selected':'');b.disabled=!rank;
    b.innerHTML=`<b>${rank?m.name:'UNKNOWN MEMORY'}</b><span>${rank?m.desc:'Defeat a Nemesis adapted against this mutation.'}</span><em>${rank?'RANK '+rank+' / 5 · '+m.short:'LOCKED'}</em>`;
    b.onclick=()=>{if(!rank)return;meta.activeMemory=kind;saveMeta();renderMemoryArchive();updateLab();beep(720,.08,'triangle',.025);};
    wrap.appendChild(b);
  }
}

function discover(id){
  if(!id||meta.seen.includes(id))return;
  meta.seen.push(id);saveMeta();
}

function renderUnlockTree(){
  const wrap=$('unlockGrid');if(!wrap)return;
  const nodes=[
    {need:0,name:'PRIME',sub:'START'},
    {need:2,name:'RAZOR',sub:'CLEAR 2'},
    {need:4,name:'ACID',sub:'CLEAR 4'},
    {need:5,name:'APEX',sub:'STAGE 5 TROPHY'},
    {need:6,name:'FORGE',sub:'CLEAR 6'},
    {need:10,name:'ABYSS',sub:'OMEGA CLEAR'}
  ];
  const next=nodes.find(x=>(meta.clears||0)<x.need);
  wrap.innerHTML='';
  for(const n of nodes){
    const on=(meta.clears||0)>=n.need,now=!on&&next&&n.need===next.need;
    const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='unlockNode'+(on?' on':'')+(now?' now':'');
    d.innerHTML=`<b>${on?n.name:'LOCKED'}</b><span>${n.sub}</span>`;wrap.appendChild(d);
  }
}
function renderOrigins(){
  if(!originUnlocked(meta.origin))meta.origin='prime';
  const wrap=$('originGrid');if(!wrap)return;wrap.innerHTML='';
  for(const [id,o] of Object.entries(ORIGINS)){
    const unlocked=originUnlocked(id),b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='originCard'+(meta.origin===id?' selected':'');b.disabled=!unlocked;
    b.innerHTML=`<b>${unlocked?o.name:'LOCKED ORGANISM'}</b><span>${unlocked?(o.desc+' · ASCEND: '+o.asc):('CLEAR '+o.unlock+' STAGES TO UNLOCK')}</span>`;
    b.onclick=()=>{if(!originUnlocked(id))return;meta.origin=id;discover('origin:'+id);saveMeta();renderOrigins();beep(620,.06,'triangle',.02);};
    wrap.appendChild(b);
  }
}
function applyOrigin(){
  let id=originUnlocked(meta.origin)?meta.origin:'prime';
  discover('origin:'+id);player.origin=id;player.forgeVolley=0;player.voidTick=0;player.voidKills=0;player.fusionShot=0;
  if(id==='razor'){
    player.speed+=10;player.tailPower=(player.tailPower||1)*1.18;player.tailHaste=(player.tailHaste||0)+.10;player.dashBonus=(player.dashBonus||0)+.18;player.maxHp=Math.max(62,player.maxHp-8);player.hp=player.maxHp;
  }else if(id==='acid'){
    player.acidHaste=(player.acidHaste||0)+.12;player.acidPower=(player.acidPower||1)*1.12;player.pickup+=8;player.maxHp=Math.max(62,player.maxHp-6);player.hp=player.maxHp;
  }else if(id==='forge'){
    player.dmgMul*=1.08;player.speed*=.96;player.baseCd=Math.max(.55,player.baseCd-.03);
  }else if(id==='abyss'){
    player.dmgMul*=1.06;player.armor=Math.min(.48,(player.armor||0)+.04);player.speed-=5;player.maxHp+=10;player.hp=player.maxHp;genes.core=Math.max(genes.core,1);state.core=1+genes.core;
  }else{
    player.regen+=.03;
  }
  renderUnlockTree();
}
const ORIGIN_ACTIVE={
  prime:{name:'ADAPT',glyph:'AD',cd:19},
  razor:{name:'REND',glyph:'RD',cd:17},
  acid:{name:'PLAGUE',glyph:'PL',cd:18},
  forge:{name:'SIEGE',glyph:'SG',cd:21},

};
function updateAbilityUI(){
  const id=player.origin||meta.origin||'prime',info=ORIGIN_ACTIVE[id]||ORIGIN_ACTIVE.prime;
  activeMaxCd=Math.max(5.2,info.cd*(originAscension?.82:1)*(hasOrgan('abyssEye')?.80:1)*memoryActiveCdMult());
  $('abilityName').textContent=info.name;
  const glyph=$('ability').querySelector('.abilityGlyph');if(glyph)glyph.textContent=info.glyph;
  let q=activeMaxCd?clamp(activeReady/activeMaxCd,0,1):0,deg=q*360;
  $('abilityCd').style.background=`conic-gradient(#071116e9 ${deg}deg, transparent ${deg}deg)`;
  $('ability').classList.toggle('ready',activeReady<=0);
  $('ability').classList.toggle('active',activeDuration>0);
}
function originActive(){
  if(!running||paused||choosing||rewardChoosing||ended||activeReady>0)return;
  const id=player.origin||meta.origin||'prime',asc=!!originAscension,core=(1+(state.core-1)*.15)*(player.dmgMul||1);
  let name='';
  if(id==='prime'){
    name='ADAPT BURST';player.hp=Math.min(player.maxHp,player.hp+player.maxHp*(asc?.28:.18));player.inv=asc?1.35:1.0;
    let radius=asc?150:122,damage=(asc?62:38)*core;
    enemyEachCandidateCircleV425(player.x,player.y,radius+40,e=>{if(!e.dead&&dist2(player,e)<(radius+e.r)**2){hurtEnemy(e,damage,'core');let a=Math.atan2(e.y-player.y,e.x-player.x);e.x+=Math.cos(a)*10;e.y+=Math.sin(a)*10;}});
    particles.push({kind:'deathRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:10,max:radius,life:.42,maxLife:.42});
    for(let i=0;i<22;i++)particles.push({kind:'heal',x:player.x,y:player.y,vx:rand(-85,85),vy:rand(-85,85),r:rand(1.5,3.8),life:rand(.25,.55)});
    activeDuration=.55;
  }else if(id==='razor'){
    name='RENDING DANCE';player.inv=asc?.75:.55;dashReady=0;
    let radius=asc?142:118,damage=(asc?72:48)*core*(player.tailPower||1);
    for(let n=0;n<(asc?4:3);n++)particles.push({kind:'slash',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:radius-n*7,life:.38+n*.035,max:.38+n*.035,start:(player.look||0)+n*1.4-1.1,end:(player.look||0)+n*1.4+1.7});
    enemyEachCandidateCircleV425(player.x,player.y,radius+40,e=>{if(!e.dead&&dist2(player,e)<(radius+e.r)**2)hurtEnemy(e,damage,'tail');});
    activeDuration=.7;cameraShake=7;
  }else if(id==='acid'){
    name='PLAGUE BLOOM';let count=asc?24:18,damage=(asc?30:22)*core*(player.acidPower||1);
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){let a=i/count*TAU;bullets.push({x:player.x+Math.cos(a)*10,y:player.y+Math.sin(a)*10,vx:Math.cos(a)*(asc?245:215),vy:Math.sin(a)*(asc?245:215),r:6*PROJECTILE_SCALE,dmg:damage,life:1.5,pierce:asc?3:2,kind:'acid'});}
    {const rr=asc?145:118,rr2=rr*rr;enemyEachCandidateCircleV425(player.x,player.y,rr+32,e=>{if(!e.dead&&dist2(player,e)<rr2)e.slow=Math.max(e.slow||0,asc?2.2:1.5);});}
    for(let i=0;i<26;i++)particles.push({kind:'acid',x:player.x,y:player.y,vx:rand(-110,110),vy:rand(-110,110),r:rand(2,5),life:rand(.25,.65)});
    activeDuration=.65;cameraShake=5;
  }else if(id==='forge'){
    name='SIEGE MODE';activeDuration=asc?5.2:4.0;player.inv=Math.max(player.inv,.28);
    let target=nearest(420),base=target?Math.atan2(target.y-player.y,target.x-player.x):(player.look||0),count=asc?8:6;
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){let a=base+(i-(count-1)/2)*.09;bullets.push({x:player.x+Math.cos(a)*18,y:player.y+Math.sin(a)*18,vx:Math.cos(a)*350,vy:Math.sin(a)*350,r:5*PROJECTILE_SCALE,dmg:(28+state.arm*7)*core,life:1.35,pierce:2,kind:'artillery',blast:asc?30:22});}
    activeDuration=asc?5.2:4.0;cameraShake=8;
  }else if(id==='abyss'){
    name=asc?'BLACK SUN':'GRAVITY WELL';activeDuration=asc?3.4:2.6;player.voidTick=0;player.inv=Math.max(player.inv,asc?.55:.32);cameraShake=asc?10:6;
    particles.push({kind:'voidRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:18,max:asc?184:150,life:.52,maxLife:.52});
    for(let i=0;i<30;i++)particles.push({kind:'void',x:player.x,y:player.y,vx:rand(-105,105),vy:rand(-105,105),r:rand(1.5,4),life:rand(.3,.75)});
  }
  activeMaxCd=Math.max(5.2,(ORIGIN_ACTIVE[id]||ORIGIN_ACTIVE.prime).cd*(originAscension?.82:1)*(hasOrgan('abyssEye')?.80:1)*memoryActiveCdMult());
  activeReady=activeMaxCd;flash(name,800);audioCue('active',id==='prime'?0:id==='razor'?1:id==='acid'?2:id==='forge'?3:4);hapticCue('active');if(firstRunCoach()&&coachStep<=3){coachStep=4;coachHint('TAP AN ENEMY · FOCUS PRIORITY TARGET',4.0);updateCoachVisuals()}updateAbilityUI();
}

function renderCodex(){
  const always=new Set(['mutation:arm','mutation:mouth','mutation:tail','mutation:core','mutation:speed','enemy:crawler']);
  let total=0,known=0,html='';
  for(const [section,entries] of Object.entries(CODEX)){
    html+=`<div class="codexSection"><h3>${section}</h3><div class="codexGrid">`;
    for(const [id,name,desc] of entries){
      total++;let seen=always.has(id)||meta.seen.includes(id);if(seen)known++;
      html+=`<div class="codexEntry${seen?'':' locked'}"><b>${seen?name:'UNKNOWN'}</b><span>${seen?desc:'Encounter or evolve to discover'}</span></div>`;
    }
    html+='</div></div>';
  }
  $('codexSummary').textContent='DISCOVERED '+known+' / '+total+' · ORIGIN '+(ORIGINS[meta.origin]?.name||'PRIME CELL');
  $('codexContent').innerHTML=html;
}

const ROUTES={
  cryo:{name:'CRYO VAULT',sub:'CONTROLLED HUNT',desc:'Cryogenic zone. Enemies move 12% slower, but have 4% more HP. Elite enemies gain 15% HP, and CRYO LOCK restricts the field. Pickup range +8.',mods:'ENEMY SPEED -12% · ELITE HP +15% · PICKUP +8',mastery:'cryoMastery'},
  gene:{name:'GENE TEMPLE',sub:'RECOMBINATION ZONE',desc:'Recombination zone. Enemy HP +10%, elite HP +4%, pickup range +12, XP +12%, DNA +18% (plus route mastery bonuses).',mods:'XP +12% · DNA +18% · ENEMY HP +10%',mastery:'geneMastery'},
  inferno:{name:'INFERNO HIVE',sub:'AGGRESSIVE DOMAIN',desc:'Extreme-heat zone. Player damage +12%, enemy damage +10%, enemy speed +9%, enemy HP +4%, elite HP +8%, and DNA gain +12% (plus route mastery bonuses).',mods:'PLAYER DAMAGE +12% · ENEMY DAMAGE +10% · ENEMY SPEED +9%',mastery:'infernoMastery'}
};
function routeMasteryText(id,lv){
  if(id==='cryo')return 'ARMOR +'+(lv*2)+'% · CRYO SLOW RESIST +'+(lv*8)+'%';
  if(id==='gene')return 'DNA +'+(lv*4)+'% · XP +'+(lv*3)+'%';
  return 'PLAYER DAMAGE +'+(lv*3)+'% · HEAT DAMAGE -'+(lv*3)+'%';
}
function renderCampaignTrack(){
  const el=$('campaignTrack');if(!el)return;const s=Math.max(1,meta.stage||1),c=campaignCycleStage(s),next=nextCampaignMilestone(s),mil=campaignMilestone(s);
  const marks=Array.from({length:15},(_,i)=>`<i class="${i<c?'on ':''}${i===4||i===9?'apex ':''}${i===14?'omega':''}"></i>`).join('');
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="campaignTrackTop"><span><b>CAMPAIGN CYCLE ${campaignCycle(s)}</b> · STAGE ${s}</span><span>${mil?mil+' STAGE':'NEXT '+next.name+' · '+next.stage}</span></div><div class="campaignTrackBar"><div class="campaignTrackFill" style="width:${c/15*100}%"></div></div><div class="campaignTrackMarks">${marks}</div>`;
  if($('heroMilestone'))$('heroMilestone').textContent=mil?(mil+' MILESTONE · STAGE '+s):('NEXT '+next.name+' · STAGE '+next.stage);
}
function grantStageClearReward(){
  const s=meta.stage||1;
  if(meta.claimedStages.includes(s)){stageRewardText='STAGE '+s+' REWARD ALREADY SECURED';return;}
  meta.claimedStages.push(s);
  const cycle=campaignCycle(s),c=campaignCycleStage(s),baseGain=Math.min(110,22+(s-1)*5);meta.dna+=baseGain;
  let parts=['CLEAR CACHE · +'+baseGain+' DNA'];
  if(c===15){
    milestoneBonus=220+Math.min(120,(cycle-1)*20);meta.dna+=milestoneBonus;const geneBefore=meta.apexGenes||0;meta.apexGenes=Math.min(5,geneBefore+1);meta.organLuck=(meta.organLuck||0)+.035;meta.relicLuck=(meta.relicLuck||0)+.015;meta.stageVitality=(meta.stageVitality||0)+5;
    parts.unshift('CHAPTER TROPHY · +'+milestoneBonus+' DNA');parts.push(meta.apexGenes>geneBefore?'APEX GENE +1':'APEX GENE MAX','MAX HP +5','ORGAN +3.5%','RELIC +1.5%');discover('boss:BIO TITAN');
  }else if(c===5||c===10){
    milestoneBonus=105+Math.min(75,(cycle-1)*15);meta.dna+=milestoneBonus;meta.organLuck=(meta.organLuck||0)+.0225;meta.relicLuck=(meta.relicLuck||0)+.0075;
    parts.unshift('BOSS REPEL BONUS · +'+milestoneBonus+' DNA');parts.push(s===5?'EPIC ORGAN ACCESS':'APEX ORGAN QUALITY','ORGAN +2.25%','RELIC +0.75%');discover('boss:BIO TITAN');
  }else if(c===2||c===7){
    meta.organLuck=(meta.organLuck||0)+.0125;parts.push(s===2?'RARE BIO-ORGANS UNLOCKED':'ORGAN QUALITY +1.25%');
  }else if(c===3||c===8){
    meta.stageVitality=(meta.stageVitality||0)+3;parts.push('CELL ARCHIVE · MAX HP +3');
  }else if(c===4||c===9){
    meta.relicLuck=(meta.relicLuck||0)+.0075;parts.push('RELIC SENSE +0.75%');
  }else{
    let extra=12+Math.min(36,cycle*4);meta.dna+=extra;parts.push('MUTAGEN RESERVE +'+extra+' DNA');
  }
  if(c===3||c===6||c===9){const mk=meta.route==='cryo'?'cryoMastery':meta.route==='gene'?'geneMastery':meta.route==='inferno'?'infernoMastery':null;if(mk&&meta[mk]<5){meta[mk]++;parts.push('ROUTE MASTERY +1')}}stageRewardText=parts.join(' · ');saveMeta();updateLab();
}
function showRouteSelect(){
  let rb=$('stageRewardBanner');rb.innerHTML='<b>CLEAR REWARD</b>'+stageRewardText;rb.style.display=stageRewardText?'block':'none';
  let wrap=$('routeCards');wrap.innerHTML='';const nextStage=Math.min(99,(meta.stage||1)+1),nextMil=campaignMilestone(nextStage);
  for(const [id,r] of Object.entries(ROUTES)){
    let b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='routeCard'+(nextMil==='APEX'?' milestoneNext':nextMil==='OMEGA'?' omegaNext':'');b.dataset.route=id;
    let ml=meta[r.mastery]||0,bonus=routeMasteryText(id,ml);
    b.innerHTML=`<div class="routeThumb"></div><h3>${r.name}</h3><p>${r.desc}</p><div class="routeMods">${r.mods}<br>MASTERY Lv.${ml}/5<br>${bonus}<span class="routeThreat">NEXT STAGE · ${stageThreatText(nextStage)}${nextMil?' · '+nextMil+' BOSS':''}</span></div>`;
    b.onclick=()=>{meta.route=id;meta.stage=nextStage;meta.homeStageV522=nextStage;homeSelectedStageV522=nextStage;meta.unlockedStageV522=Math.max(homeUnlockedStageV522(),nextStage);meta.clears=(meta.clears||0)+1;saveMeta();$('routeOverlay').style.display='none';announceEncounter(r.name,(nextMil?nextMil+' · ':'')+'STAGE '+meta.stage+' SELECTED');finish();};
    wrap.appendChild(b);
  }
  $('routeCurrent').textContent='NEXT: STAGE '+nextStage+' · '+(nextMil?nextMil+' MILESTONE · ':'')+'ROUTE MASTERY PERSISTS';
  $('routeOverlay').style.display='flex';
}
function renderNemesisStatus(force=false){
  const panel=$('nemesisPanel'),badge=$('nemesisBadge'),n=meta.nemesis;
  const key=n?[(n.name||''),(n.power||1),(n.riskName||'STANDARD'),(n.adaptKind||'core'),(meta.nemesisDefeats||0)].join('|'):'none';
  if(!force&&renderNemesisStatus._key===key)return;renderNemesisStatus._key=key;
  if(!n){if(panel)panel.style.display='none';if(badge)badge.style.display='none';return;}
  const power=Math.max(1,n.power||1),risk=n.riskName||'STANDARD',adapt=n.adaptKind&&MEMORY_TRAITS[n.adaptKind]?MEMORY_TRAITS[n.adaptKind].short:'UNKNOWN';
  if(panel){panel.style.display='block';panel.innerHTML=`<b>NEMESIS ACTIVE · ${n.name}</b><br>${risk} MEMORY · POWER ${power}/5 · ADAPTED VS ${adapt} · DEFEATS ${meta.nemesisDefeats||0}`;}
  if(badge){badge.style.display='block';badge.innerHTML=`<b>NEMESIS · ${n.name}</b><br>POWER ${power}/5 · ADAPTED VS ${adapt}`;}
}
function recordNemesis(e){
  if(!e)return;
  let raw=(e.contractBossTitle||e.eliteName||e.bossName||'UNKNOWN PREDATOR').replace(/^NEMESIS · /,'');
  let old=meta.nemesis,power=(old&&old.name===raw)?Math.min(5,(old.power||1)+1):1;
  const adaptKind=dominantMutationKind();discover('nemesis:memory');meta.nemesis={
    name:raw,
    contract:e.contractVariant||contract?.id||'exterminate',
    risk:selectedRisk,
    riskName:riskTier().name,
    power,
    sourceType:e.type||'brute',
    adaptKind
  };
  saveMeta();renderNemesisStatus();
}
function spawnNemesis(){
  if(nemesisSpawned||!meta.nemesis||ended||!directorCanStart('nemesis'))return null;directorHold(1.7);
  nemesisSpawned=true;
  const n=meta.nemesis,power=Math.max(1,n.power||1);
  let type=n.contract==='harvest'?'spitter':'brute';
  let e=spawnEnemy(type);
  e.elite=true;e.nemesis=true;e.eliteName='NEMESIS · '+n.name;e.elitePatternCd=1.15;
  e.hp=Math.max(e.hp*(2.4+.38*power),560*stageScale*(1+.18*power));e.maxHp=e.hp;
  e.dmg*=1.10+.07*power;e.spd*=n.contract==='predator'?(1.18+.035*power):(1.05+.02*power);e.xp=160+power*30;e.r+=2+power*.4;
  e.nemesisPower=power;e.adaptKind=n.adaptKind||'core';if(e.adaptKind==='speed')e.spd*=1.15+.02*power;
  if(type==='spitter'){e.shot=Math.min(e.shot||.7,.5);e.strafe=e.strafe||1;}
  const entry=stageMajorArrival(e,'NEMESIS',1.04,'#c47aff');
  $('bossbar').style.display='block';$('bossname').textContent=e.eliteName;$('bossfill').style.width='100%';
  announceEncounter(e.eliteName,entry.side+' APPROACH · POWER '+power+' · ADAPTED VS '+(MEMORY_TRAITS[e.adaptKind]?.short||'CORE'));
  flash('NEMESIS SIGNAL · '+entry.side,1250);cameraShake=9;audioCue('boss');hapticCue('boss');
  return e;
}

function updateLab(){

function enemyAppearanceKeyV516(e){
 const type=typeof e==='string'?e:e?.type||'crawler';
 return e?.visualSetV516==='legacy'&&(type==='crawler'||type==='warden')?'legacy_'+type:type;
}
function enemyAppearanceAssetV516(key){
 return key.startsWith('legacy_')?'enemy_legacy/'+key.slice(7):'enemy/'+(key==='froststalker'?'frost_stalker':key);
}
function assignEnemyAppearanceV516(e,stage){
 if(e.finalBoss||e.type==='boss'||(e.type!=='crawler'&&e.type!=='warden'))return;
 e.visualSetV516=stage>=LEGACY_ENEMY_STAGE_V516&&++enemyAppearanceCountV516[e.type]%2===0?'legacy':'base';
}
function prepareApprovedEnemySurfaceV516(im){
 // Uploaded RGB references have an opaque black exterior. Remove ONLY exact black
 // pixels connected to the image border; colored anatomy and interior dark pixels stay intact.
 // Original PNG bytes are retained verbatim. This surface is made once, before caches.
 const w=im.naturalWidth,h=im.naturalHeight,c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=w;c.height=h;
 const g=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});g.drawImage(im,0,0);
 const data=g.getImageData(0,0,w,h),p=data.data,q=new Uint32Array(w*h);let head=0,tail=0;
 const enqueue=i=>{const j=i*4;if(p[j+3]&&p[j]===0&&p[j+1]===0&&p[j+2]===0){p[j+3]=0;q[tail++]=i;}};
 for(let x=0;x<w;x++){enqueue(x);enqueue((h-1)*w+x);}
 for(let y=1;y<h-1;y++){enqueue(y*w);enqueue(y*w+w-1);}
 while(head<tail){const i=q[head++],x=i%w;if(x>0)enqueue(i-1);if(x<w-1)enqueue(i+1);if(i>=w)enqueue(i-w);if(i<w*(h-1))enqueue(i+w);}
 let left=w,top=h,right=-1,bottom=-1;
 for(let y=0;y<h;y++)for(let x=0;x<w;x++)if(p[(y*w+x)*4+3]>=128){left=Math.min(left,x);top=Math.min(top,y);right=Math.max(right,x);bottom=Math.max(bottom,y);}
 if(right<left)throw new Error('Empty approved enemy silhouette');
 g.putImageData(data,0,0);im._spriteCrop=[left,top,right-left+1,bottom-top+1];
 return c;
}

function maskFromRunsV518(text,length){

}

function ensureXPVisibleBBoxV453(icon,im){

 if(icon.lockedVisibleBBox){icon.fullW=icon.fullW||im.naturalWidth;icon.fullH=icon.fullH||im.naturalHeight;icon._calibrationImage=im;return;}

// V416: every unit is an independently visible, collectible pickup.
function packXPV415(x,y,total,life=26,spread=14){
  let whole=Math.max(0,Math.floor(total));
  const units=[];
  for(const unit of [100,10,1]){
    const count=Math.floor(whole/unit);whole-=count*unit;
    for(let j=0;j<count;j++)units.push(unit);
  }
  const phase=Math.random()*TAU,spacing=Math.max(8,Math.min(12,spread*.55));
  const radius=spacing*Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,units.length-1));
  const cx=clamp(x,12+radius,WORLD_W-12-radius),cy=clamp(y,12+radius,WORLD_H-12-radius);
  for(let i=0;i<units.length;i++){
    const unit=units[i],a=i*2.3999632297+phase,r=spacing*Math.sqrt(i);
    const d={x:cx+Math.cos(a)*r,y:cy+Math.sin(a)*r,r:4,xp:unit,xpUnit:unit,life};resolveTerrainBody(d,4);drops.push(d);
  }
}
function spawnXPV415(x,y,value,life=26,spread=14){

}

function calibrationRngV453(seed){let x=seed|0;return ()=>{x|=0;x=x+0x6D2B79F5|0;let t=Math.imul(x^x>>>15,1|x);t=t+Math.imul(t^t>>>7,61|t)^t;return ((t^t>>>14)>>>0)/4294967296;};}
// This generator is intentionally independent of Math.random and time. A 100-pickup sample
// will have the identical placement for a given canvas viewport and calibration seed.
function dropXPSampleV447(cx,cy){
  xpPreviewGroupsV448.length=0;
  const rng=calibrationRngV453(VISUAL_CALIBRATION_V453.layoutSeed);
  const viewport=cameraRect(0),colors=[1,10,100];
  const centers=[[.17,.20],[.72,.23],[.18,.43],[.80,.46],[.20,.72],[.76,.74],[.40,.27],[.57,.62]];
  const points=[];
  for(let cluster=0;cluster<centers.length;cluster++){
    const [nx,ny]=centers[cluster];
    for(let i=0;i<9;i++){
      const angle=rng()*TAU,r=(5+Math.sqrt(rng())*27)*Math.min(W/390,1.4);
      points.push([nx*W+Math.cos(angle)*r,ny*H+Math.sin(angle)*r]);
    }
  }
  for(let i=0;i<28;i++){
    let px=30,py=110;
    for(let j=0;j<20;j++){
      px=W*(.075+.85*rng());py=H*(.13+.72*rng());
      if(Math.hypot(px-W*.5,py-H*.50)>W*.15)break;
    }
    points.push([px,py]);
  }
  for(let i=0;i<points.length;i++){
    const [px,py]=points[i],u=colors[i%3];
    const x=clamp(viewport.l+clamp(px,18,W-18),30,WORLD_W-30);
    const y=clamp(viewport.t+clamp(py,85,H-65),30,WORLD_H-30);
    drops.push({x,y,r:4,xp:u,xpUnit:u,life:1e10,previewXPV448:true,calibrationV453:true});
  }
}

function resolveTerrainBody(o,r=10){
 for(let pass=0;pass<3;pass++){
  let touched=false,rr=r+.1;
  for(const n of terrainCandidatesV424(o.x-rr,o.y-rr,o.x+rr,o.y+rr)){
   if(n.arenaHidden)continue;
   const b=structureCollisionBoxV423(n);
   if(o.x<b.l-rr||o.x>b.r+rr||o.y<b.t-rr||o.y>b.b+rr)continue;
   const qx=clamp(o.x,b.l,b.r),qy=clamp(o.y,b.t,b.b),dx=o.x-qx,dy=o.y-qy,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
   if(d>=rr)continue;
   if(d>1e-7){o.x=qx+dx/d*rr;o.y=qy+dy/d*rr;}
   else{
    const dl=o.x-b.l,dr=b.r-o.x,dt0=o.y-b.t,db=b.b-o.y;let m=dl,side=0;if(dr<m){m=dr;side=1}if(dt0<m){m=dt0;side=2}if(db<m){side=3}
    if(side===0)o.x=b.l-rr;else if(side===1)o.x=b.r+rr;else if(side===2)o.y=b.t-rr;else o.y=b.b+rr;
   }
   touched=true;
  }
  if(!touched)break;
 }
}
function moveTerrainBodyV418(o,dx,dy,r=10){
 const steps=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.hypot(dx,dy)/4));
 for(let i=0;i<steps;i++){
  o.x+=dx/steps;o.y+=dy/steps;resolveTerrainBody(o,r);
  o.x=worldClampX(o.x,r+2);o.y=worldClampY(o.y,r+2);
 }
}
function terrainHitV418(x0,y0,x1,y1,r=0){
 const dx=x1-x0,dy=y1-y0;let best=null,bestT=Infinity;
 const l=Math.min(x0,x1)-r,t0=Math.min(y0,y1)-r,rr=Math.max(x0,x1)+r,b=Math.max(y0,y1)+r;
 const cand=terrainCandidatesV424(l,t0,rr,b);
 for(let ci=0;ci<cand.length;ci++){
  const n=cand[ci];if(n.arenaHidden)continue;const box=structureCollisionBoxV423(n);let near=0,far=1;
  const xl=box.l-r,xr=box.r+r,yt=box.t-r,yb=box.b+r;
  if(Math.abs(dx)<1e-9){if(x0<xl||x0>xr)continue;}else{let a=(xl-x0)/dx,z=(xr-x0)/dx;if(a>z){const q=a;a=z;z=q}near=Math.max(near,a);far=Math.min(far,z);if(near>far)continue;}
  if(Math.abs(dy)<1e-9){if(y0<yt||y0>yb)continue;}else{let a=(yt-y0)/dy,z=(yb-y0)/dy;if(a>z){const q=a;a=z;z=q}near=Math.max(near,a);far=Math.min(far,z);if(near>far)continue;}
  if(near<bestT){best=n;bestT=near;}
 }
 return best;
}

  const cross=(e.x-cx)*(player.y-cy)-(e.y-cy)*(player.x-cx);

 }

}

function worldCircleVisibleV441(x,y,r=0,margin=0){const view=cameraRect(r+margin);return x>=view.l&&x<=view.r&&y>=view.t&&y<=view.b;}
function telegraphVisibleV441(tg){
 const points=tg.points||null;
 if(points&&points.length){
  let minX=tg.x,maxX=tg.x,minY=tg.y,maxY=tg.y;
  for(const p of points){if(p.x<minX)minX=p.x;if(p.x>maxX)maxX=p.x;if(p.y<minY)minY=p.y;if(p.y>maxY)maxY=p.y;}
  const pad=(tg.r||tg.w||24)+34,view=cameraRect(pad);
  return !(maxX<view.l||minX>view.r||maxY<view.t||minY>view.b);
 }
 if(tg.kind==='charge'||tg.kind==='runnerDash'||tg.kind==='frostLine'||tg.kind==='apexSweep'){
  const len=tg.len||230,a=tg.a||0,m=(tg.w||18)+34,ex=tg.x+Math.cos(a)*len,ey=tg.y+Math.sin(a)*len,view=cameraRect(m);
  const minX=Math.min(tg.x,ex),maxX=Math.max(tg.x,ex),minY=Math.min(tg.y,ey),maxY=Math.max(tg.y,ey);
  return !(maxX<view.l||minX>view.r||maxY<view.t||minY>view.b);
 }
 if(tg.kind==='frostCross'||tg.kind==='abyssCross'){
  const len=tg.len||Math.max(W,H)*.72,pad=(tg.w||22)+36,view=cameraRect(pad);
  return !(tg.x+len<view.l||tg.x-len>view.r||tg.y+len<view.t||tg.y-len>view.b);
 }
 if(tg.kind==='genesisFracture'){
  const len=tg.len||360,off=Math.max(...(tg.offsets||[-48,0,48]).map(v=>Math.abs(v))),pad=(tg.w||18)+off+36,view=cameraRect(pad);
  return !(tg.x+len<view.l||tg.x-len>view.r||tg.y+len<view.t||tg.y-len>view.b);
 }
 return worldCircleVisibleV441(tg.x,tg.y,Math.max(tg.r||0,tg.w||0,18),40);
}

  return ART['boss/'+k];
 }

}

let renderedWorld=null,renderedWorldKey='',renderedFront=null;
// Native-resolution chunks retain detail without allocating a full-world HD bitmap.
const worldChunksV413=new Map();
let worldChunkKeyV413='';
const WORLD_CHUNK_V413=256;
function drawRenderedWorld(){
  // V437: world geometry + source texture + DPI determine tile pixels. Viewport bars and sector HUD do not.
  // Stage/route resets invalidate renderedWorldKey separately, so HD tiles need not repaint on announcements.
  const scale=DPR,key=[WORLD_W,WORLD_H,scale,ART['floor_decals/floor_containment_clean']?.naturalWidth||0].join(':');
  if(key!==worldChunkKeyV413||renderedWorldKey===''){
    for(const tile of worldChunksV413.values()){tile.canvas.width=1;tile.canvas.height=1;}
    worldChunksV413.clear();worldChunkKeyV413=key;renderedWorldKey=key;renderedWorld=null;
  }
  const step=WORLD_CHUNK_V413,vr=cameraRect(24),x0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(vr.l/step)),y0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(vr.t/step));
  const x1=Math.min(Math.ceil(WORLD_W/step)-1,Math.floor(vr.r/step)),y1=Math.min(Math.ceil(WORLD_H/step)-1,Math.floor(vr.b/step));
  for(let row=y0;row<=y1;row++)for(let col=x0;col<=x1;col++){
    const id=col+':'+row;let tile=worldChunksV413.get(id);
    if(!tile){
      const x=col*step,y=row*step,w=Math.min(step,WORLD_W-x),h=Math.min(step,WORLD_H-y),pad=2;
      const surface=document.createElement('canvas');surface.width=Math.ceil((w+pad*2)*scale);surface.height=Math.ceil((h+pad*2)*scale);
      const c=surface.getContext('2d');c.setTransform(scale,0,0,scale,(pad-x)*scale,(pad-y)*scale);c.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;c.imageSmoothingQuality='high';
      paintWorldChunkV413(c,{l:x-pad,t:y-pad,r:x+w+pad,b:y+h+pad});
      tile={canvas:surface,x,y,w,h,pad};worldChunksV413.set(id,tile);
    }else{worldChunksV413.delete(id);worldChunksV413.set(id,tile);}
    ctx.drawImage(tile.canvas,tile.pad*scale,tile.pad*scale,tile.w*scale,tile.h*scale,tile.x,tile.y,tile.w,tile.h);
  }
  const visible=(x1-x0+1)*(y1-y0+1),bytesPerTile=Math.ceil((step+4)*scale)**2*4;
  const limit=Math.max(visible,Math.floor(48*1024*1024/bytesPerTile));
  while(worldChunksV413.size>limit){const id=worldChunksV413.keys().next().value,tile=worldChunksV413.get(id);tile.canvas.width=1;tile.canvas.height=1;worldChunksV413.delete(id);}
}

function drawRenderedFloorMarks(){

}
function drawAssetParticle(p){
 let key,size,alpha=Math.min(1,p.life/(p.maxLife||.25)),rotation=p.a||0;
 if(p.kind==='slash'){key='tail_slash';size=p.r*2.18;alpha=clamp(p.life/p.max,0,1);rotation=(p.start||0)+.2;}
 else if(p.kind==='impact'||p.kind==='weaponHit'){key=p.color==='acid'?'acid_splash':p.color==='tail'?'tail_slash':'arm_impact';size=(p.max||p.r||10)*2;}
 else if(p.kind==='deathRing'){key='death_burst';size=p.r*2.2;}
 else if(p.kind==='bossCollapse'){key='boss_impact';size=(p.r+(p.max-p.r)*(1-alpha))*2;}
 else if(p.kind==='motionRing'){key='haste_ring';size=(p.r+(p.max-p.r)*(1-alpha))*2;}
 else if(p.kind==='muzzle'){key=p.color==='acid'?'acid_splash':'arm_impact';size=p.r*4.5;alpha=Math.min(1,p.life*6);}
 else if(p.kind==='bossVfx'){key=p.asset||'boss_impact';size=p.size||((p.max||p.r||48)*2);rotation=p.rotation!=null?p.rotation:(p.spin?((p.a||0)+t*p.spin):(p.a||0));alpha=Math.min(1,p.life/(p.maxLife||p.life||.4));}
 else return false;
 stamp('vfx/'+key,p.x,p.y,Math.max(8,size),rotation,alpha*.9);return true;
}

const RISK_TIERS=[
  {id:0,name:'STANDARD',unlock:0,target:1,deadline:0,hp:1,dmg:1,spd:1,reward:1,organLuck:0},
  {id:1,name:'HAZARD',unlock:4,target:1.18,deadline:-6,hp:1.18,dmg:1.12,spd:1.06,reward:1.5,organLuck:.035},
  {id:2,name:'NIGHTMARE',unlock:8,target:1.35,deadline:-12,hp:1.38,dmg:1.24,spd:1.12,reward:2,organLuck:.085}
];
function riskTier(){return RISK_TIERS[selectedRisk]||RISK_TIERS[0]}
const CONTRACT_DEFS={
  exterminate:{name:'EXTERMINATION',icon:'✕',desc:'Break through HIVE SURGE and defeat the target number of enemies.',reward:'SUCCESS · +12 BANK DNA · DAMAGE +10%',deadline:188},
  predator:{name:'PREDATOR TRIAL',icon:'⟲',desc:'Maintain a kill streak against HUNT PACK.',reward:'SUCCESS · +12 BANK DNA · SPEED +8',deadline:188},
  harvest:{name:'BIO HARVEST',icon:'◉',desc:'Capture and absorb 3 BIOMASS CORES yourself.',reward:'SUCCESS · +14 BANK DNA · GUARANTEED RELIC',deadline:188}
};
function createContract(id){
  const stage=Math.max(1,meta.stage||1),d=CONTRACT_DEFS[id]||CONTRACT_DEFS.exterminate,risk=riskTier();
  let baseTarget=id==='exterminate'?Math.min(520,105+stage*14):id==='predator'?Math.min(90,34+Math.floor(stage*2.8)):3;
  let target=id==='harvest'?baseTarget:Math.ceil(baseTarget*risk.target);
  return {id,name:d.name,deadline:d.deadline+risk.deadline,target,reward:d.reward,risk:risk.id,riskName:risk.name};
}
function contractProgress(){
  if(!contract)return 0;
  if(contract.id==='exterminate')return kills;
  if(contract.id==='predator')return maxChain;
  return contractCores;
}
function updateContractHUD(){
  const el=$('contractBadge');if(!contract){el.style.display='none';updateContractHUD._key='';return}
  const p=Math.min(contract.target,contractProgress()),left=Math.max(0,contract.deadline-t),sec=Math.ceil(left),key=[contract.name,p,contract.target,sec,contractDone?1:0,contractFailed?1:0].join('|');
  if(updateContractHUD._key===key)return;updateContractHUD._key=key;el.style.display='block';el.classList.toggle('contractGood',contractDone);el.classList.toggle('contractBad',contractFailed);
  if(contractDone)el.innerHTML='<b>✓ '+contract.name+'</b> · COMPLETE';else if(contractFailed)el.innerHTML='<b>! '+contract.name+'</b> · RETALIATION';else el.innerHTML='<b>'+contract.name+'</b> · '+p+'/'+contract.target+' · '+fmt(sec);
}
function completeContract(){
  if(contractDone||contractFailed)return;
  contractDone=true;contractNodes=[];
  const rewardMult=riskTier().reward;
  if(contract.id==='exterminate'){player.dmgMul*=1.10;contractBankBonus=Math.round(12*rewardMult)}
  else if(contract.id==='predator'){player.speed+=8;contractBankBonus=Math.round(12*rewardMult);predation=Math.min(100,predation+35)}
  else{player.pickup+=15;contractBankBonus=Math.round(14*rewardMult);dropRelic(player.x+rand(-20,20),player.y+rand(-20,20),true)}
  contractBankBonus+=Math.min(20,(meta.contractStreak||0)*2);directorBreathe(1.25);
  announceEncounter('CONTRACT COMPLETE',contract.name);flash(contract.reward,1200);cameraShake=6;beep(680,.11,'triangle',.035);
}
function contractSpawnPoint(){
  const edge=Math.floor(Math.random()*4),m=28,c=cameraRect(m);
  if(edge===0)return {x:rand(c.l,c.r),y:c.t};
  if(edge===1)return {x:c.r,y:rand(c.t,c.b)};
  if(edge===2)return {x:rand(c.l,c.r),y:c.b};
  return {x:c.l,y:rand(c.t,c.b)};
}
function spawnContractCore(){
  const s=sitePointV422({r:28,minCenter:0,minSep:95,preferred:worldPointAroundPlayer(Math.min(W,H)*.32,Math.min(W,H)*.62)});if(!s)return;
  contractNodes.push({x:s.x,y:s.y,r:28,hold:0,need:1.05,life:38,pulse:Math.random()*TAU});
  announceEncounter('BIOMASS CORE DETECTED','ENTER THE FIELD AND ABSORB');flash('BIO HARVEST · CORE DETECTED',850);
}
function triggerContractEncounter(i){
  if(!contract||contractDone||contractFailed||contractEventFlags[i])return;
  contractEventFlags[i]=true;
  if(contract.id==='exterminate'){
    let count=8+i*2+Math.min(4,Math.floor((meta.stage||1)/4));
    announceEncounter('HIVE SURGE','EXTERMINATION WAVE '+(i+1));
    for(let k=0;k<count;k++)setTimeout(()=>{if(running&&!ended&&!contractDone){let e=spawnEnemy(Math.random()<.32?'runner':Math.random()<.18?'spitter':'crawler');e.contractPrey=true}},k*65);
    flash('HIVE SURGE · '+count+' HOSTILES',780);cameraShake=4;
  }else if(contract.id==='predator'){
    let count=6+i*2;
    announceEncounter('HUNT PACK','KEEP THE CHAIN ALIVE');
    for(let k=0;k<count;k++)setTimeout(()=>{if(running&&!ended&&!contractDone){let type=meta.route==='cryo'&&Math.random()<.28?'froststalker':meta.route==='inferno'&&Math.random()<.28?'embermaw':'runner';let e=spawnEnemy(type);e.contractPrey=true;e.spd*=1.08}},k*72);
    predation=Math.min(100,predation+10);chainTimer=Math.max(chainTimer,2.8);flash('HUNT PACK · '+count+' PREY',780);
  }else{
    spawnContractCore();
  }
}
function updateContractNodes(dt){
  for(const n of contractNodes){
    n.life-=dt;n.pulse+=dt*3.2;
    let d=Math.hypot(player.x-n.x,player.y-n.y);
    if(d<n.r+player.r+8)n.hold=Math.min(n.need,n.hold+dt);
    else n.hold=Math.max(0,n.hold-dt*.65);
    if(n.hold>=n.need&&!n.done){
      n.done=true;contractCores++;dnaTotal+=12;player.hp=Math.min(player.maxHp,player.hp+10);
      predation=Math.min(100,predation+18);flash('BIOMASS ABSORBED · '+contractCores+' / '+contract.target,700);
      beep(760,.09,'triangle',.03);cameraShake=4;
      for(let j=0;j<18;j++)particles.push({kind:'relic',x:n.x,y:n.y,vx:rand(-70,70),vy:rand(-70,70),r:rand(1.5,3.5),life:rand(.2,.55)});
    }
  }
  contractNodes=contractNodes.filter(n=>!n.done&&n.life>0);
}
function updateContract(){
  if(!contract||contractDone||contractFailed)return;
  const marks=[58,124,176];
  for(let i=0;i<marks.length;i++){
    if(t>=marks[i]&&!contractEventFlags[i]){
      const majorBusy=directorLock>0||directorRelief>0||tacticalWave||rift||purges.length||rewardChoosing||choosing||enemies.some(e=>!e.dead&&(e.elite||e.nemesis||e.finalBoss));
      if(!majorBusy){directorHold(.9);triggerContractEncounter(i);}
    }
  }
  if(contractProgress()>=contract.target){completeContract();return}
  if(t>=contract.deadline){contractFailed=true;contractNodes=[];announceEncounter('CONTRACT FAILED',contract.name);flash('TITAN RESPONSE ESCALATED',1100);beep(105,.14,'sawtooth',.035)}
}

function updatePredationHUD(){

    label.textContent='PREDATION '+Math.floor(predation)+'%'+(killChain>1?' · CHAIN '+killChain:'');
  }
}

function gainPredation(v){
  if(evoForm.speed==='hunt')v*=1.35;
  if(frenzy>0){frenzy=Math.min(8,frenzy+v*.018);return}
  predation=Math.min(100,predation+v);
  if(predation>=100)triggerPredation();
}

// V417: one artwork header, with simulation-time final-boss deadlines.
const FINAL_BOSS_LIMIT_V417=90;
function tickBossDeadlineV417(dt){
  const boss=enemies.find(e=>e.finalBoss&&!e.dead&&e.hp>0);
  if(!boss)return false;
  if(boss.encounterPhaseV526){boss.bossSecondsV417=Math.max(0,t-(boss.encounterAtV526??300));return false;}
  if(boss.bossSecondsV417===undefined)boss.bossSecondsV417=FINAL_BOSS_LIMIT_V417;
  boss.bossSecondsV417=Math.max(0,boss.bossSecondsV417-dt);
  if(boss.bossSecondsV417<=0){
    syncArtHUDV417();lose('bossDeadlineV484');$('endTitle').textContent='TIME OVER';
    $('endSub').textContent=(boss.bossName||'FINAL BOSS')+' · Failed to defeat the boss before the time limit.';
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}
function fitArtTextV417(el,text,maxSize,width){
  if(el.textContent===text)return;
  el.textContent=text;el.setAttribute('font-size',maxSize);
  const length=el.getComputedTextLength();
  if(length>width)el.setAttribute('font-size',Math.max(24,maxSize*width/length));
}

let timerGlyphLastTextV472='';
function renderTimerGlyphsV472(str){
  const s=(String(str||'00:00')).slice(-5).padStart(5,'0');
  if(s===timerGlyphLastTextV472)return;
  const ids=['timerGlyph0V472','timerGlyph1V472','timerGlyph2V472','timerGlyph3V472','timerGlyph4V472'];
  const slotXs=[1198,1228,1264,1286,1316];
  const slotWs=[26,26,12,26,26];
  const digitH=41;
  const colonH=24;
  const digitY=54;
  const colonY=62;
  for(let i=0;i<5;i++){
    const ch=s[i];
    const el=$(ids[i]); if(!el)continue;
    const data=TIMER_GLYPH_SRC_V472[ch]||TIMER_GLYPH_SRC_V472['0'];
    if(el.getAttribute('href')!==data)el.setAttribute('href',data);
    const wh=TIMER_GLYPH_SIZE_V472[ch]||TIMER_GLYPH_SIZE_V472['0'];
    const isColon=ch===':';
    const h=isColon?colonH:digitH;
    const y=isColon?colonY:digitY;
    const fitW=Math.max(1,Math.round(wh[0]*h/wh[1]));
    const w=Math.min(slotWs[i],fitW);
    const x=Math.round(slotXs[i] + (slotWs[i]-w)/2);
    el.setAttribute('x',String(x));
    el.setAttribute('y',String(y));
    el.setAttribute('width',String(w));
    el.setAttribute('height',String(h));
  }
  timerGlyphLastTextV472=s;
}

  root.hidden=!active;if(!active)return;

  renderTimerGlyphsV472(hudTimeV472);
  $('artXPWidthV417').setAttribute('width',String(700*q));
  $('normalArtV417').setAttribute('aria-label','LEVEL '+player.level+' · XP '+Math.round(q*100)+'% · '+hudTimeV472);
  if(!threat){root.dataset.bioV484='false';syncBossCollectionHUDV487(null,'',0);return;}
  const name=threat.finalBoss?(threat.bossName||'BIO TITAN'):(threat.bossName||threat.eliteName||'MID-BOSS');

  syncBossCollectionHUDV487(threat,name,hp);

    fitArtTextV417($('midNameV417'),name,66,1070);
    $('midHPWidthV417').setAttribute('width',String(1180*hp));
    $('midTimeV417').textContent=fmt(t);
    $('midArtV417').setAttribute('aria-label',name+' · HP '+Math.round(hp*100)+'%');
  }
}

function feedbackCfg(){if(!meta.feedback)meta.feedback={sound:true,haptic:true,shake:2,flash:2,reducedMotion:false,damageNumbers:true,highContrast:false,graphics:'auto',perfInfo:false};const f=meta.feedback;f.sound=f.sound!==false;f.haptic=f.haptic!==false;f.shake=clamp(Number.isFinite(Number(f.shake))?Number(f.shake):2,0,2);f.flash=clamp(Number.isFinite(Number(f.flash))?Number(f.flash):2,0,2);f.reducedMotion=!!f.reducedMotion;f.damageNumbers=f.damageNumbers!==false;f.highContrast=!!f.highContrast;f.graphics=['auto','eco','quality'].includes(f.graphics)?f.graphics:'auto';f.perfInfo=!!f.perfInfo;return f}

let resetArmedUntil=0;function resetProgressSafe(){const now=Date.now();if(now>resetArmedUntil){resetArmedUntil=now+4500;$('resetSave').innerHTML='CONFIRM RESET<br>TAP AGAIN';settingsStatus('TAP RESET AGAIN WITHIN 4.5s TO ERASE LOCAL PROGRESS.');clearTimeout(resetProgressSafe._timer);resetProgressSafe._timer=setTimeout(()=>{if(Date.now()>resetArmedUntil){$('resetSave').innerHTML='RESET PROGRESS · TAP TWICE';settingsStatus('RESET CANCELLED · NO DATA CHANGED.')}},4700);return}localStorage.removeItem('EVOLVE_V575_TEST_META_V1');settingsStatus('PROGRESS RESET · RELOADING…');setTimeout(()=>location.reload(),220)}
function primeAudio(){try{const AC=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!AC)return null;const ac=beep.ac||(beep.ac=new AC());if(ac.state==='suspended')ac.resume().catch(()=>{});return ac}catch(e){return null}}
function beep(freq=240,d=.05,type='sine',vol=.025){if(feedbackCfg().sound===false)return;try{const ac=primeAudio();if(!ac)return;const o=ac.createOscillator(),g=ac.createGain();o.type=type;o.frequency.value=freq;g.gain.setValueAtTime(Math.max(.0001,vol),ac.currentTime);o.connect(g).connect(ac.destination);o.start();g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,ac.currentTime+d);o.stop(ac.currentTime+d)}catch(e){}}
function hapticCue(kind='tap'){if(feedbackCfg().haptic===false||!navigator.vibrate)return;const p={tap:8,dash:12,active:[14,18,18],damage:[26,18,14],warning:[32,34,32],boss:[48,38,66],perfect:[10,14,10],level:[8,20,13],evolve:[16,18,26],death:[45,34,72]}[kind]||8;try{navigator.vibrate(p)}catch(e){}}
function audioCue(kind,arg=0){
  const later=(ms,f,d,t='sine',v=.02)=>setTimeout(()=>beep(f,d,t,v),ms);
  if(kind==='level'){beep(500,.065,'sine',.025);later(72,760,.10,'triangle',.022)}
  else if(kind==='elite'){beep(104,.12,'sawtooth',.032);later(92,156,.10,'square',.022)}
  else if(kind==='boss'){beep(58,.20,'sawtooth',.040);later(120,78,.18,'sawtooth',.032);later(245,116,.15,'square',.026)}
  else if(kind==='intent'){beep(205,.055,'square',.018);later(62,162,.05,'square',.014)}
  else if(kind==='perfect'){beep(620,.05,'triangle',.025);later(55,930,.075,'triangle',.021)}
  else if(kind==='damage'){beep(48,.11,'sawtooth',.032)}
  else if(kind==='dash'){beep(185,.055,'sawtooth',.014)}
  else if(kind==='active'){beep(120+arg*55,.075,arg===0?'triangle':'sawtooth',.027);later(58,220+arg*70,.07,'triangle',.018)}
  else if(kind==='evolve'){beep(680,.085,'triangle',.030);later(80,980,.13,'triangle',.026)}
  else if(kind==='arm')beep(188+arg*28,.020,'square',.009);
  else if(kind==='acid')beep(96+arg*7,.045,'sawtooth',.0085);
  else if(kind==='tail')beep(66+arg*5,.060,'triangle',.013);
}
function shakeFeedbackMul(){const f=feedbackCfg(),n=f.shake;let m=n<=0?0:n===1?.56:1;return f.reducedMotion?m*.32:m}

  for(const id of ['homeShake','pauseShake']){const e=$(id);if(e){e.textContent='SHAKE · '+shakeName;e.className='feedbackBtn '+(f.shake===0?'off':f.shake===1?'low':'on')}}
}

  {id:'kinetic',name:'HYPERKINETIC',sub:'ENEMY SPEED +12%',apply(){threatMods.speed*=1.12;}},

function applyThreatAdaptation(){
  let pool=THREAT_POOL.filter(x=>!usedThreats.includes(x.id));if(!pool.length)return;
  let pick=pool[Math.floor(Math.random()*pool.length)];usedThreats.push(pick.id);pick.apply();threatNames.push(pick.name);
  $('threatBadge').style.display='block';$('threatBadge').innerHTML='ADAPTATION<br>'+threatNames.join(' · ');
  announceEncounter(pick.name,pick.sub);flash('ENEMY ADAPTATION DETECTED',850);cameraShake=Math.max(cameraShake,4);
}
function setSector(n){
  if(sector===n)return;sector=n;directorHold(1.25);buildFloorDecor(true);triggerSectorPresentation();

  announceEncounter('SECTOR '+(n+1),SECTORS[n].name);
  cameraShake=Math.max(cameraShake,5);
  for(let i=0;i<22;i++){let p=worldPointInView(24);particles.push({kind:'rift',x:p.x,y:p.y,vx:rand(-20,20),vy:rand(-20,20),r:rand(1.2,2.8),life:rand(.22,.55)});}
  setTimeout(()=>{if(running&&!ended&&!extractionMode)applyThreatAdaptation()},520);
}
// V352: stage exit must always spawn inside the CURRENT visible/accessible camera area.
// It never trusts a boss death position, so an off-screen/edge boss cannot strand the exit outside the map.
function safeBreachGatePoint(){
  const vr=cameraRect();
  // Keep the exit away from HUD/top controls and the floating joystick zone at the bottom.
  const safe={
    l:clamp(vr.l+56,34,Math.max(34,WORLD_W-34)),
    r:clamp(vr.r-56,34,Math.max(34,WORLD_W-34)),
    t:clamp(vr.t+118,34,Math.max(34,WORLD_H-34)),
    b:clamp(vr.b-110,34,Math.max(34,WORLD_H-34))
  };
  if(safe.r<safe.l){const c=clamp(vr.l+W*.5,34,WORLD_W-34);safe.l=safe.r=c;}
  if(safe.b<safe.t){const c=clamp(vr.t+H*.5,34,WORLD_H-34);safe.t=safe.b=c;}
  const cx=clamp(vr.l+W*.5,safe.l,safe.r),cy=clamp(vr.t+H*.5,safe.t,safe.b);
  const radius=Math.min(158,Math.min(W,H)*.34);
  const dirs=[[1,0],[-1,0],[0,1],[0,-1],[.707,.707],[.707,-.707],[-.707,.707],[-.707,-.707]];
  const candidates=[{x:cx,y:cy}];
  for(const [dx,dy] of dirs)candidates.push({x:clamp(player.x+dx*radius,safe.l,safe.r),y:clamp(player.y+dy*radius,safe.t,safe.b)});
  let best=null,bestScore=-1e9;
  for(const p of candidates){
    const d=Math.hypot(p.x-player.x,p.y-player.y);
    if(d<72)continue;
    let hazardPenalty=0;
    for(const s of worldSites||[]){if(s.claimed||s.type!=='hazard')continue;const rr=(s.r||36)+48;if((p.x-s.x)**2+(p.y-s.y)**2<rr*rr){hazardPenalty=90;break;}}
    for(const tg of telegraphs||[]){if(tg.life<=0)continue;const rr=(tg.r||28)+42;if((p.x-(tg.x||0))**2+(p.y-(tg.y||0))**2<rr*rr){hazardPenalty+=55;break;}}
    // Prefer a short, obvious walk (~130 px) while staying near the visible screen center.
    const centerDist=Math.hypot(p.x-cx,p.y-cy);
    const score=210-Math.abs(d-132)-centerDist*.10-hazardPenalty;
    if(score>bestScore){bestScore=score;best=p;}
  }
  if(!best)best={x:cx,y:cy};
  // Final hard safety clamp: world bounds AND the currently visible safe rectangle.
  return {x:clamp(best.x,safe.l,safe.r),y:clamp(best.y,safe.t,safe.b)};
}
// V353: keep the final boss inside the playable world AND inside the current camera viewport.
// This prevents charge/knockback/edge-camera interactions from letting a boss die outside the map or off-screen.
function finalBossPlayableRect(e){
  if(bossArena?.v526&&bossArena.active){const a=bossArena,p=a.half+(e?.r||76)+2;return {l:a.l+p,r:a.r-p,t:a.t+p,b:a.b-p};}
  const expand=Math.min(42,Math.max(18,(e?.r||38)*.24)),vr=cameraRect(expand),pad=Math.max(26,(e?.r||38)*.82+8);
  let l=Math.max(pad,vr.l+pad),r=Math.min(WORLD_W-pad,vr.r-pad),t0=Math.max(pad,vr.t+pad),b=Math.min(WORLD_H-pad,vr.b-pad);
  if(r<l){const c=clamp(vr.l+W*.5,pad,WORLD_W-pad);l=r=c;}
  if(b<t0){const c=clamp(vr.t+H*.5,pad,WORLD_H-pad);t0=b=c;}
  return {l,r,t:t0,b};
}
function containFinalBoss(e){
  if(!e||e.dead||!e.finalBoss)return;
  const q=finalBossPlayableRect(e),ox=e.x,oy=e.y;
  e.x=clamp(e.x,q.l,q.r);e.y=clamp(e.y,q.t,q.b);
  if(e.x!==ox){e.vx=0;e.kx=0;if(e.burst>0)e.burst=Math.min(e.burst,.18);}
  if(e.y!==oy){e.vy=0;e.ky=0;if(e.burst>0)e.burst=Math.min(e.burst,.18);}
}
// All final-boss death FX, XP, relics and follow-up rewards resolve from a safe visible anchor.
function safeBossResolutionPoint(e){
  const vr=cameraRect(),pad=Math.max(46,(e?.r||38)+14);
  const safe={
    l:clamp(vr.l+pad,pad,Math.max(pad,WORLD_W-pad)),
    r:clamp(vr.r-pad,pad,Math.max(pad,WORLD_W-pad)),
    t:clamp(vr.t+Math.max(92,pad),pad,Math.max(pad,WORLD_H-pad)),
    b:clamp(vr.b-Math.max(86,pad),pad,Math.max(pad,WORLD_H-pad))
  };
  if(safe.r<safe.l){const c=clamp(vr.l+W*.5,pad,WORLD_W-pad);safe.l=safe.r=c;}
  if(safe.b<safe.t){const c=clamp(vr.t+H*.5,pad,WORLD_H-pad);safe.t=safe.b=c;}
  let x=clamp(Number.isFinite(e?.x)?e.x:player.x,safe.l,safe.r),y=clamp(Number.isFinite(e?.y)?e.y:player.y,safe.t,safe.b);
  // If the clamped point lands directly on the player, move the resolution inward to an obvious nearby spot.
  if(Math.hypot(x-player.x,y-player.y)<72){
    const cx=clamp(vr.l+W*.5,safe.l,safe.r),cy=clamp(vr.t+H*.5,safe.t,safe.b),a=Math.atan2(cy-player.y,cx-player.x)||(-Math.PI/2);
    x=clamp(player.x+Math.cos(a)*112,safe.l,safe.r);y=clamp(player.y+Math.sin(a)*112,safe.t,safe.b);
  }
  return {x:worldClampX(x,pad),y:worldClampY(y,pad)};
}

// V354: reserve a visible entry side, then let the boss physically enter from that side.
// The side can be announced in advance while the exact world point is recomputed at spawn time,
// so edge-camera movement cannot make the promised entrance disappear off-screen.
function bossEntryPlan(preferredSide=null){
  const vr=cameraRect(),pad=58,topPad=124,bottomPad=98;
  const safe={
    l:clamp(vr.l+pad,pad,Math.max(pad,WORLD_W-pad)),
    r:clamp(vr.r-pad,pad,Math.max(pad,WORLD_W-pad)),
    t:clamp(vr.t+topPad,pad,Math.max(pad,WORLD_H-pad)),
    b:clamp(vr.b-bottomPad,pad,Math.max(pad,WORLD_H-pad))
  };
  if(safe.r<safe.l){const c=clamp(vr.l+W*.5,pad,WORLD_W-pad);safe.l=safe.r=c;}
  if(safe.b<safe.t){const c=clamp(vr.t+H*.5,pad,WORLD_H-pad);safe.t=safe.b=c;}
  const cx=(safe.l+safe.r)*.5,cy=(safe.t+safe.b)*.5,inset=Math.min(104,Math.min(W,H)*.22);
  const plans={
    NORTH:{side:'NORTH',x:cx,y:safe.t,tx:cx,ty:clamp(safe.t+inset,safe.t,safe.b)},
    EAST:{side:'EAST',x:safe.r,y:cy,tx:clamp(safe.r-inset,safe.l,safe.r),ty:cy},
    SOUTH:{side:'SOUTH',x:cx,y:safe.b,tx:cx,ty:clamp(safe.b-inset,safe.t,safe.b)},
    WEST:{side:'WEST',x:safe.l,y:cy,tx:clamp(safe.l+inset,safe.l,safe.r),ty:cy}
  };
  const list=preferredSide&&plans[preferredSide]?[plans[preferredSide]]:Object.values(plans);
  let best=list[0],bestScore=-1e9;
  for(const p of list){
    const d=Math.hypot(p.x-player.x,p.y-player.y),td=Math.hypot(p.tx-player.x,p.ty-player.y);
    let penalty=0;
    for(const site of worldSites||[]){if(site.claimed||site.type!=='hazard')continue;const rr=(site.r||36)+52;if((p.tx-site.x)**2+(p.ty-site.y)**2<rr*rr){penalty+=110;break;}}
    // Prefer the side farthest from the player so the entrance is readable and never spawns on top of them.
    const score=d+td*.35-penalty;
    if(score>bestScore){bestScore=score;best=p;}
  }
  return {...best};
}
function armBossArrivalWarning(){
  const p=bossEntryPlan();bossArrivalPlan={side:p.side};bossArrivalCue={side:p.side,until:t+6,label:'BOSS',color:isOmegaApexStage()?'#d57cff':isApexStage()?'#ff9a63':'#ff755d'};
  const dir=p.side;flash('TITAN SIGNAL · '+dir,1150);announceEncounter('APEX SIGNAL',dir+' APPROACH · CLEAR THE LANE');audioCue('warning');hapticCue('warning');
}
function stageMajorArrival(e,label='ELITE',duration=.88,color='#ffb05f'){
  if(!e)return null;
  const p=bossEntryPlan();
  e.x=p.x;e.y=p.y;e.arrivalStartX=p.x;e.arrivalStartY=p.y;e.arrivalTargetX=p.tx;e.arrivalTargetY=p.ty;
  e.arrivalTime=duration;e.arrivalMax=duration;e.arrivalInvuln=true;e.arrivalSide=p.side;e.arrivalLabel=label;e.arrivalColor=color;
  e.spawnFx=Math.max(e.spawnFx||0,.70);e.patternLock=Math.max(e.patternLock||0,duration+.18);e.vx=e.vy=e.kx=e.ky=0;
  majorArrivalCue={side:p.side,until:t+duration+.28,label,color,owner:e};
  return p;
}
function drawBossArrivalCue(){
  const cue=(majorArrivalCue&&t<=majorArrivalCue.until)?majorArrivalCue:bossArrivalCue;
  if(!cue||t>cue.until||ended)return;
  const side=cue.side,pulse=.55+.45*Math.sin(t*9),label=cue.label||'BOSS',color=cue.color||(label==='NEMESIS'?'#c47aff':label==='ELITE'?'#ffb05f':'#ff755d');let x=W*.5,y=H*.5,a=0;
  if(side==='NORTH'){x=W*.5;y=144;a=Math.PI/2;}
  else if(side==='SOUTH'){x=W*.5;y=H-104;a=-Math.PI/2;}
  else if(side==='WEST'){x=34;y=H*.53;a=0;}
  else {x=W-34;y=H*.53;a=Math.PI;}
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.globalAlpha=.58+.32*pulse;ctx.fillStyle=color;ctx.strokeStyle='#fff2e8';ctx.shadowBlur=14;ctx.shadowColor=color;ctx.lineWidth=1.5;
  ctx.save();ctx.rotate(a);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(13,0);ctx.lineTo(-5,-8);ctx.lineTo(-1,0);ctx.lineTo(-5,8);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
  ctx.globalAlpha=.86;ctx.fillStyle='#fff4ec';ctx.shadowBlur=6;ctx.font='1000 7px system-ui';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText(label+' · '+side,0,side==='NORTH'?22:side==='SOUTH'?-18:24);ctx.restore();
}
function restoreBossArena(){
  if(!bossArena)return;
  for(const s of worldSites||[])if(s.arenaHidden)s.arenaHidden=false;
  for(const n of terrainNodes)n.arenaHidden=false;
  resolveTerrainBody(player,player.r);for(const e of enemies)if(!e.dead)resolveTerrainBody(e,e.r);for(const d of drops)resolveTerrainBody(d,4);
  bossArena=null;
}
function drawBossArena(){
  if(!bossArena||!finalBossAlive())return;
  const a=bossArena,p=.5+.5*Math.sin(t*2.2);ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.12+.035*p;ctx.strokeStyle=a.color||'#ff755d';ctx.shadowBlur=18;ctx.shadowColor=a.color||'#ff755d';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.setLineDash([10,12]);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(a.x,a.y,a.r,0,TAU);ctx.stroke();ctx.setLineDash([]);ctx.globalAlpha=.035;ctx.fillStyle=a.color||'#ff755d';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(a.x,a.y,a.r,0,TAU);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
}

const RELICS=[
 {id:'lens',name:'VORACIOUS LENS',apply(){player.pickup+=28;player.relicDna=(player.relicDna||1)*1.08;}},
 {id:'gland',name:'SERRATED GLAND',apply(){player.tailPower=(player.tailPower||1)*1.16;player.dmgMul*=1.04;}},
 {id:'sac',name:'THERMAL SAC',apply(){player.dmgMul*=1.10;player.maxHp+=8;player.hp+=8;}},
 {id:'node',name:'CRYO NODE',apply(){player.armor=Math.min(.48,(player.armor||0)+.06);player.speed+=5;}},
 {id:'shard',name:'NEURAL SHARD',apply(){player.baseCd=Math.max(.42,player.baseCd-.06);player.speed+=7;}}
];
function updateRelicBadge(){
  let el=$('relicBadge');if(!runRelics.length){el.style.display='none';return}
  el.style.display='block';el.innerHTML='RELICS<br>'+runRelics.map(x=>x.name).join(' · ');
}
function dropRelic(x,y,guaranteed=false){
  x=worldClampX(Number.isFinite(x)?x:player.x,18);y=worldClampY(Number.isFinite(y)?y:player.y,18);
  if(!guaranteed&&Math.random()>(.055+Math.min(.10,meta.relicLuck||0)))return;
  let pool=RELICS.filter(r=>!r.zoneExclusive&&!runRelics.some(x=>x.id===r.id));
  if(!pool.length)return;
  let r=pool[Math.floor(Math.random()*pool.length)];
  relicDrops.push({x,y,r:8,life:24,relic:r,pulse:Math.random()*TAU});
}
function collectRelic(d){
  if(!d||d.dead)return;d.dead=true;d.relic.apply();runRelics.push(d.relic);discover('relic:'+d.relic.id);updateRelicBadge();
  flash('RELIC ABSORBED · '+d.relic.name,1050);beep(880,.12,'triangle',.035);cameraShake=Math.max(cameraShake,4);
  for(let i=0;i<16;i++)particles.push({kind:'relic',x:d.x,y:d.y,vx:rand(-60,60),vy:rand(-60,60),r:rand(1.5,3.2),life:rand(.25,.6)});
}

function triggerSectorPresentation(){
  const el=$('sectorFlash'),accent=sectorAccent();if(!el||feedbackCfg().flash===0)return;
  el.style.background=`radial-gradient(circle at 50% 48%,${accent}55 0%,${accent}18 38%,transparent 72%)`;
  el.classList.remove('active');void el.offsetWidth;el.classList.add('active');
}

  const el=$('bossIntro');cinematicFreeze=Math.max(cinematicFreeze,.72);player.inv=Math.max(player.inv,.95);el.style.display='flex';requestAnimationFrame(()=>el.classList.add('show'));
  clearTimeout(showBossIntro._timer);
  showBossIntro._timer=setTimeout(()=>{el.classList.remove('show');setTimeout(()=>{if(!el.classList.contains('show'))el.style.display='none'},180)},1450);
}

function drawCommercialSetDressing(){
  ctx.save();const top=104,bottom=H-72,accent=sectorAccent();
  // deep edge shadows make the arena feel embedded inside a containment bay
  let lg=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,58,0);lg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(0,0,0,.72)');lg.addColorStop(.72,'rgba(0,0,0,.12)');lg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,0,0,0)');ctx.fillStyle=lg;ctx.fillRect(0,top,64,bottom-top);
  let rg=ctx.createLinearGradient(W,0,W-58,0);rg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(0,0,0,.72)');rg.addColorStop(.72,'rgba(0,0,0,.12)');rg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,0,0,0)');ctx.fillStyle=rg;ctx.fillRect(W-64,top,64,bottom-top);
  // industrial barrels / tanks along the non-play-critical outer edge

  // infected tendrils frame the arena without becoming gameplay objects

  const vines=[[-8,H*.60,58,H*.48], [W+8,H*.55,W-62,H*.44], [W+6,H*.88,W-72,H*.76]];

  ctx.restore();
}

function buildFloorDecor(keepMarks=false){
  floorDecor=[];if(!keepMarks)floorMarks=[];
  let kinds=sector===0?['plate','stripe','crack','stain']:sector===1?['stripe','burn','crack','plate']:sector===2?['vein','podmark','stain','crack']:['vein','crack','plate','stain'];
  if(meta.route==='cryo')kinds=['frost','crack','plate','stain',...kinds.slice(0,2)];
  else if(meta.route==='gene')kinds=['vein','podmark','crack','stain',...kinds.slice(0,2)];
  else if(meta.route==='inferno')kinds=['burn','stripe','crack','stain',...kinds.slice(0,2)];
  let decorCount=(W<700||matchMedia('(pointer:coarse)').matches)?96:132;
  for(let i=0;i<decorCount;i++)floorDecor.push({kind:kinds[i%kinds.length],x:rand(24,WORLD_W-24),y:rand(24,WORLD_H-24),r:rand(8,28),a:rand(0,TAU),s:rand(.6,1.35),alpha:rand(.10,.30)});
}
function addFloorMark(e){
  if(!e)return;let color=e.type==='spitter'||e.type==='symbiote'?'#57c74d':e.finalBoss||e.elite?'#8c1f25':'#67201f';
  floorMarks.push({x:e.x,y:e.y,r:rand(e.r*.55,e.r*1.15),a:rand(0,TAU),color,alpha:e.finalBoss?.38:e.elite?.28:.18});
  if(floorMarks.length>24)floorMarks.splice(0,floorMarks.length-24);
}
function drawFloorDecor(){
  ctx.save();
  for(const d of floorDecor){ctx.save();ctx.translate(d.x,d.y);ctx.rotate(d.a);ctx.globalAlpha=d.alpha;
    if(d.kind==='crack'){ctx.strokeStyle='#7e8b9438';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-d.r,0);ctx.lineTo(-d.r*.3,-d.r*.2);ctx.lineTo(d.r*.1,d.r*.12);ctx.lineTo(d.r,0);ctx.stroke();ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-d.r*.15,-d.r*.12);ctx.lineTo(-d.r*.05,-d.r*.65);ctx.stroke();}
    else if(d.kind==='stripe'){ctx.fillStyle='#d78a3840';for(let k=-2;k<=2;k++)ctx.fillRect(k*7,-2,4,d.r*.65);}
    else if(d.kind==='plate'){ctx.strokeStyle='#7785902e';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.strokeRect(-d.r*.8,-d.r*.45,d.r*1.6,d.r*.9);ctx.fillStyle='#89939c22';ctx.fillRect(-d.r*.55,-1,d.r*1.1,2);}
    else if(d.kind==='vein'){ctx.strokeStyle='#52be653d';ctx.shadowBlur=5;ctx.shadowColor='#53d76b';ctx.lineWidth=1.5;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-d.r,0);ctx.bezierCurveTo(-d.r*.4,-d.r*.4,d.r*.15,d.r*.35,d.r,0);ctx.stroke();}
    else if(d.kind==='podmark'){ctx.fillStyle='#6acc7330';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,d.r*.8,d.r*.3,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();}
    else if(d.kind==='frost'){ctx.strokeStyle='#89dfff3b';ctx.lineWidth=1;for(let k=0;k<4;k++){let a=k/4*TAU;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,0);ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*d.r,Math.sin(a)*d.r);ctx.stroke();}}
    else if(d.kind==='burn'){let g=ctx.createRadialGradient(0,0,0,0,0,d.r);g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,95,30,.13)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(40,8,4,0)');ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,d.r,0,TAU);ctx.fill();}
    else{ctx.fillStyle='#35141555';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,d.r*d.s,d.r*.42,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();}
    ctx.restore();
  }
  for(const m of floorMarks){ctx.save();ctx.translate(m.x,m.y);ctx.rotate(m.a);ctx.globalAlpha=m.alpha;ctx.fillStyle=m.color;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,m.r,m.r*.42,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();for(let i=0;i<3;i++){let a=i/3*TAU+m.a,rr=m.r*(1.1+i*.12);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(Math.cos(a)*rr*.55,Math.sin(a)*rr*.35,Math.max(1.5,m.r*.11),0,TAU);ctx.fill();}ctx.restore();}
  ctx.restore();
}
