// V433 · phase transition recovery / stale telegraph guard
function bossHasLiveTelegraphV433(e){
  if(!e)return false;
  for(const tg of telegraphs)if(tg&&tg.owner===e&&!tg.done&&tg.life>0)return true;
  return false;
}
function bossPhaseResumeV433(e,delay=.18){
  if(!e)return;
  const grace=e.finalBoss?1.55:1.10;
  for(const b of eBullets)if(b&&((e.finalBoss&&b.bossShot)||b._bio489&&b.owner===e))b.life=0;
  for(const tg of telegraphs)if(tg&&(tg.owner===e||(e.finalBoss&&['acidZone','nullZone','bioSpikeZone'].includes(tg.kind)))){tg.done=true;tg.life=0;}
  if(e.finalBoss){for(const hz of biomeHazards){hz.warn=0;hz.active=0;}purges=[];}
  e.telegraphing=null;e.patternLock=grace;e.burst=0;e.dashPrep=0;e.chargePrep=0;
  e.patternCd=Math.max(grace+.25,Number.isFinite(e.patternCd)?Math.min(e.patternCd,grace+.25):grace+.25);
  e._v490PhaseUntil=t+grace;e._v490RecoverUntil=t+grace;
  e._phaseResumeGraceV433=grace;e._phaseIdleV433=0;
  // A new phase demonstrates its signature attack before the first counter.
  e.patternIndex=0;
}

// ===== V490 BOSS COMBAT DIRECTOR: shared fair-play budget =====
const BOSS_V490_RECOVERY={acid:.90,bioBreath:1.25,bioSpiral:1.25,bioSpikes:1.15,bioCross:1.10,
  apexNova:1.45,summon:1.15,charge:1.05,frostFan:1.05,frostCross:1.15,cryoCage:1.30,
  geneBloom:1.15,geneLattice:1.25,omegaPool:.85,pyreWheel:1.0,magmaCrown:1.30,
  originQuake:1.55,voidCollapse:1.50,apexSweep:1.10,omegaLattice:1.25,
  genesisFracture:1.25,abyssCross:1.25,counter:1.20};
function bossV490Arc(a,b){return Math.abs(Math.atan2(Math.sin(a-b),Math.cos(a-b)));}
function bossV490SafeAngle(e,cx,cy){
  // Select the visible/world-reachable opening, not a gap behind the screen edge.
  const preferred=Math.atan2(cy-e.y,cx-e.x),vr=cameraRect(14),rad=160;
  let best=preferred,score=-1e9;
  for(let i=0;i<16;i++){
    const a=preferred+i*TAU/16,tx=cx+Math.cos(a)*rad,ty=cy+Math.sin(a)*rad;
    const worldClear=Math.min(tx-25,WORLD_W-25-tx,ty-25,WORLD_H-25-ty);
    if(worldClear<0)continue;
    const visibleClear=Math.min(tx-vr.l,vr.r-tx,ty-vr.t,vr.b-ty);
    const sc=Math.min(50,visibleClear)*1.4+Math.min(70,worldClear)*.3-bossV490Arc(a,preferred)*17;
    if(sc>score){score=sc;best=a;}
  }
  return best;
}
function bossV490Ring(e,cx,cy,rad,count,speed,damage,kind,radius,gapAngle,gapWidth=.60,inward=false){
  // One clearly indicated opening is preserved in every circular wave.
  count=Math.max(3,Math.min(28,count|0));
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    const a=i/count*TAU;
    if(bossV490Arc(a,gapAngle)<gapWidth*.5)continue;
    const sx=inward?cx+Math.cos(a)*rad:cx,sy=inward?cy+Math.sin(a)*rad:cy;
    const angle=inward?a+Math.PI:a;
    if(inward)pushBossShotFromV434(e,sx,sy,angle,speed,damage,kind,radius,3.1,true);
    else pushBossShotV431(e,angle,speed,damage,kind,radius,3.1);
  }
}
function bossV490Schedule(e,key){
  const tg=telegraphs.findLast?.(z=>z.owner===e&&!z.done&&!z.active&&z.life>0);
  const windup=tg?tg.life:1.0,phase=bossPhaseV431(e);
  if(isBioTitanV435(e)){
    // V491: No double-wait. Old V490 added windup + full effect + recovery + spacing,
    // even though the attack animation and projectile flight already consumed that time.
    const active=tg?.duration??.35;
    const after=key==='bioRush'?.27:key==='bioSpikes'?.21:key==='bioBreath'?.18:.24;
    const duration=windup+Math.max(active,key==='bioRush'?.65:.48)+after;
    e._v490RecoverUntil=t+duration;
    e.patternCd=Math.max(e.patternCd||0,duration);
    e._v490CurrentKey=key;return;
  }
  const hard=key==='originQuake'||key==='apexNova'||key==='magmaCrown'||key==='voidCollapse';
  const active=hard?.95:key==='bioSpikes'||key==='geneLattice'||key==='geneBloom'?1.0:key==='bioBreath'?.72:.58;
  const recovery=(BOSS_V490_RECOVERY[key]??1.0)*(phase>=4?.85:phase>=3?.9:phase>=2?.97:1);
  const spacing=(phase>=4?.45:phase>=3?.55:phase>=2?.72:.90);
  const duration=windup+active+recovery+spacing;
  e._v490RecoverUntil=t+duration;e.patternCd=Math.max(e.patternCd||0,duration);e._v490CurrentKey=key;
}
function bossV490CanAttack(e){
  if(t<(e._v490PhaseUntil||0)||t<(e._v490RecoverUntil||0)||e.burst>0)return false;
  // If the existing attack still saturates nearby space, delay rather than stack.
  let nearby=0;
  for(const b of eBullets){if(b.bossShot&&b.life>0&&Math.abs(b.x-player.x)<255&&Math.abs(b.y-player.y)<255&&++nearby>=22)return false;}
  let zones=0;
  for(const z of telegraphs){if(z.active&&z.life>0&&['nullZone','acidZone','bioSpikeZone'].includes(z.kind)&&Math.hypot(z.x-player.x,z.y-player.y)<215)zones++;}
  return zones<7;
}
function bossV490MarkSafety(tg,e){
  if(!tg||!e)return;
  if(['pyreWheel','cryoCage','voidCollapse','originQuake','apexNova','magmaCrown'].includes(tg.kind)){
    tg.safeA=bossV490SafeAngle(e,tg.x,tg.y);tg.safeW=.68;
  }
  if(tg.kind==='geneLattice'){
    tg.gap=bossPhaseV431(e)>=3?88:100;tg.zoneRadius=bossPhaseV431(e)>=3?36:34;
  }
}
function bossV490DrawEscape(tg){
  if(!Number.isFinite(tg.safeA)||tg.active)return;
  const rad=tg.r||145,width=tg.safeW||.68;
  ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle='#d7fff1';ctx.shadowColor='#76ffe1';ctx.shadowBlur=10;
  ctx.globalAlpha=.56+.14*Math.sin(t*9);ctx.lineWidth=5;
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(tg.x,tg.y,rad,tg.safeA-width*.5,tg.safeA+width*.5);ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
}
function bossPhaseWatchdogV433(e,dt){
  if(!e)return;
  e._phaseResumeGraceV433=Math.max(0,(e._phaseResumeGraceV433||0)-dt);
  const live=bossHasLiveTelegraphV433(e);
  if(e.telegraphing&&!live&&e.patternLock<=0){e.telegraphing=null;e.patternCd=Math.max(e.patternCd||0,Math.max(0,(e._v490RecoverUntil||0)-t));}
  const activeAttack=live||e.telegraphing||e.burst>0||e.patternLock>0;
  if(!activeAttack&&(e.patternCd||0)<=0){e._phaseIdleV433=(e._phaseIdleV433||0)+dt;if(e._phaseIdleV433>.22){e.telegraphing=null;e.patternLock=0;e.patternCd=Math.max(.02,(e._v490RecoverUntil||0)-t);e._phaseIdleV433=0;}}
  else e._phaseIdleV433=0;
}

function directorMajorActive(){
  if(choosing||rewardChoosing||ended||extractionMode)return true;
  if(tacticalWave&&!tacticalWave.cleared)return true;
  if(rift)return true;
  if(purges.some(z=>z.warn>0||z.active>0))return true;
  return enemies.some(e=>!e.dead&&(e.elite||e.nemesis||e.finalBoss));
}
function directorCanStart(kind='major'){
  if(ended||choosing||rewardChoosing||extractionMode)return false;
  if(directorLock>0||directorRelief>0)return false;
  if(kind==='boss')return !tacticalWave&&!rift&&!purges.length&&!enemies.some(e=>!e.dead&&(e.elite||e.nemesis||e.finalBoss));
  if(kind==='hazard')return !tacticalWave&&!rift&&!purges.length&&!enemies.some(e=>!e.dead&&(e.elite||e.nemesis||e.finalBoss));
  return !directorMajorActive();
}
function directorHold(sec=1.2){directorLock=Math.max(directorLock,sec);}
function directorBreathe(sec=2.4){directorRelief=Math.max(directorRelief,sec);spawnAcc=0;}
function performanceBankReward(won){
  let bonus=0;
  bonus+=Math.min(18,rapidWaveClears*5);
  bonus+=Math.min(15,perfectDodges*3);
  bonus+=Math.min(12,Math.floor(maxChain/10)*2);
  if(contractDone)bonus+=8+riskTier().id*3;
  if(blueprintCompleted)bonus+=10;
  if(nemesisKilledThisRun)bonus+=8;
  if(riftSuccess>0&&riftFail===0)bonus+=5;
  const hpq=player.maxHp?player.hp/player.maxHp:0;if(won&&hpq>=.65)bonus+=5;
  const cap=38+Math.min(24,Math.max(0,(meta.stage||1)-1)*3);bonus=Math.min(cap,bonus);
  return won?bonus:Math.floor(bonus*.45);
}
function applyContractBossEdge(fb){
  if(!fb||!contractDone||contractFailed||contractEdgeUsed)return '';
  contractEdgeUsed=true;
  if(contract.id==='exterminate'){fb.hp*=.90;fb.maxHp=fb.hp;fb.exposed=Math.max(fb.exposed||0,2.6);return 'EXTERMINATION EDGE · TITAN CORE EXPOSED';}
  if(contract.id==='predator'){predation=0;frenzy=Math.max(frenzy,6);activeReady=0;return 'PREDATOR EDGE · FRENZY PRIMED';}
  player.hp=Math.min(player.maxHp,player.hp+player.maxHp*.22);fb.contractRegen=Math.min(fb.contractRegen||0,.0011);fb.exposed=Math.max(fb.exposed||0,1.4);return 'HARVEST EDGE · TITAN METABOLISM DISRUPTED';
}
function combatClarityLevel(){
  // V440: visibility is never reduced because a wave becomes dense; graphics selection is explicit.
  if(feedbackCfg().graphics!=='eco')return 0;
  if(combatClarityLevel._n===enemies.length&&Math.abs((combatClarityLevel._at??-99)-t)<.10)return combatClarityLevel._v||0;
  const c=livingCount(),touch=coarsePointer,wide=W/H>1.15;
  const dense=touch?(wide?90:72):110,critical=touch?(wide?160:125):220,v=c>=critical?2:c>=dense?1:0;
  combatClarityLevel._n=enemies.length;combatClarityLevel._at=t;combatClarityLevel._v=v;return v;
}
function updateAdaptiveHorde(dt){
  lootSweep=Math.max(0,lootSweep-dt);
  hordeSampleTimer-=dt;
  if(hordeSampleTimer>0)return;
  const span=2.5,gained=Math.max(0,kills-hordeKillsPrev);hordeKillsPrev=kills;hordeKps=gained/span;hordeSampleTimer=span;
  const hpq=player.maxHp?player.hp/player.maxHp:1;
  const desired=1.5+Math.min(3.8,t/42)+Math.min(2.0,powerScore()*.11);
  const ceiling=t<70?.96:t<FINAL_BOSS_TIME?1.00:1.04;
  let target=.94;
  if(hpq<.24)target=.80;
  else if(hordeKps>desired*1.28&&hpq>.52)target=ceiling;
  else if(hordeKps>desired*.95&&hpq>.42)target=Math.min(ceiling,.99);
  else if(hordeKps<desired*.42)target=.82;
  else if(hordeKps<desired*.68)target=.88;
  hordeTempo=clamp(hordeTempo+(target-hordeTempo)*.28,.78,ceiling);
}
function bossDangerActive(){
  return finalBossAlive()&&telegraphs.some(tg=>tg.life>0&&tg.owner&&tg.owner.finalBoss&&!['acidZone','nullZone','omegaPool'].includes(tg.kind));
}
function spawnRate(){
  let base=t<35?(.94-t*.006):t<85?(.72-(t-35)*.0030):t<130?(.57-(t-85)*.0022):Math.max(.32,.47-(t-130)*.0016);
  if(t>145)base*=.96;
  if(powerScore()>18&&t>115)base*=.97;
  base/=Math.sqrt(Math.max(.80,hordeTempo));
  if(contract?.id==='exterminate'&&!contractDone&&!contractFailed&&t>48)base*=.97;
  base/=Math.min(1.06,1+Math.max(0,meta.stage-1)*.008);
  const hp=player.maxHp?player.hp/player.maxHp:1;if(hp<.25)base*=1.48;else if(hp<.40)base*=1.24;
  return Math.max(.28,base);
}
function hordeBatchSize(){
  let n=t<58?1:t<112?2:t<168?3:4;
  if(powerScore()>=18&&t>165)n++;if(frenzy>0&&t>150)n++;if(finalBossAlive())n=Math.min(n,2);
  return clamp(n,1,5)|0;
}
function spawnHordePack(room){
  let count=Math.min(Math.max(0,room|0),hordeBatchSize());if(count<=0)return 0;
  const side=Math.random()*4|0,m=28,vr=cameraRect(m),anchorX=rand(vr.l+20,vr.r-20),anchorY=rand(vr.t+40,vr.b-40),span=22+count*7;
  const common=Math.random()<.72?(Math.random()<(t<80?.70:.58)?'crawler':'runner'):null;let made=0;
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){let type=(common&&i<count-1)?common:enemyType(),e=spawnEnemy(type),off=(i-(count-1)/2)*span/count+rand(-7,7);
    if(side===0){e.x=clamp(anchorX+off,16,WORLD_W-16);e.y=clamp(vr.t-rand(0,18),16,WORLD_H-16)}
    else if(side===1){e.x=clamp(vr.r+rand(0,18),16,WORLD_W-16);e.y=clamp(anchorY+off,16,WORLD_H-16)}
    else if(side===2){e.x=clamp(anchorX+off,16,WORLD_W-16);e.y=clamp(vr.b+rand(0,18),16,WORLD_H-16)}
    else{e.x=clamp(vr.l-rand(0,18),16,WORLD_W-16);e.y=clamp(anchorY+off,16,WORLD_H-16)}e.spawnFx=.18;made++;}
  return made;
}

function pointSegmentDistance(px,py,x1,y1,x2,y2){
  const vx=x2-x1,vy=y2-y1,wx=px-x1,wy=py-y1,c1=vx*wx+vy*wy;
  if(c1<=0)return Math.hypot(px-x1,py-y1);
  const c2=vx*vx+vy*vy;if(c2<=c1)return Math.hypot(px-x2,py-y2);
  const u=c1/c2;return Math.hypot(px-(x1+u*vx),py-(y1+u*vy));
}
function isEvasionTelegraph(tg){
  return ['bioMelee','bioSpikes','charge','alphaSlam','omegaPool','counterPurify','counterExecution','counterNull','apexSweep','omegaLattice','genesisFracture','abyssCross'].includes(tg.kind);
}
function telegraphDangerContains(tg){
  if(!tg)return false;
  if(tg._bio489)return tg.areas.some(g=>bioAreaHitV489(g,player));
  if(tg.kind==='bioMelee')return bioMeleeContainsV436(tg.owner,tg.a,tg.r);
  if(tg.kind==='bioSpikes')return tg.points.some(p=>Math.hypot(player.x-p.x,player.y-p.y)<tg.r+player.r);
  if(tg.kind==='acidBurst'&&isBioTitanV435(tg.owner))return bioMeleeContainsV436(tg.owner,tg.a,330);
  if(tg.kind==='charge'){
    const x2=tg.x+Math.cos(tg.a||0)*(tg.len||190),y2=tg.y+Math.sin(tg.a||0)*(tg.len||190);
    return pointSegmentDistance(player.x,player.y,tg.x,tg.y,x2,y2)<26+player.r;
  }
  if(tg.kind==='counterNull')return (tg.points||[]).some(p=>Math.hypot(player.x-p.x,player.y-p.y)<(tg.r||32)+player.r);
  if(tg.kind==='omegaLattice')return (tg.points||[]).some(p=>Math.hypot(player.x-p.x,player.y-p.y)<(tg.r||30)+player.r);
  if(tg.kind==='apexSweep'){const x2=tg.x+Math.cos(tg.a||0)*(tg.len||260),y2=tg.y+Math.sin(tg.a||0)*(tg.len||260);return pointSegmentDistance(player.x,player.y,tg.x,tg.y,x2,y2)<30+player.r;}
  if(tg.kind==='genesisFracture'){const a=tg.a||0,px=-Math.sin(a),py=Math.cos(a),dx=Math.cos(a),dy=Math.sin(a),len=tg.len||360;return (tg.offsets||[-48,0,48]).some(off=>{const cx=tg.x+px*off,cy=tg.y+py*off;return pointSegmentDistance(player.x,player.y,cx-dx*len*.5,cy-dy*len*.5,cx+dx*len*.5,cy+dy*len*.5)<(tg.w||18)+player.r;});}
  if(tg.kind==='abyssCross'){const a=tg.a||0,len=tg.len||300;for(const aa of [a,a+Math.PI/2]){const dx=Math.cos(aa),dy=Math.sin(aa);if(pointSegmentDistance(player.x,player.y,tg.x-dx*len*.5,tg.y-dy*len*.5,tg.x+dx*len*.5,tg.y+dy*len*.5)<(tg.w||20)+player.r)return true;}return false;}
  return Math.hypot(player.x-tg.x,player.y-tg.y)<(tg.r||44)+player.r;
}
function updateEvasionHUD(){
  const el=$('evasionBadge'),threat=enemies.find(e=>e.finalBoss)||enemies.find(e=>e.elite);
  if(!threat&&evasionCharge<=0&&riposteTime<=0){el.style.display='none';el.classList.remove('break');return}
  el.style.display='block';el.classList.toggle('break',riposteTime>0);
  if(riposteTime>0)el.innerHTML=`<b>COUNTER BREAK · ${riposteTime.toFixed(1)}s</b>EXPOSED CORE · DAMAGE +35%`;
  else el.innerHTML=`<b>PERFECT EVASION · ${perfectDodges}</b>COUNTER CHARGE ${Math.floor(evasionCharge)}% · ENTER DANGER, ESCAPE BEFORE IMPACT`;
}
function triggerCounterBreak(owner){
  evasionCharge=0;riposteTime=3.0;discover('skill:counterbreak');
  const target=(owner&&!owner.dead)?owner:(enemies.find(e=>e.finalBoss)||enemies.find(e=>e.elite));
  if(target){target.exposed=Math.max(target.exposed||0,3.0);target.patternLock=Math.max(target.patternLock||0,.28);}
  activeReady=Math.max(0,activeReady-1.2);predation=Math.min(100,predation+20);
  announceEncounter('COUNTER BREAK','EXPOSED CORE · DAMAGE +35%');
  flash('COUNTER BREAK',1150);cameraShake=9;beep(820,.09,'triangle',.038);setTimeout(()=>beep(1120,.13,'triangle',.032),85);
  particles.push({kind:'breakRing',x:target?.x||player.x,y:target?.y||player.y,r:10,max:96,life:.46,maxLife:.46});
}
function awardPerfectEvasion(owner){
  perfectDodges++;if(perfectDodges===1)discover('skill:evasion');evasionCharge=Math.min(100,evasionCharge+34);activeReady=Math.max(0,activeReady-.55);predation=Math.min(100,predation+7);
  if(owner&&!owner.dead)owner.exposed=Math.max(owner.exposed||0,.52);
  flash('PERFECT EVASION · +COUNTER',520);audioCue('perfect');hapticCue('perfect');
  for(let i=0;i<12;i++)particles.push({kind:'evade',x:player.x,y:player.y,vx:rand(-62,62),vy:rand(-62,62),r:rand(1.3,3),life:rand(.18,.42)});
  if(evasionCharge>=100)triggerCounterBreak(owner);
  updateEvasionHUD();
}

function triggerEvolutionSurge(tier='capstone',color='#ff9a63'){
  if(tier!=='capstone'&&tier!=='fusion')return;
  const fusionTier=tier==='fusion',radius=fusionTier?315:175;
  evolutionSurge=Math.max(evolutionSurge,fusionTier?12.0:3.8);lootSweep=Math.max(lootSweep,fusionTier?5.2:2.2);activeReady=fusionTier?0:Math.max(0,activeReady-1.1);
  player.hp=Math.min(player.maxHp,player.hp+player.maxHp*(fusionTier?.28:.05));player.inv=Math.max(player.inv,fusionTier?1.10:.38);
  for(const e of enemies){if(e.dead)continue;let dx=e.x-player.x,dy=e.y-player.y,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;if(d>radius+e.r)continue;if(e.finalBoss){if(fusionTier)e.exposed=Math.max(e.exposed||0,1.05);continue}if(e.elite){e.stunned=Math.max(e.stunned||0,fusionTier?.48:.18);if(fusionTier)e.exposed=Math.max(e.exposed||0,.42);continue}let push=fusionTier?118:58;e.kx=(e.kx||0)+dx/d*push;e.ky=(e.ky||0)+dy/d*push;e.stunned=Math.max(e.stunned||0,fusionTier?.92:.45);if(fusionTier)hurtEnemy(e,Math.min(80,e.maxHp*.62),'core');}
  particles.push({kind:'phaseRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:10,max:radius,life:.78,maxLife:.78,color});if(fusionTier){particles.push({kind:'fusionRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:18,max:radius*.82,life:.72,maxLife:.72});particles.push({kind:'staggerRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:24,max:radius*1.08,life:.82,maxLife:.82});}cameraShake=Math.max(cameraShake,fusionTier?20:9);
}

}

function drawEvolutionPulse(){
  if(!evolutionPulse)return;
  const q=clamp(evolutionPulse.life/evolutionPulse.max,0,1),p=1-q,c=evolutionPulse.color;
  ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.34*q;ctx.strokeStyle=c;ctx.shadowBlur=22;ctx.shadowColor=c;ctx.lineWidth=evolutionPulse.tier==='fusion'?3.2:2;
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(player.x,player.y,18+p*(evolutionPulse.tier==='fusion'?142:58),0,TAU);ctx.stroke();
  ctx.globalAlpha=.18*q;ctx.fillStyle=c;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(player.x,player.y,13+p*(evolutionPulse.tier==='fusion'?46:24),0,TAU);ctx.fill();
  if(evolutionPulse.tier==='fusion'){ctx.globalAlpha=.30*q;ctx.setLineDash([9,6]);for(let j=0;j<3;j++){ctx.lineWidth=2.4+j*.7;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(player.x,player.y,32+j*18+p*(115+j*18),t*(.8+j*.2)+j,t*(.8+j*.2)+j+Math.PI*(1.25+j*.16));ctx.stroke();}ctx.setLineDash([]);ctx.globalAlpha=.22*q;ctx.lineWidth=2;for(let i=0;i<16;i++){let a=i/16*TAU+t*.35;let r0=28+p*26,r1=78+p*130;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(player.x+Math.cos(a)*r0,player.y+Math.sin(a)*r0);ctx.lineTo(player.x+Math.cos(a)*r1,player.y+Math.sin(a)*r1);ctx.stroke();}}
  ctx.restore();
}
function bossIntentLabel(e){
  if(!e||!e.finalBoss||!e.telegraphing)return null;
  const map={charge:['SIDESTEP','CHARGE'],acidBurst:['FIND GAP','ACID BLOOM'],alphaSlam:['MOVE OUT','GROUND BREAK'],omegaPool:['RELOCATE',e.apex?'OMEGA POOL':'BIO POOL'],apexNova:['FIND GAP',e.apex?'ORIGIN NOVA':'CORE NOVA'],counterPurify:['BACK OFF','PURIFIER'],counterExecution:['MOVE','EXECUTION'],counterNull:['BREAK AWAY','NULL FIELD'],frostLine:['SIDESTEP','CRYO LANCE']};
  return map[e.telegraphing]||['DODGE',String(e.telegraphing).replace(/([A-Z])/g,' $1').toUpperCase()];
}
function updateBossIntent(e){
  const el=$('bossIntentBadge'),info=bossIntentLabel(e);
  if(!info){el.style.display='none';el.innerHTML='';el.dataset.intent='';el.classList.remove('danger');return}
  const key=info.join('|');if(el.dataset.intent!==key){el.dataset.intent=key;audioCue('intent');hapticCue('warning')}
  const remain=Math.max(0,Number(e.patternLock)||0);
  el.innerHTML=`<span class="intentVerb">${info[0]}</span><span class="intentAttack">${info[1]}</span><span class="intentTime">${remain.toFixed(1)}s</span>`;el.style.display='flex';el.classList.add('danger');
}
function milestonePatternPool(e){
  if(!e?.apex)return null;const p=Math.max(1,e.apexPhase||1);
  if(e.superApex){
    if(p===1)return ['acid','charge','summon','apexSweep'];
    if(p===2)return ['omegaLattice','apexSweep','acid','summon'];
    if(p===3)return ['abyssCross','omegaLattice','summon','apexNova'];
    return ['abyssCross','omegaLattice','apexNova','charge','abyssCross'];
  }
  if(p===1)return ['acid','charge','summon','apexSweep'];
  if(p===2)return ['genesisFracture','apexSweep','summon','acid'];
  return ['genesisFracture','apexNova','charge','apexSweep','genesisFracture'];
}
function bossPhaseInfo(e){if(!e||!e.finalBoss)return {phase:0,total:0};const total=e.superApex?4:e.apex?3:3,phase=e.apex?Math.max(1,e.apexPhase||1):Math.max(1,e.titanPhase||1);return {phase,total}}
function updateBossPhaseStrip(e){

  el.style.display='flex';if(el.dataset.key===key)return;el.dataset.key=key;el.innerHTML='';
  for(let i=1;i<=info.total;i++){let p=document.createElement('i');p.className=i<info.phase?'on':i===info.phase?'current':'';el.appendChild(p)}
}

}
function playerBossCounterMode(){
  if(fusion.sunLance)return 'prism';
  if(fusion.broodstorm)return 'brood';
  if(fusion.plagueVortex)return 'purify';
  if(fusion.redReaper)return 'execution';
  if(fusion.voidCarapace)return 'null';
  if(evoForm.arm)return 'prism';
  if(evoForm.mouth)return 'purify';
  if(evoForm.tail)return 'execution';
  if(evoState.wing)return 'prism';
  if(evoState.leg)return 'execution';
  const ranked=[['arm',state.arm],['mouth',state.mouth],['tail',state.tail],['wing',state.wing],['leg',state.leg]].sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);
  return ranked[0]?.[0]==='mouth'?'purify':ranked[0]?.[0]==='tail'||ranked[0]?.[0]==='leg'?'execution':ranked[0]?.[0]==='wing'?'prism':'prism';
}
function assignBossCounter(e){
  if(!e?.finalBoss)return;
  e.counterMode=playerBossCounterMode();e.counterCd=e.superApex?3.5:e.apex?4.2:5.0;e.counterPulse=0;e.prism=0;e.purify=0;
  const c=BOSS_COUNTERS[e.counterMode];e.counterName=c.name;e.counterTarget=c.target;discover('bossCounter:'+e.counterMode);
  updateCounterBadge(e);
}
function updateCounterBadge(e){
  const el=$('counterBadge');
  if(!e||!e.finalBoss||e.dead||!e.counterMode){el.style.display='none';el.classList.remove('active');return}
  const c=BOSS_COUNTERS[e.counterMode],active=(e.prism||0)>0||(e.purify||0)>0;
  el.style.display='block';el.classList.toggle('active',active||e.telegraphing==='counter');
  let status=active?(e.prism>0?'REFRACTION ACTIVE':'PURIFICATION ACTIVE'):(e.telegraphing==='counter'?'COUNTER CHARGING':'COUNTER · TELEGRAPHED PATTERN');
  el.innerHTML=`<b>${c.name}</b>${c.target}<br>${status}`;
}
function triggerBossCounter(e){
  if(!e||e.dead||!e.finalBoss||e.telegraphing||e.patternLock>0)return;
  const mode=e.counterMode||playerBossCounterMode(),hard=e.superApex||riskTier().id>=2;
  e.telegraphing='counter';e.patternLock=mode==='null'?1.0:.86;
  if(mode==='prism'){
    telegraphs.push({kind:'counterPrism',owner:e,x:e.x,y:e.y,r:e.r+42,life:.86,max:.86});
  }else if(mode==='brood'){
    telegraphs.push({kind:'counterBrood',owner:e,x:e.x,y:e.y,r:e.r+54,life:.92,max:.92});
  }else if(mode==='purify'){
    telegraphs.push({kind:'counterPurify',owner:e,x:e.x,y:e.y,r:hard?126:108,life:.92,max:.92});
  }else if(mode==='execution'){
    telegraphs.push({kind:'counterExecution',owner:e,x:player.x,y:player.y,r:hard?58:50,life:.82,max:.82,locked:true});
  }else{
    const pts=[];
    const base=Math.atan2(player.y-e.y,player.x-e.x);
    for(let i=0;i<(hard?4:3);i++){let a=base+i*TAU/(hard?4:3)+.45,rr=54+i*18;pts.push({x:clamp(player.x+Math.cos(a)*rr,38,WORLD_W-38),y:clamp(player.y+Math.sin(a)*rr,38,WORLD_H-38)})}
    telegraphs.push({kind:'counterNull',owner:e,x:e.x,y:e.y,points:pts,r:32,life:1.0,max:1.0,locked:true});
  }
  const c=BOSS_COUNTERS[mode];flash(c.telegraph+' · ADAPT',620);beep(112,.08,'sawtooth',.025);
  e.counterCd=(e.superApex?4.2:e.apex?5.0:6.0)+Math.random()*.9;
}

function enemyDisplayName(e){if(!e)return '';return e.bossName||e.eliteName||({crawler:'CRAWLER',runner:'RUNNER',brute:'BRUTE',spitter:'SPITTER',warden:'WARDEN',froststalker:'FROST STALKER',symbiote:'SYMBIOTE',embermaw:'EMBERMAW',boss:'TITAN'}[e.type]||String(e.type).toUpperCase())}
function setFocusEnemy(e){
  if(!e||e.dead){focusEnemy=null;return}
  focusEnemy=e;focusHintTimer=.9;discover('combat:focus');$('focusHint').textContent='FOCUS · '+enemyDisplayName(e);$('focusHint').style.display='block';$('focusHint').classList.remove('coachStrong');beep(520,.035,'triangle',.014);hapticCue('tap');if(firstRunCoach()&&coachStep<=4){coachStep=5;coachHint('FOCUS LOCKED · AUTO ATTACK PRIORITIZES THIS TARGET',2.8);updateCoachVisuals()}
}
function nearest(max=9999){
  if(focusEnemy&&focusEnemy.dead)focusEnemy=null;
  if(focusEnemy&&!focusEnemy.dead){let fd=dist2(player,focusEnemy),limit=max>=9000?Infinity:(max*1.28)*(max*1.28);if(fd<=limit)return focusEnemy}
  const lim=max>=9000?Infinity:max*max;
  let priority=null,ps=Infinity,best=null,bd=lim;
  const test=e=>{if(e.dead)return;let d=dist2(player,e);if(d>lim*1.22)return;
    let bias=e.finalBoss?.38:e.elite?.52:e.type==='warden'?.72:e.type==='spitter'?.84:1,score=d*bias;
    if((e.finalBoss||e.elite||e.type==='warden'||e.type==='spitter')&&score<ps){ps=score;priority=e}
    if(d<bd){bd=d;best=e}
  };
  if(max<9000&&enemyGridV424.length)enemyEachCandidateCircleV425(player.x,player.y,max*1.11,e=>{test(e);});
  else for(const e of enemies)test(e);
  if(priority&&dist2(player,priority)<=lim*1.22)return priority;return best
}

function weaponPairKey(a,b){return [a,b].sort().join('+')}
function weaponPairTier(a,b){const x=state[a]||0,y=state[b]||0;return Math.min(x,y)>=2?Math.min(x,y):0}
function activeWeaponKeys(){return ['arm','mouth','tail','wing','leg'].filter(k=>(state[k]||0)>0)}
function activePairSynergies(){const ks=activeWeaponKeys(),out=[];for(let i=0;i<ks.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<ks.length;j++){const tier=weaponPairTier(ks[i],ks[j]);if(tier>=2)out.push({key:weaponPairKey(ks[i],ks[j]),tier,name:WEAPON_PAIR_NAMES[weaponPairKey(ks[i],ks[j])]||'BIO SYNC'});}return out}
function triadResonanceActive(){const ks=activeWeaponKeys();return ks.length>=3&&ks.slice(0,3).every(k=>(state[k]||0)>=3)}
function triadDamageMul(kind){return ['arm','artillery','acid','tail','wing','leg'].includes(kind)&&triadResonanceActive()?1.08:1}
function triadTempoMul(){return triadResonanceActive()?.96:1}
function weaponPairDamageMul(e,kind){let m=1;
  const has=(a,b)=>weaponPairTier(a,b)>=2,boost=t=>1.035+Math.min(5,t)*.012;
  if(has('arm','mouth')){let q=boost(weaponPairTier('arm','mouth'));if((kind==='arm'||kind==='artillery')&&(e.corrosion||0)>0)m*=q;if(kind==='acid'&&(e.puncture||0)>0)m*=q;}
  if(has('arm','tail')){let q=boost(weaponPairTier('arm','tail'));if((kind==='arm'||kind==='artillery')&&(e.cutMark||0)>0)m*=q;if(kind==='tail'&&(e.puncture||0)>0)m*=q;}
  if(has('arm','wing')){let q=boost(weaponPairTier('arm','wing'));if((kind==='arm'||kind==='artillery')&&(e.cutMark||0)>0)m*=q;if(kind==='wing'&&(e.puncture||0)>0)m*=q;}
  if(has('arm','leg')){let q=boost(weaponPairTier('arm','leg'));if((kind==='arm'||kind==='artillery')&&(e.coreShock||0)>0)m*=q;if(kind==='leg'&&(e.puncture||0)>0)m*=q;}
  if(has('mouth','tail')){let q=boost(weaponPairTier('mouth','tail'));if(kind==='acid'&&(e.cutMark||0)>0)m*=q;if(kind==='tail'&&(e.corrosion||0)>0)m*=q;}
  if(has('mouth','wing')){let q=boost(weaponPairTier('mouth','wing'));if(kind==='acid'&&(e.cutMark||0)>0)m*=q;if(kind==='wing'&&(e.corrosion||0)>0)m*=q;}
  if(has('mouth','leg')){let q=boost(weaponPairTier('mouth','leg'));if(kind==='acid'&&(e.coreShock||0)>0)m*=q;if(kind==='leg'&&(e.corrosion||0)>0)m*=q;}
  if(has('tail','wing')&&(kind==='tail'||kind==='wing'))m*=1.06+Math.min(5,weaponPairTier('tail','wing'))*.01;
  if(has('tail','leg')){let q=boost(weaponPairTier('tail','leg'));if(kind==='tail'&&(e.coreShock||0)>0)m*=q;if(kind==='leg'&&(e.cutMark||0)>0)m*=q;}
  if(has('wing','leg')){let q=boost(weaponPairTier('wing','leg'));if(kind==='wing'&&(e.coreShock||0)>0)m*=q;if(kind==='leg'&&(e.cutMark||0)>0)m*=q;}
  return m;
}

function weaponPairProcKey(e,kind){
  const c=[];const add=k=>c.push({key:k,tier:weaponPairTier(...k.split('+'))});
  if(weaponPairTier('arm','mouth')>=2&&(((kind==='arm'||kind==='artillery')&&(e.corrosion||0)>0)||(kind==='acid'&&(e.puncture||0)>0)))add('arm+mouth');
  if(weaponPairTier('arm','tail')>=2&&(((kind==='arm'||kind==='artillery')&&(e.cutMark||0)>0)||(kind==='tail'&&(e.puncture||0)>0)))add('arm+tail');
  if(weaponPairTier('arm','wing')>=2&&(((kind==='arm'||kind==='artillery')&&(e.cutMark||0)>0)||(kind==='wing'&&(e.puncture||0)>0)))add('arm+wing');
  if(weaponPairTier('arm','leg')>=2&&(((kind==='arm'||kind==='artillery')&&(e.coreShock||0)>0)||(kind==='leg'&&(e.puncture||0)>0)))add('arm+leg');
  if(weaponPairTier('mouth','tail')>=2&&((kind==='acid'&&(e.cutMark||0)>0)||(kind==='tail'&&(e.corrosion||0)>0)))add('mouth+tail');
  if(weaponPairTier('mouth','wing')>=2&&((kind==='acid'&&(e.cutMark||0)>0)||(kind==='wing'&&(e.corrosion||0)>0)))add('mouth+wing');
  if(weaponPairTier('mouth','leg')>=2&&((kind==='acid'&&(e.coreShock||0)>0)||(kind==='leg'&&(e.corrosion||0)>0)))add('mouth+leg');
  if(weaponPairTier('tail','wing')>=2&&(kind==='tail'||kind==='wing'))add('tail+wing');
  if(weaponPairTier('tail','leg')>=2&&((kind==='tail'&&(e.coreShock||0)>0)||(kind==='leg'&&(e.cutMark||0)>0)))add('tail+leg');
  if(weaponPairTier('wing','leg')>=2&&((kind==='wing'&&(e.coreShock||0)>0)||(kind==='leg'&&(e.cutMark||0)>0)))add('wing+leg');
  if(!c.length)return '';c.sort((a,b)=>b.tier-a.tier);return c[0].key;
}

function telemetryEvoProfile(){try{let arr=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('EVOLVE_V575_TEST_RUN_TELEMETRY_V1')||'[]');if(!Array.isArray(arr)||!arr.length)return {n:0,one:0,two:0,three:0,apexWin:0,omegaWin:0};arr=arr.slice(-16);const n=arr.length,rate=k=>arr.filter(x=>(x.evos||0)>=k).length/n,mil=(m)=>{let q=arr.filter(x=>x.milestone===m);return q.length?q.filter(x=>x.won).length/q.length:0};return {n,one:rate(1),two:rate(2),three:rate(3),apexWin:mil('APEX'),omegaWin:mil('OMEGA')};}catch(e){return {n:0,one:0,two:0,three:0,apexWin:0,omegaWin:0}}}

function telemetryMultiEvoBias(){const p=telemetryEvoProfile();if(p.n<6)return 0;if(p.two<.24)return 1;if(p.two>.68)return -1;return 0}

function attackTempoMul(){
  let m=1-Math.max(0,genes.haste||0)*.040-Math.max(0,genes.phase||0)*.012;
  if(synergy.overdrive)m*=.88;m*=evoResonanceTempoMul()*triadTempoMul();
  return clamp(m,.58,1);
}
function coreDamageBoost(){
  return (1+Math.max(0,genes.core||0)*.05)*(player.dmgMul||1);
}
function weaponSync(kind){
  if(kind==='arm')return (genes.hydra||0)>=2;
  if(kind==='mouth')return (genes.core||0)>=2;
  if(kind==='tail')return (genes.haste||0)>=2;
  if(kind==='wing')return (genes.predator||0)>=2;
  if(kind==='leg')return (genes.core||0)>=2;
  return false;
}
function weaponSyncName(kind){return {arm:'SPLIT CHAMBER',mouth:'CATALYTIC ACID',tail:'BLOOD DANCE',wing:'RAPTOR MATRIX',leg:'GRAVITY STEP'}[kind]||'';}
function armProjectileCount(){
  const lv=Math.max(0,state.arm|0),base=lv<=1?1:lv===2?2:lv===3?2:lv===4?3:4;
  return base+((genes.hydra||0)>=2?1:0);
}
function acidProjectileCount(){
  const lv=Math.max(0,state.mouth|0),base=lv<=2?1:lv<=4?2:3;
  return base+((genes.hydra||0)>=3?1:0);
}
function angleDelta(a,b){let d=(a-b)%(Math.PI*2);if(d>Math.PI)d-=Math.PI*2;if(d<-Math.PI)d+=Math.PI*2;return Math.abs(d)}
function weaponStarFeature(kind,lv){
  const map={
    arm:['','SINGLE SHOT','TWIN VOLLEY','PIERCE + PRECISION','TRIPLE VOLLEY','OVERBORE ROUND'],
    mouth:['','ACID GLOB','CORROSIVE SPLASH','TWIN GLOB','DEEP CORROSION','PLAGUE BURST'],
    tail:['','FRONT SLASH','WIDE ARC','360 SWEEP','DOUBLE SWEEP','EXECUTION'],
    wing:['','TWIN BLADES','TRIPLE BLADES','RETURN CUT','WIDE ORBIT','FEATHER STORM'],
    leg:['','STOMP','HEAVY KNOCKBACK','SHOCKWAVE','DOUBLE STOMP','MOMENTUM CRUSH']
  };
  return map[kind]?.[lv]||'';
}
// V552: the largest circle contained in one unzoomed viewport, centered on the shooter.
// CSS world units only: camera zoom, preferred zoom and DPR never increase this limit.
function playerBasicRangeV552(){return Math.max(1,Math.min(W,H)*.5);}
function withinBasicRangeV552(e,origin=player,range=playerBasicRangeV552()){
 const dx=e.x-origin.x,dy=e.y-origin.y;return dx*dx+dy*dy<=range*range+1e-7;
}
function tagBasicShotV552(b){
 const range=playerBasicRangeV552(),dx=b.x-player.x,dy=b.y-player.y,speed=Math.hypot(b.vx,b.vy),ux=speed?b.vx/speed:0,uy=speed?b.vy/speed:0,edge=Math.max(0,range-(b.r||0)),dot=dx*ux+dy*uy;
 b.basicRangeV552=range;b.rangeOriginXV552=player.x;b.rangeOriginYV552=player.y;
 b.rangeLeftV552=speed?Math.max(0,-dot+Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,dot*dot+edge*edge-dx*dx-dy*dy))):0;
 return b;
}
function basicShotCanHitV552(b,e){
 if(!Number.isFinite(b.basicRangeV552))return true;
 const dx=e.x-b.rangeOriginXV552,dy=e.y-b.rangeOriginYV552;
 return dx*dx+dy*dy<=b.basicRangeV552*b.basicRangeV552+1e-7;
}
function stepBasicShotV552(b,dt){
 const speed=Math.hypot(b.vx,b.vy),life=Math.max(0,b.life),step=Math.min(dt,life,speed?b.rangeLeftV552/speed:0);
 b.x+=b.vx*step;b.y+=b.vy*step;b.rangeLeftV552=Math.max(0,b.rangeLeftV552-speed*step);
 const end=b.rangeLeftV552<=1e-7||step>=life-1e-9||!speed;
 // Keep the final in-range segment eligible for collision, then expire after hit resolution.
 b.life=end?Number.EPSILON:Math.max(0,life-step);return end;
}

function nearestBasicTargetV552(max){
 const range=Math.min(max,playerBasicRangeV552()),limit=range*range;
 if(focusEnemy?.dead)focusEnemy=null;
 if(focusEnemy&&withinBasicRangeV552(focusEnemy,player,range))return focusEnemy;
 let priority=null,priorityScore=Infinity,best=null,bestDistance=limit+1e-7;
 const test=e=>{if(e.dead)return;const d=dist2(player,e);if(d>limit+1e-7)return;
  const bias=e.finalBoss?.38:e.elite?.52:e.type==='warden'?.72:e.type==='spitter'?.84:1,score=d*bias;
  if((e.finalBoss||e.elite||e.type==='warden'||e.type==='spitter')&&score<priorityScore){priority=e;priorityScore=score;}
  if(d<bestDistance){best=e;bestDistance=d;}
 };
 if(enemyGridV424.length)enemyEachCandidateCircleV425(player.x,player.y,range,test);else for(const e of enemies)test(e);
 return priority||best;
}
function pushBasicShotV552(b){bullets.push(tagBasicShotV552(b));}

function firePlayer(dt){
  player.shoot-=dt;player.acid-=dt;player.tailCd-=dt;player.wingCd=(player.wingCd||0)-dt;player.legCd=(player.legCd||0)-dt;
  const coreBoost=coreDamageBoost(),tempo=attackTempoMul()*(evolutionSurge>0?.58:1),armBoost=coreBoost*(synergy.artillery?1.18:1)*(evoState.arm?(player.armEvoPower||1):1);

  // ARM: 1★ single → 2★ twin → 3★ precision/pierce → 4★ triple → 5★ overbore.
  if(state.arm>0&&player.shoot<=0){
    const lv=state.arm;let target=nearestBasicTargetV552(202+lv*14);
    if(target){let a=Math.atan2(target.y-player.y,target.x-player.x);
      let siegeActive=(player.origin==='forge'&&activeDuration>0),forgeBurst=(player.origin==='forge'&&((player.forgeVolley=(player.forgeVolley||0)+1)%6===0));
      const sun=evoState.arm,split=weaponSync('arm');let shots=sun?(1+((genes.hydra||0)>=4?1:0)):armProjectileCount()+(player.bonusShots||0)+(forgeBurst?1:0)+(siegeActive?(originAscension?2:1):0),spread=shots===1?0:(sun?.022:.098);
      const pierce=sun?14:1+(lv>=3?1:0)+(lv>=5?1:0)+(split?1:0);
      for(let i=0;i<shots;i++){
        let aa=a+(i-(shots-1)/2)*spread,px=player.x+Math.cos(aa)*18,py=player.y+Math.sin(aa)*18;
        pushBasicShotV552({x:px,y:py,vx:Math.cos(aa)*(sun?720:320+lv*24),vy:Math.sin(aa)*(sun?720:320+lv*24),r:(sun?3.4:4+lv*.72)*PROJECTILE_SCALE,dmg:(sun?(42+lv*7.4):(9.8+lv*3.4))*armBoost*(player.origin==='forge'?1.06:1),life:sun?1.02:1.25,pierce,kind:sun?'artillery':((synergy.artillery||forgeBurst)?'artillery':'arm'),blast:sun?(split?38:28):(synergy.artillery?31:(forgeBurst?24:0)),blastMul:sun?.56:undefined,lance:sun});
        for(let j=0;j<3;j++)particles.push({kind:'muzzle',x:px,y:py,vx:Math.cos(aa)*rand(12,42)+rand(-20,20),vy:Math.sin(aa)*rand(12,42)+rand(-20,20),r:rand(1.8,3.6),life:rand(.12,.22),color:'warm'});
      }
      if(lv>=3){const cadence=lv>=5?5:lv>=4?7:8;if(((player.precisionVolley=(player.precisionVolley||0)+1)%cadence)===0){let px=player.x+Math.cos(a)*20,py=player.y+Math.sin(a)*20;pushBasicShotV552({x:px,y:py,vx:Math.cos(a)*580,vy:Math.sin(a)*580,r:3.0*PROJECTILE_SCALE,dmg:(20+lv*5.0)*armBoost,life:1.05,pierce:5+(lv>=5?2:0)+(evoForm.arm==='rail'?3:0),kind:'arm',blast:0,precision:true});particles.push({kind:'muzzle',x:px,y:py,vx:Math.cos(a)*65,vy:Math.sin(a)*65,r:4.2,life:.20,color:'warm'});}}
      if(lv>=5&&((player.overboreCycle=(player.overboreCycle||0)+1)%4===0)){let px=player.x+Math.cos(a)*20,py=player.y+Math.sin(a)*20;pushBasicShotV552({x:px,y:py,vx:Math.cos(a)*470,vy:Math.sin(a)*470,r:5.4*PROJECTILE_SCALE,dmg:(26+lv*5.2)*armBoost,life:1.12,pierce:4+(evoForm.arm==='rail'?3:0),kind:'artillery',blast:26,blastMul:.48,overbore:true});particles.push({kind:'motionRing',x:px,y:py,r:3,max:22,life:.22,maxLife:.22,color:'dash'});cameraShake=Math.max(cameraShake,3);}
      if(fusion.sunLance&&((player.fusionShot=(player.fusionShot||0)+1)%2===0)){let px=player.x+Math.cos(a)*20,py=player.y+Math.sin(a)*20;pushBasicShotV552({x:px,y:py,vx:Math.cos(a)*680,vy:Math.sin(a)*680,r:3.2*PROJECTILE_SCALE,dmg:(88+lv*12)*armBoost,life:1.1,pierce:10,kind:'artillery',blast:48,blastMul:.62,lance:true,overcharge:true});particles.push({kind:'fusion',x:px,y:py,vx:Math.cos(a)*80,vy:Math.sin(a)*80,r:5,life:.25});cameraShake=Math.max(cameraShake,4);}
      if(sun){particles.push({kind:'fusionRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:8,max:46,life:.18,maxLife:.18});if((player.sunFx=(player.sunFx||0)+1)%2===0)particles.push({kind:'phaseRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:6,max:72,life:.24,maxLife:.24,color:'#ffd66f'});}
      player.armKick=.18;player.shoot=Math.max(sun?.22:.22,(player.baseCd-lv*.055)*tempo*(sun?.86:1)*(player.origin==='forge'&&activeDuration>0?.52:1)*(frenzy>0?.76:1));audioCue('arm',lv);
    }else player.shoot=.12;
  }

  // MOUTH: splash starts at 2★, extra glob at 3★, deeper corrosion at 4★, plague burst at 5★.
  if(state.mouth>0&&player.acid<=0){
    const lv=state.mouth,plague=evoState.mouth,catalytic=weaponSync('mouth');let target=nearestBasicTargetV552(plague?340:270);
    if(target){let a=Math.atan2(target.y-player.y,target.x-player.x),n=plague?Math.max(3,acidProjectileCount()):acidProjectileCount(),cycle=(player.acidCycle=(player.acidCycle||0)+1),plagueBurst=lv>=5&&cycle%(plague?3:4)===0;
      for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
        let aa=a+(n===1?0:rand(-.20,.20)),px=player.x+Math.cos(aa)*24,py=player.y+Math.sin(aa)*24;
        const blast=plagueBurst?(plague?86:62):(lv>=2?12+lv*5+(catalytic?8:0):0),blastMul=plagueBurst?(plague?.58:.50):(catalytic?.42:.34);
        pushBasicShotV552({x:px,y:py,vx:Math.cos(aa)*rand(205,250),vy:Math.sin(aa)*rand(205,250),r:(5.4+(plagueBurst?1.5:0))*PROJECTILE_SCALE,dmg:(10.2+lv*5.0)*(plague?1.24:1)*coreBoost*(player.acidPower||1),life:1.08,pierce:1+(lv>=3?1:0)+(lv>=5?1:0)+(player.origin==='acid'?1:0)+(originAscension==='PLAGUE WYRM'?1:0)+(evoForm.mouth==='virulent'?2:0),kind:'acid',blast,blastMul,slowDur:lv>=4?1.45:.95,plagueBurst});
        for(let j=0;j<3;j++)particles.push({kind:'muzzle',x:px,y:py,vx:Math.cos(aa)*rand(8,22)+rand(-18,18),vy:Math.sin(aa)*rand(8,22)+rand(-18,18),r:rand(2.2,4.2),life:rand(.16,.28),color:'acid'});
      }
      if(plagueBurst){particles.push({kind:'supportPulse',x:target.x,y:target.y,r:8,max:plague?92:66,life:.34,maxLife:.34,color:'acid'});cameraShake=Math.max(cameraShake,plague?5:3);}
      if(plague){let aura=178,cap=0;if(cycle%2===0)enemyEachCandidateCircleV425(player.x,player.y,aura+40,e=>{if(e.dead||!withinBasicRangeV552(e))return;if(dist2(player,e)<(aura+e.r)*(aura+e.r)){hurtEnemy(e,(7.4+lv*1.75)*coreBoost*(player.acidPower||1),'acid');e.slow=Math.max(e.slow||0,2.2);if(++cap>=16)return false;}});particles.push({kind:'supportPulse',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:12,max:aura,life:.38,maxLife:.38,color:'acid'});}
      player.acidKick=.24;player.acid=Math.max(.30,(1.15-lv*.125-(player.acidHaste||0))*tempo*(frenzy>0?.78:1)*(plague?.68:1));audioCue('acid',lv);
    }else player.acid=.2;
  }

  // TAIL: directional at low stars, then full sweep, double sweep, execution at 5★.
  if(state.tail>0&&player.tailCd<=0){
    const lv=state.tail,bladeAsc=originAscension==='BLADE SERAPH',execution=evoState.tail,bloodDance=weaponSync('tail');
    let radius=60+lv*17+(synergy.toxicCyclone?14:0)+(bladeAsc?14:0)+(execution?30:0),dmg=(15.5+lv*10.0)*coreBoost*(synergy.toxicCyclone?1.15:1)*(player.tailPower||1),tailHits=0;
    const aim=nearest(radius+90),facing=aim?Math.atan2(aim.y-player.y,aim.x-player.x):(player.look||0),halfArc=execution?Math.PI+.01:(lv===1?.88:lv===2?1.62:Math.PI+.01),doubleSweep=lv>=4||bladeAsc||execution;
    particles.push({kind:'slash',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:radius,life:.32,max:.32,start:facing-halfArc,end:facing+halfArc});
    if(doubleSweep)particles.push({kind:'slash',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:radius*.94,life:.35,max:.35,start:facing+Math.PI-.95,end:facing+Math.PI+.95});
    if(execution)particles.push({kind:'slash',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:radius*.78,life:.38,max:.38,start:facing-2.45,end:facing+2.45});
    enemyEachCandidateCircleV425(player.x,player.y,radius+40,e=>{if(e.dead)return;let inside=dist2(player,e)<(radius+e.r)**2;if(!inside)return;let a=Math.atan2(e.y-player.y,e.x-player.x);if(lv<3&&angleDelta(a,facing)>halfArc)return;tailHits++;hurtEnemy(e,dmg,synergy.toxicCyclone?'acid':'tail');if(doubleSweep)hurtEnemy(e,dmg*(execution?.66:.43),'tail');if(execution){hurtEnemy(e,dmg*.72,'tail');hurtEnemy(e,dmg*.34,'tail');}if(synergy.toxicCyclone)e.slow=Math.max(e.slow||0,1.1);});
    if(tailHits===0){let tw=nearest(300);if(tw){hurtEnemy(tw,dmg*(lv>=2?.82:.62),'tail');tw.stagger=(tw.stagger||0)+10+lv*2;let splash=0;enemyEachCandidateCircleV425(tw.x,tw.y,76,x=>{if(x===tw||x.dead||x.finalBoss||x.elite)return;if((x.x-tw.x)**2+(x.y-tw.y)**2<(38+x.r)**2&&splash<(lv>=4?3:2)){hurtEnemy(x,dmg*(lv>=4?.40:.30),'tail');splash++;if(splash>=(lv>=4?3:2))return false;}});particles.push({kind:'weaponHit',x:tw.x,y:tw.y,vx:0,vy:0,r:2,max:24,life:.18,maxLife:.18,color:'tail',a:Math.atan2(tw.y-player.y,tw.x-player.x)});tailHits=1;}}
    if(lv>=4&&tailHits)player.hp=Math.min(player.maxHp,player.hp+Math.min(bloodDance?4.4:3.2,tailHits*(bloodDance?.64:.46)));if(execution&&tailHits)player.hp=Math.min(player.maxHp,player.hp+Math.min(13.5,tailHits*1.75));if(lv>=3&&tailHits>=3)player.inv=Math.max(player.inv,.055);
    player.tailKick=.34;player.tailCd=Math.max(.44,(1.50-lv*.180-(synergy.toxicCyclone?.08:0)-(player.tailHaste||0))*tempo*(bloodDance?.88:1)*(frenzy>0?.78:1)*(execution?.58:1));cameraShake=5;audioCue('tail',lv);
  }

  // WING: blade count, return cut, wider orbit, then periodic feather storm.
  if(state.wing>0&&player.wingCd<=0){
    const lv=state.wing,phantom=evoState.wing,raptor=weaponSync('wing'),range=Math.min(playerBasicRangeV552(),142+lv*19+(lv>=4?18:0)+(phantom?72:0)),count=(lv===1?2:lv===2?3:lv===3?3:lv===4?4:5)+(phantom?5:0),dmg=(8.5+lv*5.8)*(phantom?1.28:1)*coreBoost*(player.wingPower||1),targets=collectNearestV426(nearestScratchAV426,player.x,player.y,range,count,e=>withinBasicRangeV552(e));
    let n=0;for(const e of targets){n++;let focusBoost=(raptor&&(e.elite||e.finalBoss))?1.25:1;hurtEnemy(e,dmg*(1+(n===1?.12:0))*focusBoost,'wing');if(lv>=3)hurtEnemy(e,dmg*(phantom?.72:.38)*focusBoost,'wing');if(phantom){hurtEnemy(e,dmg*.42*focusBoost,'wing');hurtEnemy(e,dmg*.22*focusBoost,'wing');}e.stagger=(e.stagger||0)+5+lv;particles.push({kind:'weaponHit',x:e.x,y:e.y,vx:0,vy:0,r:2,max:20+(phantom?8:0),life:.18,maxLife:.18,color:'wing',a:Math.atan2(e.y-player.y,e.x-player.x)});}
    if(targets.length&&count>targets.length){const focus=targets[0],extra=count-targets.length;for(let i=0;i<extra;i++)hurtEnemy(focus,dmg*(phantom?.92:.46)*(raptor&&(focus.elite||focus.finalBoss)?1.22:1),'wing');focus.stagger=(focus.stagger||0)+extra*(3+lv*.5);}
    if(lv>=4&&targets.length){let last=targets[targets.length-1],chain=collectNearestV426(nearestScratchBV426,last.x,last.y,74,1,e=>!targets.includes(e)&&withinBasicRangeV552(e))[0];if(chain){hurtEnemy(chain,dmg*.58,'wing');particles.push({kind:'weaponHit',x:chain.x,y:chain.y,vx:0,vy:0,r:2,max:24,life:.18,maxLife:.18,color:'wing',a:Math.atan2(chain.y-last.y,chain.x-last.x)});}}
    const storm=lv>=5&&((player.wingStormCycle=(player.wingStormCycle||0)+1)%(phantom?1:3)===0);if(storm){let extra=collectNearestV426(nearestScratchBV426,player.x,player.y,260,phantom?14:6,e=>!targets.includes(e)&&withinBasicRangeV552(e));for(const e of extra){hurtEnemy(e,dmg*(phantom?1.04:.58),'wing');particles.push({kind:'weaponHit',x:e.x,y:e.y,vx:0,vy:0,r:2,max:22,life:.16,maxLife:.16,color:'wing',a:Math.atan2(e.y-player.y,e.x-player.x)});}particles.push({kind:'fusionRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:12,max:range*.92,life:.30,maxLife:.30});}
    else particles.push({kind:'fusionRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:12,max:range*.58,life:.25,maxLife:.25});
    player.wingCd=Math.max(.38,(1.02-lv*.105)*tempo*(phantom?.54:1)*(frenzy>0?.80:1));
  }

  // LEG: stomp → knockback → outer shockwave → double stomp → moving momentum crush.
  if(state.leg>0){if(state.leg>=5){if((player.moveBlend||0)>.32)player.legMomentum=Math.min(1,(player.legMomentum||0)+dt*.82);else player.legMomentum=Math.max(0,(player.legMomentum||0)-dt*.32);}else player.legMomentum=0;}
  if(state.leg>0&&player.legCd<=0){
    const lv=state.leg,titan=evoState.leg,gravityStep=weaponSync('leg'),moving=(player.moveBlend||0)>.35,momentum=lv>=5?(player.legMomentum||0):0,radius=50+lv*13+(lv>=3?4:0)+(titan?52:0)+(gravityStep?10:0)+momentum*18,dmg=(17+lv*8.5)*(titan?1.30:1)*coreBoost*(player.legPower||1)*(moving?1.10:1)*(1+momentum*(titan?.62:.44)),push=28+lv*9+(lv>=2?10:0)+(titan?18:0);let hits=0;
    enemyEachCandidateCircleV425(player.x,player.y,radius+40,e=>{if(!e.dead&&dist2(player,e)<(radius+e.r)*(radius+e.r)){let impact=dmg*((e.elite||e.finalBoss)&&lv>=4?1.24:1);hurtEnemy(e,impact,'leg');if(lv>=4)hurtEnemy(e,impact*(titan?.56:.40),'leg');let ax=e.x-player.x,ay=e.y-player.y,al=Math.hypot(ax,ay)||1;e.kx=(e.kx||0)+ax/al*(e.finalBoss?push*.18:e.elite?push*.42:push);e.ky=(e.ky||0)+ay/al*(e.finalBoss?push*.18:e.elite?push*.42:push);e.stagger=(e.stagger||0)+(titan?22:11)+lv*2+(gravityStep?6:0);hits++;}});
    if(lv>=4){let heavy=collectNearestV426(nearestScratchBV426,player.x,player.y,330,1,e=>e.elite||e.finalBoss)[0];if(heavy){hurtEnemy(heavy,dmg*(titan?1.18:.58),'leg');if(titan)hurtEnemy(heavy,dmg*.58,'leg');heavy.stagger=(heavy.stagger||0)+18+lv*2;particles.push({kind:'weaponHit',x:heavy.x,y:heavy.y,vx:0,vy:0,r:3,max:32,life:.20,maxLife:.20,color:'leg',a:Math.atan2(heavy.y-player.y,heavy.x-player.x)});}}
    if(lv>=3){const outer=radius+46+(titan?16:0);enemyEachCandidateCircleV425(player.x,player.y,outer+40,e=>{if(e.dead)return;let d=Math.sqrt(dist2(player,e));if(d>radius+e.r&&d<outer+e.r){hurtEnemy(e,dmg*(titan?.62:.43),'leg');e.stagger=(e.stagger||0)+7+lv;}});particles.push({kind:'staggerRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:radius*.72,max:outer,life:.30,maxLife:.30});}
    if(hits===0){let quake=nearest(235+(lv>=3?35:0));if(quake){hurtEnemy(quake,dmg*(lv>=3?.70:.52),'leg');quake.stagger=(quake.stagger||0)+8+lv*2;let qn=0;if(lv>=3)enemyEachCandidateCircleV425(quake.x,quake.y,82,x=>{if(x===quake||x.dead)return;if(dist2(quake,x)<(44+x.r)*(44+x.r)&&qn<(lv>=5?3:2)){hurtEnemy(x,dmg*.30,'leg');qn++;if(qn>=(lv>=5?3:2))return false;}});particles.push({kind:'weaponHit',x:quake.x,y:quake.y,vx:0,vy:0,r:2,max:28,life:.18,maxLife:.18,color:'leg',a:Math.atan2(quake.y-player.y,quake.x-player.x)});hits=1;}}
    if(titan){let fiss=collectNearestV426(nearestScratchCV426,player.x,player.y,360,3,null,true);for(const e of fiss){hurtEnemy(e,dmg*(e.finalBoss||e.elite?1.02:.74),'leg');e.stagger=(e.stagger||0)+24;particles.push({kind:'weaponHit',x:e.x,y:e.y,vx:0,vy:0,r:3,max:34,life:.22,maxLife:.22,color:'leg',a:Math.atan2(e.y-player.y,e.x-player.x)});}}
    particles.push({kind:'deathRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:8,max:radius,life:.24,maxLife:.24});if(titan){particles.push({kind:'staggerRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:12,max:radius+52,life:.48,maxLife:.48});particles.push({kind:'deathRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:18,max:radius+82,life:.38,maxLife:.38});}if(hits>=3)player.inv=Math.max(player.inv,titan?.10:.045);if(lv>=5)player.legMomentum=.16;
    player.legCd=Math.max(.58,(1.78-lv*.17)*tempo*(titan?.56:1)*(frenzy>0?.82:1));cameraShake=Math.max(cameraShake,hits?4+(momentum>0.7?2:0):2);
  }

  if(false&&state.core>=1){
    player.corePulse=(player.corePulse??.45)-dt;
    if(player.corePulse<=0){
      const radius=42+state.core*10+(evoForm.core==='reactor'?12:0)+(fusion.voidCarapace?14:0),damage=(4.2+state.core*3.4)*coreBoost*(evoForm.core==='reactor'?1.12:1);
      let hits=0;for(const e of enemies){if(!e.dead&&dist2(player,e)<(radius+e.r)**2){hurtEnemy(e,damage,'core');let ax=e.x-player.x,ay=e.y-player.y,al=Math.hypot(ax,ay)||1,push=e.finalBoss?7:e.elite?13:28;e.kx=(e.kx||0)+ax/al*push;e.ky=(e.ky||0)+ay/al*push;e.stagger=(e.stagger||0)+(e.finalBoss?0:state.core*2.1);hits++;}}
      if(hits){particles.push({kind:'fusionRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:8,max:radius,life:.28,maxLife:.28});impactPulse=Math.max(impactPulse,.34);}else if(state.core>=1){let cb=nearest(260);if(cb){hurtEnemy(cb,damage*(state.core>=2?.88:.45),'core');cb.stagger=(cb.stagger||0)+8+state.core*2;let arc=0;for(const x of enemies){if(x===cb||x.dead||x.finalBoss||x.elite)continue;if((x.x-cb.x)**2+(x.y-cb.y)**2<(34+x.r)**2&&arc<1){hurtEnemy(x,damage*.34,'core');arc++;}}particles.push({kind:'weaponHit',x:cb.x,y:cb.y,vx:0,vy:0,r:2,max:20,life:.16,maxLife:.16,color:'core',a:Math.atan2(cb.y-player.y,cb.x-player.x)});}}
      player.corePulse=Math.max(1.08,(2.65-state.core*.16)*tempo*(synergy.overdrive?.88:1));
    }
  }
}
function protectedByWarden(e){
  if(e.type==='warden')return null;
  let found=null;
  enemyEachCandidateCircleV425(e.x,e.y,76,w=>{if(w.dead||w.type!=='warden')return;let rr=w.aura||62;if((w.x-e.x)**2+(w.y-e.y)**2<rr*rr){found=w;return false;}});
  return found;
}
function hurtEnemy(e,dmg,kind){if(e.dead||e.arrivalInvuln)return;if(kind==='tail'&&state.tail>=5&&e.hp/Math.max(1,e.maxHp)<=(evoState.tail?.48:.34))dmg*=evoState.tail?1.76:1.26;if((kind==='arm'||kind==='artillery')&&state.arm>=3&&(e.elite||e.finalBoss))dmg*=1.05;const pairMul=weaponPairDamageMul(e,kind);dmg*=pairMul*triadDamageMul(kind)*organDamageMul(e,kind)*memoryDamageMul(kind)*evoDamageMul(e,kind);if((e.barrier||0)>0){let absorbed=Math.min(e.barrier,dmg);e.barrier-=absorbed;dmg-=absorbed;particles.push({kind:'shield',x:e.x,y:e.y,vx:0,vy:0,r:e.r+7,life:.13});if(e.barrier<=0){particles.push({kind:'shieldBreak',x:e.x,y:e.y,r:6,max:e.r+24,life:.30,maxLife:.30});hitStop=Math.max(hitStop,.009);}if(dmg<=.01)return;}if((e.exposed||0)>0)dmg*=1.35;else if(e.finalBoss)dmg*=.70;if(e.prism>0&&(kind==='arm'||kind==='artillery'))dmg*=.58;if(e.purify>0&&kind==='acid')dmg*=.68;if(frenzy>0)dmg*=1.20;if(e.nemesis){if(e.adaptKind==='arm'&&(kind==='arm'||kind==='artillery'))dmg*=.66;else if(e.adaptKind==='mouth'&&kind==='acid')dmg*=.66;else if(e.adaptKind==='tail'&&kind==='tail')dmg*=.66;else if(e.adaptKind==='core')dmg*=.86;}if(kind==='acid'&&hasOrgan('omegaBladder'))e.slow=Math.max(e.slow||0,1.45);if(kind==='acid'&&evoForm.mouth==='virulent')e.slow=Math.max(e.slow||0,2.0);if(e.eliteArmor)dmg*=1-e.eliteArmor;if(e.contractArmor)dmg*=1-e.contractArmor;let guard=protectedByWarden(e);if(guard){dmg*=.62;particles.push({kind:'shield',x:e.x,y:e.y,vx:0,vy:0,r:e.r+5,life:.16});}if(!e.finalBoss&&e.staggerLock<=0){let resist=e.elite?1.75:(e.type==='brute'||e.type==='warden'?1.45:1),gain=dmg*(kind==='leg'?1.42:kind==='tail'?1.30:kind==='wing'?.82:kind==='artillery'?1.12:kind==='acid'?.62:.76)/resist;e.stagger=(e.stagger||0)+gain;let threshold=e.elite?92:(e.type==='brute'||e.type==='warden'?52:38);if(e.stagger>=threshold){e.stagger=0;e.staggerLock=e.elite?1.7:1.05;e.stunned=e.elite?.14:.26;discover('combat:stagger');particles.push({kind:'staggerRing',x:e.x,y:e.y,r:5,max:e.r+18,life:.28,maxLife:.28});hitStop=Math.max(hitStop,e.elite?.018:.012);}}let dealt=Math.min(Math.max(0,dmg),Math.max(0,e.hp));let bucket=kind==='acid'?'acid':kind==='tail'?'tail':kind==='wing'?'wing':kind==='leg'?'leg':kind==='core'?'core':'arm';runDamage[bucket]=(runDamage[bucket]||0)+dealt;e.hp-=dmg;bioHitV435(e);e.hit=.11;e.bodyKick=Math.max(e.bodyKick||0,.12);e.lastHitKind=kind;if(kind==='acid')e.corrosion=Math.max(e.corrosion||0,.48);else if(kind==='tail'||kind==='wing')e.cutMark=Math.max(e.cutMark||0,.34);else if(kind==='arm'||kind==='artillery')e.puncture=Math.max(e.puncture||0,.24);else if(kind==='core'||kind==='leg')e.coreShock=Math.max(e.coreShock||0,.30);e.deathA=Math.atan2(e.y-player.y,e.x-player.x);let heavy=(e.exposed||0)>0||(kind==='artillery'&&fusion.sunLance)||(kind==='tail'&&fusion.redReaper)||(kind==='acid'&&fusion.plagueVortex)||(kind==='wing'&&evoState.wing)||(kind==='leg'&&evoState.leg);let ka=Math.atan2(e.y-player.y,e.x-player.x),kb=kind==='tail'?72:kind==='leg'?82:kind==='wing'?26:kind==='arm'||kind==='artillery'?42:kind==='acid'?18:28;if(e.finalBoss)kb*=.12;else if(e.elite)kb*=.32;e.kx=(e.kx||0)+Math.cos(ka)*kb;e.ky=(e.ky||0)+Math.sin(ka)*kb;if((heavy||e.elite||e.finalBoss)&&hitStop<=0)hitStop=Math.max(hitStop,e.finalBoss?.032:e.elite?.024:.018);if(heavy||e.elite||e.finalBoss){cameraKickX-=Math.cos(ka)*(e.finalBoss?1.8:e.elite?2.6:3.4);cameraKickY-=Math.sin(ka)*(e.finalBoss?1.8:e.elite?2.6:3.4);}impactPulse=Math.max(impactPulse,heavy?.8:.38);const clarity=combatClarityLevel();if(feedbackCfg().damageNumbers!==false&&(heavy||e.elite||e.finalBoss||clarity===0||Math.random()<(clarity===1?.13:.055)))damageTexts.push({x:e.x+rand(-6,6),y:e.y-8,v:heavy?-30:-22,life:heavy?.72:.55,text:Math.round(dmg),kind,heavy});const hitFx=heavy||e.elite||e.finalBoss||clarity===0||Math.random()<(clarity===1?.20:.07);if(hitFx){particles.push({kind:'impact',x:e.x,y:e.y,vx:0,vy:0,r:heavy?7:4,max:heavy?23:14,life:heavy?.18:.12,maxLife:heavy?.18:.12,color:kind});particles.push({kind:'weaponHit',x:e.x,y:e.y,vx:0,vy:0,r:2,max:heavy?24:16,life:heavy?.20:.14,maxLife:heavy?.20:.14,color:kind,a:Math.atan2(e.y-player.y,e.x-player.x)});for(let i=0;i<(kind==='tail'?(clarity?2:5):(clarity?1:3));i++)particles.push({kind:kind==='acid'?'acid':kind==='tail'?'ember':'spark',x:e.x+rand(-4,4),y:e.y+rand(-4,4),vx:rand(-48,48),vy:rand(-48,48),r:kind==='tail'?rand(1.6,3.6):rand(1.3,2.8),life:rand(.16,.34)});}if(pairMul>1.01){const pk=weaponPairProcKey(e,kind);if(pk)spawnPairSyncVfx(e,pk,kind);}if(e.finalBoss&&evoCount()>=2){let next=e._evoHitFxAt||0;if(t>=next){e._evoHitFxAt=t+(evoCount()>=3?.085:.15);const c=activeEvoColorForKind(kind);particles.push({kind:'phaseRing',x:e.x,y:e.y,r:4,max:e.r+(evoCount()>=3?34:23),life:evoCount()>=3?.26:.20,maxLife:evoCount()>=3?.26:.20,color:c});if(evoCount()>=3&&perfMode<2){for(let i=0;i<3;i++)particles.push({kind:'evolutionShard',x:e.x+rand(-e.r*.4,e.r*.4),y:e.y+rand(-e.r*.4,e.r*.4),vx:rand(-70,70),vy:rand(-70,70),r:rand(1.4,3.5),life:rand(.18,.42),color:c});}impactPulse=Math.max(impactPulse,evoCount()>=3?.72:.48);}}if(e.hp<=0)killEnemy(e)}

 {id:'impactMarrow',slot:'predator',icon:'IMP',name:'IMPACT MARROW',desc:'ALPHA BRUTE impact marrow; boosts predatory damage at close range.',tag:'CLOSE DAMAGE +16%'},

 {id:'titanHeart',slot:'survival',icon:'THR',name:'TITAN HEART',desc:'TITAN heart core; improves sustained HP regeneration.',tag:'REGEN +0.22 / sec'},
 {id:'originNucleus',slot:'predator',icon:'ORG',name:'ORIGIN NUCLEUS',desc:'ORIGIN COLOSSUS nucleus; amplifies all attacking organs.',tag:'ALL DAMAGE +12%'},

const ORGAN_RARITY={
  common:{name:'COMMON',mult:1},
  rare:{name:'RARE',mult:1.28},
  epic:{name:'EPIC',mult:1.62}
};
function rollOrganRarity(){
  const clears=meta.clears||0,stage=meta.stage||1,r=Math.random();
  const luck=Math.min(.22,(meta.organLuck||0)+(riskTier().organLuck||0)),epicChance=clears>=5?Math.min(.30,.055+stage*.006+luck*.45):0;
  const rareChance=clears>=2?Math.min(.52,.20+stage*.012+luck):0;
  if(r<epicChance)return 'epic';
  if(r<epicChance+rareChance)return 'rare';
  return 'common';
}
function rollOrgan(base){const rarity=rollOrganRarity();return {...base,rarity,power:ORGAN_RARITY[rarity].mult}}
function signatureOrganFor(source){
  const s=String(source||'');
  if(s.includes('ABYSS MATRIARCH'))return 'abyssEye';
  if(s.includes('ORIGIN COLOSSUS'))return 'originNucleus';
  if(s.includes('ALPHA BRUTE'))return 'impactMarrow';
  if(s.includes('OMEGA SPITTER'))return 'omegaBladder';
  if(s.includes('TITAN'))return 'titanHeart';
  return null;
}
function organObj(id){return Object.values(organSlots).find(o=>o?.id===id)||null}
function organPower(id){return organObj(id)?.power||0}
function activeOrganSet(){
  if(hasOrgan('predatorFang')&&hasOrgan('reactiveShell'))return {id:'apexHunter',name:'APEX HUNTER'};
  if(hasOrgan('siegeLobe')&&hasOrgan('phaseMuscle'))return {id:'overclock',name:'OVERCLOCK TISSUE'};
  if(hasOrgan('venomSack')&&hasOrgan('regenMesh'))return {id:'plagueTissue',name:'PLAGUE TISSUE'};
  if(hasOrgan('razorTendon')&&hasOrgan('absorbCilia'))return {id:'harvester',name:'HARVESTER'};
  return null;
}
function hasOrgan(id){return organSlots.predator?.id===id||organSlots.survival?.id===id}
function effectivePickup(){return player.pickup+30*organPower('absorbCilia')+(activeOrganSet()?.id==='harvester'?20:0)}
function organDamageMul(e,kind){
  let m=1,set=activeOrganSet();
  if(hasOrgan('predatorFang')&&(e.elite||e.finalBoss))m*=1+.18*organPower('predatorFang');
  if(hasOrgan('siegeLobe')&&(kind==='arm'||kind==='artillery'))m*=1+.15*organPower('siegeLobe');
  if(hasOrgan('venomSack')&&kind==='acid')m*=1+.18*organPower('venomSack');
  if(hasOrgan('razorTendon')&&kind==='tail')m*=1+.18*organPower('razorTendon');
  if(hasOrgan('impactMarrow')&&dist2(player,e)<105*105)m*=1+.16*organPower('impactMarrow');
  if(hasOrgan('omegaBladder')&&kind==='acid')m*=1+.16*organPower('omegaBladder');
  if(hasOrgan('originNucleus'))m*=1+.12*organPower('originNucleus');
  if(set?.id==='apexHunter'&&(e.elite||e.finalBoss))m*=1.10;
  if(set?.id==='overclock'&&(kind==='arm'||kind==='artillery'))m*=1.08;
  if(set?.id==='plagueTissue'&&kind==='acid')m*=1.10;
  if(set?.id==='harvester'&&kind==='tail')m*=1.10;
  return m;
}
function updateOrganHud(force=false){
  const p=organSlots.predator,s=organSlots.survival,set=activeOrganSet();
  $('organPredator').textContent=p?('['+(p.rarity==='epic'?'E':p.rarity==='rare'?'R':'C')+'] '+p.name):'EMPTY';
  $('organSurvival').textContent=s?('['+(s.rarity==='epic'?'E':s.rarity==='rare'?'R':'C')+'] '+s.name):'EMPTY';
  $('organSet').textContent=set?set.name:'—';$('organSetSlot').style.display=set?'block':'none';
  if(force)organHudTimer=4.2;
  $('organHud').style.display=(p||s)&&(organHudTimer>0||!running)?'flex':'none';
}
function equipOrgan(o){
  if(!o)return;
  const old=organSlots[o.slot];
  organSlots[o.slot]=o;organEquipCount++;
  discover('organ:'+o.id);updateOrganHud(true);updateHUD();
  flash((old?'REPLACED':'IMPLANTED')+' · '+o.name,1000);cameraShake=5;beep(o.slot==='predator'?470:650,.1,'triangle',.035);
}
function armChoiceInput(ms=220){choiceInputReadyAt=performance.now()+ms}
function choiceInputReady(){return performance.now()>=choiceInputReadyAt}
function showOrganChoice(source){
  if(ended)return;
  rewardChoosing=true;armChoiceInput(240);
  $('rewardTitle').textContent='BIO-ORGAN IMPLANT';
  $('rewardSource').textContent=(source||'ELITE')+' · EQUIP OR REPLACE';
  const currentIds=new Set(Object.values(organSlots).filter(Boolean).map(x=>x.id));
  const signatureId=signatureOrganFor(source),signature=BIO_ORGANS.find(x=>x.id===signatureId);
  const signatureIds=new Set(['impactMarrow','omegaBladder','titanHeart','originNucleus','abyssEye']);
  const basePool=BIO_ORGANS.filter(x=>!x.zoneExclusive&&!currentIds.has(x.id)&&!signatureIds.has(x.id));
  let picks=[];
  if(signature){
    let sig=rollOrgan(signature);
    if(String(source).includes('ABYSS MATRIARCH'))sig={...sig,rarity:'epic',power:ORGAN_RARITY.epic.mult};
    picks.push(sig);
  }
  let pred=basePool.filter(x=>x.slot==='predator').sort(()=>Math.random()-.5);
  let surv=basePool.filter(x=>x.slot==='survival').sort(()=>Math.random()-.5);
  if(picks.length<3&&pred.length)picks.push(rollOrgan(pred[0]));
  if(picks.length<3&&surv.length&&!picks.some(p=>p.id===surv[0].id))picks.push(rollOrgan(surv[0]));
  for(const x of basePool.sort(()=>Math.random()-.5)){
    if(picks.length>=3)break;
    if(!picks.some(p=>p.id===x.id))picks.push(rollOrgan(x));
  }
  if(picks.length<3){
    for(const x of BIO_ORGANS){if(picks.length>=3)break;if(x.zoneExclusive)continue;if(signatureIds.has(x.id)&&x.id!==signatureId)continue;if(!picks.some(p=>p.id===x.id))picks.push(rollOrgan(x));}
  }
  const wrap=$('rewardCards');wrap.innerHTML='';
  for(const o of picks.slice(0,3)){
    const old=organSlots[o.slot],b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='rewardCard organCard '+o.slot;
    let ri=ORGAN_RARITY[o.rarity]||ORGAN_RARITY.common;b.classList.add(o.rarity||'common');
    b.innerHTML=`<div class="rewardIcon">${o.icon}</div><div class="organRarity ${o.rarity}">${ri.name} ×${o.power.toFixed(2)}</div><div class="rewardTitle">${o.name}</div><div class="rewardTag">${old?'REPLACE · '+old.name:o.tag}</div>`;
    b.onclick=()=>{if(!choiceInputReady())return;equipOrgan(o);rewardChoosing=false;$('rewardOverlay').style.display='none';$('rewardTitle').textContent='CORE ABSORPTION';addXP(0);combatGrace(.55);const finalSource=/BIO TITAN|FROST TITAN|ABERRANT TITAN|PYRE TITAN|ORIGIN COLOSSUS|ABYSS MATRIARCH/.test(String(source));if(finalSource)setTimeout(()=>spawnBreachGate(),260);};
    wrap.appendChild(b);
  }
  $('rewardOverlay').style.display='flex';
}

function enemyDrawAsset(type){return ART[enemyAppearanceAssetV516(enemyAppearanceKeyV516(type))]||ART['enemy/crawler'];}

function enemyDrawSize(e){
  return e.finalBoss?(e.superApex?124:(e.apex?112:100)):e.elite?68:e.type==='brute'?51:e.type==='warden'?38:e.type==='froststalker'?35:e.type==='symbiote'?34:e.type==='embermaw'?43:e.type==='spitter'?40:(e.type==='runner'?27:29);
}
function spawnDeathEcho(e){
  if(!e)return;
  if(bioDeathEchoV435(e))return;
  const life=e.finalBoss?.78:e.elite?.62:.42;
  let da=Number.isFinite(e.deathA)?e.deathA:Math.atan2(e.vy||0,e.vx||1);let force=e.finalBoss?18:e.elite?28:38;deathEchoes.push({type:e.type,visualSetV516:e.visualSetV516,x:e.x,y:e.y,size:enemyDrawSize(e),rot:da+Math.PI/2,life,max:life,elite:e.elite,finalBoss:e.finalBoss,affix:e.eliteAffix||'',bossName:e.bossName||'',vx:Math.cos(da)*force,vy:Math.sin(da)*force-10,spin:rand(-.8,.8)});
  if(deathEchoes.length>18)deathEchoes.splice(0,deathEchoes.length-18);
  const color=e.type==='froststalker'?'frost':e.type==='spitter'||e.type==='symbiote'?'acid':e.type==='embermaw'?'ember':'flesh';
  let n=e.finalBoss?34:e.elite?22:10;
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++)particles.push({kind:'dissolve',x:e.x+rand(-e.r,e.r),y:e.y+rand(-e.r,e.r),vx:rand(-70,70),vy:rand(-100,35),r:rand(1,3.5),life:rand(.28,.72),color});
}
function drawDeathEchoes(){
  for(const d of deathEchoes){
    if(d.bioMotionV435){drawBioMotionV435(d,d.size,clamp(d.life/.16,0,1),true,true);drawBioMotionV435(d,d.size,clamp(d.life/.16,0,1),false,true);continue;}
    const q=clamp(d.life/d.max,0,1),img=renderedEnemy(d),rise=(1-q)*9,scale=.78+.22*q;
    ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=q*.48;ctx.translate(d.x,d.y-rise);ctx.rotate((1-q)*.8+(d.spin||0)*(1-q));ctx.filter='grayscale('+(1-q)*.55+') brightness('+(1.12-q*.12)+')';
    drawSpriteImage(img,0,0,d.size*scale,0,1,0);
    ctx.filter='none';ctx.globalCompositeOperation='lighter';ctx.globalAlpha=q*.16;ctx.strokeStyle=d.finalBoss?'#ff785e':d.elite?'#ffd071':'#9fb2bd';ctx.shadowBlur=13;ctx.shadowColor=ctx.strokeStyle;ctx.lineWidth=1.5;
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,d.size*(.18+.18*(1-q)),0,TAU);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
  }
}

function triggerBossDeathCinematic(e){
  if(!e)return;
  bossDeathFx={x:e.x,y:e.y,life:1.05,max:1.05,color:e.superApex?'#ef7dff':e.apex?'#ff9b63':'#ff7359',name:e.bossName||'BIO TITAN'};
  cameraShake=Math.max(cameraShake,e.superApex?20:e.apex?17:14);cameraKickX=0;cameraKickY=-3;hitStop=Math.max(hitStop,.11);
  const flashEl=$('bossDeathFlash');flashEl.classList.remove('on','coreBreach');void flashEl.offsetWidth;flashEl.classList.add('on','coreBreach');cinematicFreeze=Math.max(cinematicFreeze,.18);
  for(let i=0;i<(e.superApex?52:e.apex?40:30);i++)particles.push({kind:'bossFragment',x:e.x,y:e.y,vx:rand(-165,165),vy:rand(-165,90),r:rand(1.6,5.2),life:rand(.35,.95),color:bossDeathFx.color});
  particles.push({kind:'bossCollapse',x:e.x,y:e.y,r:8,max:e.superApex?135:e.apex?112:94,life:.85,maxLife:.85,color:bossDeathFx.color});
}
function drawBossDeathCinematic(){
  if(!bossDeathFx)return;
  let q=clamp(bossDeathFx.life/bossDeathFx.max,0,1),p=1-q;
  ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.24*q;ctx.fillStyle=bossDeathFx.color;ctx.shadowBlur=28;ctx.shadowColor=bossDeathFx.color;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(bossDeathFx.x,bossDeathFx.y,16+p*82,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
  ctx.globalAlpha=.72*q;ctx.strokeStyle='#fff0dc';ctx.lineWidth=2.5;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(bossDeathFx.x,bossDeathFx.y,10+p*120,0,TAU);ctx.stroke();
  ctx.globalAlpha=.28*q;ctx.strokeStyle=bossDeathFx.color;ctx.lineWidth=6;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(bossDeathFx.x,bossDeathFx.y,24+p*150,0,TAU);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
}
function triggerHordeRupture(e){
  if(!e||e.finalBoss||e.elite||killChain<34)return;
  const step=frenzy>0?11:15;if(killChain%step!==0)return;
  const radius=Math.min(92,42+Math.floor(killChain/34)*6+Math.max(0,player.level-9)*.6),damage=(8+powerScore()*1.35)*(player.dmgMul||1);
  let hits=0;enemyEachCandidateCircleV425(e.x,e.y,radius+40,x=>{if(x===e||x.dead||x.finalBoss||x.elite)return;if((x.x-e.x)**2+(x.y-e.y)**2<(radius+x.r)**2){hurtEnemy(x,damage,'core');hits++;}});
  if(hits){particles.push({kind:'breakRing',x:e.x,y:e.y,r:8,max:radius,life:.30,maxLife:.30});cameraShake=Math.max(cameraShake,4);}
}
function killEnemy(e){
  if(e.dead)return;if(e.venomDebugV537){e.dead=true;VENOM_V537.onDeath(e);$('bossbar').style.display='none';flash('VENOM TEST FINISHED',800);return;}if(e.venomV537)VENOM_V537.onDeath(e);if(e.finalBoss){const p=safeBossResolutionPoint(e);e.x=p.x;e.y=p.y;e.vx=e.vy=e.kx=e.ky=0;e.burst=0;}const clarity=combatClarityLevel();if(killFxFrame!==simFrame){killFxFrame=simFrame;killFxCount=0;}const killFxAllowed=e.finalBoss||e.elite||killFxCount<(clarity>=2?5:clarity===1?9:16);if(!e.finalBoss&&!e.elite&&killFxAllowed)killFxCount++;if(e.finalBoss||e.elite||(killFxAllowed&&(clarity===0||kills%4===0)))spawnDeathEcho(e);e.dead=true;kills++;if(focusEnemy===e)focusEnemy=null;if(tacticalWave&&e.waveId===tacticalWave.id&&!tacticalWave.cleared){tacticalWave.killed++;if(tacticalWave.killed>=tacticalWave.total)finishTacticalWave(true);else updateWaveBadge();}addFloorMark(e);{const ec=evoCount();if(ec>=2&&killFxAllowed){const c=activeEvoColorForKind(e.lastHitKind);if(ec>=3||kills%3===0)particles.push({kind:'phaseRing',x:e.x,y:e.y,r:3,max:e.r+(ec>=3?34:22),life:ec>=3?.34:.24,maxLife:ec>=3?.34:.24,color:c});if(ec>=3&&perfMode<2){for(let i=0;i<5;i++)particles.push({kind:'evolutionShard',x:e.x,y:e.y,vx:rand(-105,105),vy:rand(-105,105),r:rand(1.4,4.2),life:rand(.22,.58),color:c});}}}if(e.finalBoss){hitStop=Math.max(hitStop,.055);if(runTelemetry&&runTelemetry.bossKillTime==null)runTelemetry.bossKillTime=Number(t.toFixed(1));}else if(e.elite)hitStop=Math.max(hitStop,.035);else if(e.zoneElite)hitStop=Math.max(hitStop,.026);
  if(e.nemesis){
    if(majorArrivalCue?.owner===e)majorArrivalCue=null;
    const power=Math.max(1,e.nemesisPower||meta.nemesis?.power||1),adaptKind=e.adaptKind||meta.nemesis?.adaptKind||'core';
    nemesisKilledThisRun=true;meta.nemesis=null;meta.nemesisDefeats=(meta.nemesisDefeats||0)+1;
    awardMemoryTrait(adaptKind);contractBankBonus+=10+power*4;dnaTotal+=18+power*6;saveMeta();renderNemesisStatus();
    dropRelic(e.x-10,e.y,true);dropRelic(e.x+12,e.y+4,true);

function xpNeedForLevel(lv){lv=Math.max(1,lv|0);if(lv===1)return 12;if(lv===2)return 30;return Math.floor(16+lv*7.2+Math.pow(lv,1.55)*3.5+Math.max(0,lv-8)*14)}
function addXP(v){
 if(ended)return;
 if(stageLevelLimitedV521()){discardCappedXPV521();return;}
 const amount=Number(v)||0;if(!Number.isFinite(amount)||amount<0)return;
 player.xp+=amount;
 if(choosing||rewardChoosing)return;
 if(player.xp>=player.xpNeed){
  player.xp-=player.xpNeed;player.level++;player.xpNeed=xpNeedForLevel(player.level);
  if(stageLevelLimitedV521()){discardCappedXPV521();updateHUD(true);}
  // The upgrade earned by reaching level 10 is still offered exactly once.
  showLevelUp();
 }
}

function checkOriginAscension(){
  if(originAscension)return;
  const id=player.origin||meta.origin||'prime',total=state.arm+state.mouth+state.tail+state.core+state.speed;
  let asc='';
  if(id==='prime'&&state.core>=3&&total>=10){
    asc='PERFECT ADAPTATION';player.dmgMul*=1.12;player.armor=Math.min(.48,(player.armor||0)+.06);player.speed+=8;player.regen+=.10;discover('ascension:perfect');
  }else if(id==='razor'&&state.tail>=3&&state.speed>=3){
    asc='BLADE SERAPH';player.tailPower=(player.tailPower||1)*1.35;player.tailHaste=(player.tailHaste||0)+.24;player.speed+=14;player.dashBonus=(player.dashBonus||0)+.35;discover('ascension:blade');
  }else if(id==='acid'&&state.mouth>=3&&state.core>=3){
    asc='PLAGUE WYRM';player.acidPower=(player.acidPower||1)*1.35;player.acidHaste=(player.acidHaste||0)+.22;player.pickup+=16;discover('ascension:plague');
  }else if(id==='forge'&&state.arm>=3&&state.core>=3){
    asc='SIEGE ORGANISM';player.dmgMul*=1.18;player.bonusShots=(player.bonusShots||0)+1;player.baseCd=Math.max(.42,player.baseCd-.08);discover('ascension:siege');
  }else if(id==='abyss'&&state.core>=4&&total>=14){
    asc='BLACK SUN';player.dmgMul*=1.20;player.armor=Math.min(.50,(player.armor||0)+.08);player.regen+=.08;player.speed+=8;discover('ascension:blackSun');
  }
  if(asc){
    originAscension=asc;updateSynergyBadge();announceEncounter('ORIGIN ASCENSION',asc);flash('ASCENDED · '+asc,1500);cameraShake=10;
    for(let i=0;i<36;i++)particles.push({kind:'relic',x:player.x,y:player.y,vx:rand(-125,125),vy:rand(-125,125),r:rand(1.5,4.5),life:rand(.35,.85)});
    beep(720,.12,'triangle',.04);setTimeout(()=>beep(1040,.18,'triangle',.035),100);
  }
}

function evoDamageMul(e,kind){
  let m=1;
  if(evoState.arm&&(kind==='arm'||kind==='artillery'))m*=1.42;
  if(evoState.mouth&&kind==='acid')m*=1.44;
  if(evoState.tail&&kind==='tail'){m*=1.40;if(e.maxHp&&e.hp/e.maxHp<=.58)m*=1.72}
  if(evoState.wing&&kind==='wing')m*=1.48;
  if(evoState.leg&&kind==='leg')m*=1.50;
  return m*evoResonanceDamageMul();
}

function applyGene(id){
  if(!(id in genes)||genes[id]>=5)return;
  genes[id]++;const linked=GENE_INFO[id]?.evo;if(linked&&evoPity[linked]!=null)evoPity[linked]=0;
  if(id==='reactor')player.dmgMul*=1.06;
  else if(id==='virulent'){player.acidPower=(player.acidPower||1)*1.07;player.acidHaste=(player.acidHaste||0)+.012;}
  else if(id==='predator'){player.tailPower=(player.tailPower||1)*1.07;predation=Math.min(100,predation+4);}
  else if(id==='bastion'){player.maxHp+=8;player.hp=Math.min(player.maxHp,player.hp+8);player.armor=Math.min(.48,(player.armor||0)+.015);}
  else if(id==='phase'){player.speed+=4;player.dashBonus=(player.dashBonus||0)+.08;}
  else if(id==='core'){state.core=1+genes.core;player.dmgMul*=1.04;player.maxHp+=4;player.hp=Math.min(player.maxHp,player.hp+4);}
  else if(id==='haste'){state.speed=genes.haste;player.speed+=4;player.dashBonus=(player.dashBonus||0)+.055;}
  else if(id==='hydra'){player.pickup=Math.min(220,player.pickup+6);}
}

function applyEvolution(kind){

  if(r.fusion)fusion[r.fusion]=true;

  if(kind==='wing'){player.wingPower=(player.wingPower||1)*1.34;player.speed+=10;player.dashBonus=(player.dashBonus||0)+.28;}
  if(kind==='leg'){player.legPower=(player.legPower||1)*1.36;player.maxHp+=28;player.hp=Math.min(player.maxHp,player.hp+28);player.armor=Math.min(.58,(player.armor||0)+.07);}
  if(kind==='mouth'){player.acidPower=(player.acidPower||1)*1.30;player.regen+=.08;}
  if(kind==='tail'){player.tailPower=(player.tailPower||1)*1.28;player.regen+=.055;}

  if(evoN===2){player.maxHp+=12;player.hp=Math.min(player.maxHp,player.hp+player.maxHp*.18);player.armor=Math.min(.60,(player.armor||0)+.025);evolutionSurge=Math.max(evolutionSurge,16);announceEncounter('HYPERMUTATION','2 EVO · DAMAGE +18% · ATTACK SPEED UP');flash('HYPERMUTATION',1200);for(let i=0;i<72;i++)particles.push({kind:'fusion',x:player.x,y:player.y,vx:rand(-230,230),vy:rand(-230,230),r:rand(1.8,6.2),life:rand(.35,1.05)});particles.push({kind:'phaseRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:12,max:330,life:1.0,maxLife:1.0,color:'#fff0a0'});}

  updateFusionBadge();updateHUD();showEvolutionCinematic(kind,r.name,'5★ + '+GENE_INFO[r.gene].short+' · EVO','fusion');
  cameraShake=18;
  for(let i=0;i<100;i++)particles.push({kind:'fusion',x:player.x,y:player.y,vx:rand(-210,210),vy:rand(-210,210),r:rand(1.6,5.8),life:rand(.32,1.0)});particles.push({kind:'phaseRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:8,max:260,life:.82,maxLife:.82,color:EVOLUTION_COLORS[kind]||'#ffd06e'});particles.push({kind:'fusionRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:16,max:220,life:.74,maxLife:.74});
  beep(620,.12,'triangle',.045);setTimeout(()=>beep(960,.17,'triangle',.04),100);return true;
}
function showEvoChest(source,onDone){
  const ready=eligibleEvos();if(!ready.length){if(typeof onDone==='function')onDone();return false;}
  const kind=ready[Math.floor(Math.random()*ready.length)],r=EVO_RULES[kind];rewardChoosing=true;armChoiceInput(220);
  $('rewardTitle').textContent='EVO';$('rewardSource').textContent=(source||'BIO CHEST')+' · 5★ + PASSIVE';
  const wrap=$('rewardCards');wrap.innerHTML='';const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='rewardCard evoReadyCard';
  b.innerHTML=`<div class="rewardIcon">EVO</div><div class="rewardRole offense">${kind.toUpperCase()} 5★</div><div class="rewardTitle">${r.name}</div><div class="rewardTag">${GENE_INFO[r.gene].short} ★ · TAP TO EVOLVE</div>`;
  b.onclick=()=>{if(!choiceInputReady())return;applyEvolution(kind);rewardChoosing=false;$('rewardOverlay').style.display='none';combatGrace(.6);if(typeof onDone==='function')setTimeout(onDone,160);};
  wrap.appendChild(b);$('rewardOverlay').style.display='flex';return true;
}
function queueEvoChests(source,count,onDone){count=Math.max(0,count|0);const step=()=>{if(count<=0||!eligibleEvos().length){if(typeof onDone==='function')onDone();return;}count--;if(!showEvoChest(source,()=>setTimeout(step,180))&&typeof onDone==='function')onDone();};step();}
