const MAX_ACTIVE_SLOTS=3,MAX_PASSIVE_SLOTS=5;
function activeSlotCount(){return ['arm','mouth','tail','wing','leg'].filter(k=>(state[k]||0)>0).length}
function passiveSlotCount(){return Object.keys(genes).filter(k=>(genes[k]||0)>0).length}
function evoBuildStatusForWeapon(k){
  const r=EVO_RULES[k];if(!r)return null;const stars=state[k]||0,g=genes[r.gene]||0;
  return {kind:k,stars,gene:r.gene,geneStars:g,name:r.name,ready:stars>=5&&g>=1,near:stars>=4||g>=1};
}
function evoMaterialWeaponForGene(gene){
  return Object.keys(EVO_RULES).find(k=>EVO_RULES[k].gene===gene&&(state[k]||0)>0&&!evoState[k])||null;
}
function closestEvoPath(){
  const arr=['arm','mouth','tail','wing','leg'].filter(k=>(state[k]||0)>0&&!evoState[k]).map(k=>evoBuildStatusForWeapon(k));
  arr.sort((a,b)=>(b.stars*3+(b.geneStars?2:0))-(a.stars*3+(a.geneStars?2:0)));return arr[0]||null;
}
function buildProgressCandidate(active,passive){
  const owned=['arm','mouth','tail','wing','leg'].filter(k=>(state[k]||0)>0&&!evoState[k]);
  let cand=[];
  for(const k of owned){const r=EVO_RULES[k],stars=state[k]||0,g=genes[r.gene]||0;if(stars<5){let u=active.find(x=>x.kind===k);if(u)cand.push({u,score:stars*3+(g?4:0)});}if(stars>=4&&!g){let p=passive.find(x=>x.gene===r.gene);if(p)cand.push({u:p,score:stars*3+5+(evoPity[k]||0)});}}
  cand.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);return cand[0]?.u||null;
}
function levelChoiceSignals(u){
  if(u.evo)return ['EVO'];
  if(u.passive){const wk=evoMaterialWeaponForGene(u.gene);if(wk){const s=state[wk]||0;return [s>=4?'EVO MATERIAL':'PASSIVE'];}return ['PASSIVE'];}
  const r=EVO_RULES[u.kind],v=state[u.kind]||0;if(r&&v===4&&(genes[r.gene]||0)>=1)return ['EVO READY'];
  if(r&&v>=3)return ['EVO PATH'];
  const sync=weaponSync(u.kind);if(sync&&v>=1)return ['SYNC'];
  return [v>0?'ACTIVE':'NEW ACTIVE'];
}
function signalClass(name){return name==='EVO'||name==='EVO READY'?'evo':name==='EVO MATERIAL'?'material':name==='EVO PATH'||name==='SYNC'?'progress':name==='PASSIVE'?'passive':name==='NEW ACTIVE'?'new':'build'}
function geneChoiceStats(id){
  const cur=genes[id]||0,next=Math.min(5,cur+1),rows=[];
  if(id==='reactor')rows.push(compareRow('ALL DMG',100,106,'%',0));
  else if(id==='virulent')rows.push(compareRow('ACID',100,107,'%',0),onlyRow('CD','-1.2%'));
  else if(id==='predator')rows.push(compareRow('TAIL',100,107,'%',0),onlyRow('PREDATION','+4'));
  else if(id==='bastion')rows.push(compareRow('MAX HP',player.maxHp,player.maxHp+8,'',0),onlyRow('ARMOR','+1.5%'));
  else if(id==='phase')rows.push(compareRow('MOVE',player.speed,player.speed+4,'',0),onlyRow('DASH REC','+8%'));
  else if(id==='core')rows.push(compareRow('ALL DMG',100+(cur*4),100+(next*4),'%',0),compareRow('MAX HP',player.maxHp,player.maxHp+4,'',0));
  else if(id==='haste')rows.push(compareRow('ATK CD',previewTempoFor(cur)*100,previewTempoFor(next)*100,'%',1),compareRow('MOVE',player.speed,player.speed+4,'',0),onlyRow('DASH REC','+'+Math.round(next*5.5)+'%'));
  else if(id==='hydra')rows.push(compareRow('PICKUP',player.pickup,Math.min(220,player.pickup+6),'',0),onlyRow('VOLLEY',next>=3&&cur<3?'+1':'BOOST'));
  const g=GENE_INFO[id];if(g?.evo)rows.push(onlyRow('EVO',g.evoName));
  return rows.slice(0,3);
}
function levelChoiceStats(u){
  if(u.evo){const r=EVO_RULES[u.evoKind];return [onlyRow('EVO',r.name),onlyRow('REQ','5★ + '+GENE_INFO[r.gene].short)];}
  if(u.passive)return geneChoiceStats(u.gene);
  const k=u.kind,cur=state[k]||0,next=Math.min(5,cur+1);let rows=[];
  if(k==='arm'){
    const tempo=previewTempoFor(genes.haste||0),cd0=Math.max(.22,(player.baseCd-cur*.055)*tempo),cd1=Math.max(.22,(player.baseCd-next*.055)*tempo);
    rows.push(compareRow('DMG',9.8+cur*3.4,9.8+next*3.4,'',1),compareRow('RANGE',202+cur*14,202+next*14,'',0),compareRow('CD',cd0,cd1,'s',2));
  }else if(k==='mouth'){
    const tempo=previewTempoFor(genes.haste||0),haste=player.acidHaste||0,cd0=Math.max(.32,(1.15-cur*.125-haste)*tempo),cd1=Math.max(.32,(1.15-next*.125-haste)*tempo);
    rows.push(compareRow('ACID',10.2+cur*5,10.2+next*5,'',1),compareRow('CD',cd0,cd1,'s',2),compareRow('PIERCE',1+(cur>=3?1:0)+(cur>=5?1:0),1+(next>=3?1:0)+(next>=5?1:0),'',0));
  }else if(k==='tail'){
    const tempo=previewTempoFor(genes.haste||0),cd0=Math.max(.44,(1.50-cur*.180-(player.tailHaste||0))*tempo),cd1=Math.max(.44,(1.50-next*.180-(player.tailHaste||0))*tempo);
    rows.push(compareRow('TAIL',15.5+cur*10,15.5+next*10,'',1),compareRow('AREA',60+cur*17,60+next*17,'',0),compareRow('CD',cd0,cd1,'s',2));
  }else if(k==='wing'){
    const cd0=Math.max(.38,1.02-cur*.105),cd1=Math.max(.38,1.02-next*.105);
    rows.push(compareRow('WING',8.5+cur*5.8,8.5+next*5.8,'',1),compareRow('BLADES',cur<=1?2:cur===2?3:cur===3?3:cur===4?4:5,next<=1?2:next===2?3:next===3?3:next===4?4:5,'',0),compareRow('CD',cd0,cd1,'s',2));
  }else if(k==='leg'){
    const cd0=Math.max(.58,1.78-cur*.17),cd1=Math.max(.58,1.78-next*.17);
    rows.push(compareRow('STOMP',17+cur*8.5,17+next*8.5,'',1),compareRow('AREA',50+cur*13+(cur>=3?4:0),50+next*13+(next>=3?4:0),'',0),compareRow('CD',cd0,cd1,'s',2));
  }
  const feature=weaponStarFeature(k,next),r=EVO_RULES[k],sync=weaponSync(k);
  if(r&&next===5){rows=[rows[0],onlyRow('5★',feature),onlyRow((genes[r.gene]||0)>=1?'EVO READY':'EVO NEED',(genes[r.gene]||0)>=1?r.name:GENE_INFO[r.gene].short+' ★')];}
  else if(sync&&next>=2){if(rows.length<3)rows.push(onlyRow('SYNC',weaponSyncName(k)));else rows[2]=onlyRow('SYNC',weaponSyncName(k));}
  else if(feature){if(rows.length<3)rows.push(onlyRow(next===1?'STYLE':'UNLOCK',feature));else rows[2]=onlyRow(next===1?'STYLE':'UNLOCK',feature);}
  return rows.slice(0,3);
}
function levelStatHtml(u){return levelChoiceStats(u).map(r=>r.only?`<div class="statLine"><span class="statName">${r.k}</span><span class="statOnly">${r.only}</span></div>`:`<div class="statLine"><span class="statName">${r.k}</span><span class="statCompare"><span class="statFrom">${r.from}</span><span class="statArrow">›</span><b class="statTo">${r.to}</b></span></div>`).join('')}
function choiceScore(u){
  if(u.evo)return 7.0;
  if(u.passive){let v=genes[u.gene]||0,score=.72+(v>0?.72:0);const g=GENE_INFO[u.gene];if(g?.evo&&(state[g.evo]||0)>0)score+=.42;if(g?.evo&&(state[g.evo]||0)>=3)score+=.72;if(g?.evo&&(state[g.evo]||0)>=4&&v===0)score+=1.15+(evoPity[g.evo]||0)*.38;if(g?.evo&&(state[g.evo]||0)>=5&&v===0)score+=2.2;return score;}
  let v=state[u.kind]||0,score=.90+(v>0?1.08+v*.08:0);const r=EVO_RULES[u.kind];if(r&&(genes[r.gene]||0)>=1)score+=.55;return score;
}
function weightedChoice(pool,scoreFn){
  if(!pool.length)return null;let weights=pool.map(x=>Math.max(.001,scoreFn(x))),sum=weights.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0),r=Math.random()*sum;
  for(let i=0;i<pool.length;i++){r-=weights[i];if(r<=0)return pool[i]}
  return pool[pool.length-1];
}
function chooseLevelPicks(isReroll=false){
  const aCount=activeSlotCount(),pCount=passiveSlotCount();
  const active=upgrades.filter(u=>u.active&&!evoState[u.kind]&&(state[u.kind]||0)<5&&((state[u.kind]||0)>0||aCount<MAX_ACTIVE_SLOTS));
  const passive=upgrades.filter(u=>u.passive&&(genes[u.gene]||0)<5&&((genes[u.gene]||0)>0||pCount<MAX_PASSIVE_SLOTS));
  const evoChoices=[];
  let pool=[...active,...passive],picks=[];
  while(pool.length&&picks.length<3){const u=weightedChoice(pool,choiceScore);picks.push(u);pool=pool.filter(x=>x!==u);}
  const ownedActive=active.filter(u=>(state[u.kind]||0)>0);if(ownedActive.length&&!picks.some(u=>u.active&&(state[u.kind]||0)>0)){const u=weightedChoice(ownedActive,choiceScore);if(u){if(picks.length>=3)picks[picks.length-1]=u;else picks.push(u)}}
  const evoNeeds=['arm','mouth','tail','wing','leg'].filter(k=>!evoState[k]&&(state[k]||0)>=4&&(genes[EVO_RULES[k]?.gene]||0)===0).sort((a,b)=>((state[b]||0)*10+(evoPity[b]||0))-((state[a]||0)*10+(evoPity[a]||0)));
  if(evoNeeds.length){const k=evoNeeds[0],r=EVO_RULES[k],g=passive.find(u=>u.gene===r.gene),hist=telemetryEvoBias(),multi=telemetryMultiEvoBias(),force=(state[k]||0)>=5||(evoPity[k]||0)>=(hist>0?1:hist<0?3:2),chance=.58+Math.min(.28,(evoPity[k]||0)*.10)+hist*.08+multi*.04;if(g&&(force||Math.random()<clamp(chance,.38,.92))&&!picks.includes(g)){if(picks.length>=3)picks[1]=g;else picks.push(g)}for(const need of evoNeeds){const gr=EVO_RULES[need].gene;if(!(genes[gr]||0)&&!picks.some(u=>u.passive&&u.gene===gr))evoPity[need]=Math.min(6,(evoPity[need]||0)+1);}}
  const progress=buildProgressCandidate(active,passive);if(progress&&!picks.includes(progress)&&!picks.some(u=>(u.passive&&progress.passive&&u.gene===progress.gene)||(!u.passive&&!progress.passive&&u.kind===progress.kind))){if(picks.length>=3)picks[0]=progress;else picks.push(progress);}
  if(passive.length&&player.level>=2&&!picks.some(u=>u.passive)){const p=weightedChoice(passive,choiceScore);if(p){if(picks.length>=3)picks[picks.length-1]=p;else picks.push(p)}}
  return [...new Set(picks)].slice(0,3);
}
function levelBuildContext(){
  const path=closestEvoPath(),ready=eligibleEvos(),ec=evoCount(),syncs=activePairSynergies().length,slot='WEAPON '+activeSlotCount()+'/'+MAX_ACTIVE_SLOTS+' · GENE '+passiveSlotCount()+'/'+MAX_PASSIVE_SLOTS;
  const evoPath=path?(path.kind.toUpperCase()+' '+path.stars+'/5 · '+GENE_INFO[path.gene].short+' '+path.geneStars+'/1'):'';
  return [slot,evoPath,(syncs?'SYNC×'+syncs:''),(ec?'EVO×'+ec:''),(ready.length?'EVO READY':'')].filter(Boolean).join('   ');
}

}
function fmtStat(v,d=1){if(typeof v==='string')return v;const n=Number(v);if(!Number.isFinite(n))return '—';return d===0?String(Math.round(n)):n.toFixed(d).replace(/\.0$/,'')}
function previewTempoFor(speedLv){let m=1-Math.max(0,speedLv)*.040-Math.max(0,genes.phase||0)*.012;if(synergy.overdrive)m*=.88;return clamp(m,.70,1)}
function compareRow(k,from,to,unit='',d=1){return {k,from:fmtStat(from,d)+unit,to:fmtStat(to,d)+unit}}
function onlyRow(k,v){return {k,only:v}}
function renderLevelChoices(isReroll=false){
  const picks=chooseLevelPicks(isReroll),cards=$('cards');cards.innerHTML='';if($('levelContext'))$('levelContext').textContent=levelBuildContext();
  levelLastOffer=picks.map(u=>u.kind);
  picks.forEach(u=>{

    if(u.passive&&evoMaterialWeaponForGene(u.gene))b.classList.add('evoMaterialCard');else if(!u.passive&&!u.evo&&evoBuildStatusForWeapon(u.kind)?.near)b.classList.add('buildProgressCard');
    b.classList.add('renderedCard');const artKind=u.evo?u.evoKind:(u.passive?'knowledge':u.kind);b.style.setProperty('--card-frame',`url(${ASSET_SRC['ui/card_'+({mouth:'mouth',tail:'tail',arm:'arm',wing:'tail',leg:'base'}[u.evo?u.evoKind:u.kind]||'base')]})`);
    const signals=levelChoiceSignals(u),signalHtml=signals.map(x=>`<span class="choiceSignal ${signalClass(x)}">${x}</span>`).join('');
    let current='';let levelHtml='';if(u.evo){current='5★ + '+GENE_INFO[EVO_RULES[u.evoKind].gene].short+' ★';levelHtml=`<div class="cardLevel"><strong>${current}</strong></div>`;}else if(u.passive){let v=genes[u.gene]||0;levelHtml=renderChoiceStars(v,Math.min(5,v+1));}else{let v=state[u.kind]||0;levelHtml=renderChoiceStars(v,Math.min(5,v+1));}

    b.onclick=()=>{if(!choosing||!choiceInputReady())return;u.apply();discover((u.evo?'evo:':u.passive?'gene:':'mutation:')+(u.evo?u.evoKind:(u.gene||u.kind)));$('levelOverlay').style.display='none';updateHUD();audioCue('evolve');hapticCue('evolve');
      if(!u.evo){showEvolutionCinematic(u.passive?'core':u.kind,u.title().toUpperCase(),u.passive?'PASSIVE '+starString(genes[u.gene]):'ACTIVE '+starString(state[u.kind]),'mutation');}
      choosing=false;combatGrace(.48);addXP(0);
    };cards.appendChild(b)
  });
  updateRerollControls();updateHUD();
}

function dash(){if(!running||paused||choosing||rewardChoosing||ended||dashReady>0)return;let dx=joy.dx,dy=joy.dy;if(Math.hypot(dx,dy)<.1){dx=(keys.ArrowRight||keys.d?1:0)-(keys.ArrowLeft||keys.a?1:0);dy=(keys.ArrowDown||keys.s?1:0)-(keys.ArrowUp||keys.w?1:0);let l=Math.hypot(dx,dy);if(l){dx/=l;dy/=l}else{dx=1;dy=0}}let dashDist=82+(evoForm.speed==='phase'?22:0);player.dashKick=.24;player.dashAng=Math.atan2(dy,dx);cameraKickX-=dx*2.6;cameraKickY-=dy*2.6;moveTerrainBodyV418(player,dx*dashDist,dy*dashDist,player.r);player.inv=Math.max(player.inv,(synergy.overdrive?.42:.3)+(evoForm.speed==='phase'?.16:0)+(fusion.voidCarapace?.22:0));dashReady=Math.max(1.15,(synergy.overdrive?2.8:4)-(player.dashBonus||0)-.55*organPower('phaseMuscle')-(activeOrganSet()?.id==='overclock'?.25:0)-(evoForm.speed==='phase'?.65:0));
if(fusion.voidCarapace){let rr=88,dmg=(32+state.core*6)*(player.dmgMul||1),vh=0;forEnemyRadiusV426(player.x,player.y,rr+40,e=>{if(!e.dead&&dist2(player,e)<(rr+e.r)**2){hurtEnemy(e,dmg,'core');vh++}});if(vh)player.hp=Math.min(player.maxHp,player.hp+Math.min(5,vh*.8));particles.push({kind:'fusionRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:8,max:rr,life:.34,maxLife:.34});}for(let i=0;i<15;i++)particles.push({kind:'dash',x:player.x-dx*rand(10,60),y:player.y-dy*rand(10,60),vx:rand(-20,20),vy:rand(-20,20),r:rand(2,5),life:rand(.15,.35)});particles.push({kind:'motionRing',x:player.x,y:player.y,r:7,max:34,life:.22,maxLife:.22,color:'dash'});cameraShake=4;audioCue('dash');hapticCue('dash');if(firstRunCoach()&&coachStep<=2){coachStep=3;coachHint(coarsePointer?'BIO · TAP ACTIVE':'BIO ACTIVE · PRESS E',4.0);updateCoachVisuals()}}

function update(dt){
  if(paused||choosing||rewardChoosing||ended)return;if(tickBossDeadlineV417(dt))return;t+=dt;if(tickStageScheduleV526(dt))return;updateWorldZone(dt);directorLock=Math.max(0,directorLock-dt);directorRelief=Math.max(0,directorRelief-dt);player.animTime=(player.animTime||0)+dt*(4+9*(player.moveBlend||0));player.armKick=Math.max(0,(player.armKick||0)-dt);player.acidKick=Math.max(0,(player.acidKick||0)-dt);player.tailKick=Math.max(0,(player.tailKick||0)-dt);player.hurtKick=Math.max(0,(player.hurtKick||0)-dt);player.dashKick=Math.max(0,(player.dashKick||0)-dt);encounterTimer=Math.max(0,encounterTimer-dt);focusHintTimer=Math.max(0,focusHintTimer-dt);if(focusHintTimer<=0){$('focusHint').style.display='none';$('focusHint').classList.remove('coachStrong')}updateCoach();player.inv=Math.max(0,player.inv-dt);player.hurtBlink=Math.max(0,(player.hurtBlink||0)-dt);{const // Boss tracking is drawn in viewport coordinates, independently of radar range.
function bossEdgeGeometryV412(e){
  if(!running||ended||e.dead||e.hp<=0)return null;
  const sx=e.x-cameraLeft(),sy=e.y-cameraTop(),body=enemyDrawSize(e)*.5;
  if(sx+body>=0&&sx-body<=W&&sy+body>=0&&sy-body<=H)return null;
  const px=player.x-cameraLeft(),py=player.y-cameraTop(),dx=sx-px,dy=sy-py;
  const left=20,right=W-20,top=app.classList.contains('hudBoss')?Math.max(60,(parseFloat(app.style.getPropertyValue('--boss-art-bottom-v420'))||72)+8):60,bottom=Math.max(top+20,H-80);
  const ox=clamp(px,left,right),oy=clamp(py,top,bottom);
  const tx=dx>0?(right-ox)/dx:dx<0?(left-ox)/dx:Infinity;
  const ty=dy>0?(bottom-oy)/dy:dy<0?(top-oy)/dy:Infinity;
  const k=Math.min(tx,ty);let x=ox+dx*k,y=oy+dy*k;
  // Keep the edge marker clear of the radar and touch controls.
  const ar=app.getBoundingClientRect();
  for(const id of ['minimapWrap','farmRouteBadge','dash','ability']){
    const el=$(id);if(!el||getComputedStyle(el).display==='none')continue;
    const r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),l=(r.left-ar.left)*W/ar.width-17,rr=(r.right-ar.left)*W/ar.width+17;
    const tt=(r.top-ar.top)*H/ar.height-20,bb=(r.bottom-ar.top)*H/ar.height+20;
    if(x>=l&&x<=rr&&y>=tt&&y<=bb){
      const above=tt>=top,below=bb<=bottom;
      if(above&&below)y=Math.abs(y-tt)<Math.abs(y-bb)?tt:bb;
      else if(below)y=bb;else if(above)y=tt;else x=clamp(l,left,right);
    }
  }
  return {x,y,a:Math.atan2(dy,dx),distance:Math.round(Math.hypot(e.x-player.x,e.y-player.y)),label:e.finalBoss?'BOSS':e.nemesis?'NEMESIS':'ELITE'};
}
function drawBossEdgePointersV412(){
  if(!running||ended)return;
  nearestScratchCV426.length=0;for(const e of enemies)if(!e.dead&&e.hp>0&&(e.finalBoss||e.elite||e.nemesis||e.zoneElite))nearestScratchCV426.push(e);nearestScratchCV426.sort((a,b)=>Number(!!b.finalBoss)-Number(!!a.finalBoss));
  for(const e of nearestScratchCV426){
    const p=bossEdgeGeometryV412(e);if(!p)continue;
    const color=e.finalBoss?'#ffb36b':e.nemesis?'#de9aff':'#ffd977';
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(p.x,p.y);ctx.rotate(p.a);ctx.fillStyle=color;ctx.strokeStyle='#200c0c';ctx.lineWidth=2;
    ctx.shadowColor='#000';ctx.shadowBlur=4;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(11,0);ctx.lineTo(-8,-8);ctx.lineTo(-4,0);ctx.lineTo(-8,8);ctx.closePath();ctx.stroke();ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
    const text=p.label+' · '+p.distance;ctx.save();ctx.font='900 9px system-ui';
    const bw=Math.ceil(ctx.measureText(text).width)+14,bh=20;
    const bx=clamp(p.x-bw/2-Math.cos(p.a)*30,5,W-bw-5),by=clamp(p.y-10-Math.sin(p.a)*28,90,H-57);
    ctx.fillStyle='#120e12ed';ctx.strokeStyle=color;ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(bx,by,bw,bh,5);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.fillStyle='#fff4e5';ctx.fillText(text,bx+bw/2,by+bh/2);ctx.restore();
  }
}

function drawThreatPointers(){
  const e=focusEnemy;if(!e||e.dead||e.hp<=0||e.finalBoss||e.elite||e.nemesis||e.zoneElite)return;
  const margin=28,cx0=W*.5,cy0=H*.5,sx=e.x-cameraLeft(),sy=e.y-cameraTop();if(sx>=margin&&sx<=W-margin&&sy>=90&&sy<=H-70)return;
  const dx=e.x-player.x,dy=e.y-player.y,a=Math.atan2(dy,dx),rx=Math.max(24,W*.5-margin),ry=Math.max(24,H*.5-82);
  const scale=Math.min(rx/Math.max(.001,Math.abs(Math.cos(a))),ry/Math.max(.001,Math.abs(Math.sin(a))),Math.min(W,H)*.48);
  const cx=clamp(cx0+Math.cos(a)*scale,margin,W-margin),cy=clamp(cy0+Math.sin(a)*scale,102,H-78);
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(cx,cy);ctx.rotate(a);ctx.globalAlpha=.55+.25*Math.sin(t*6);ctx.fillStyle='#8be8ff';ctx.shadowBlur=10;ctx.shadowColor=ctx.fillStyle;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(8,0);ctx.lineTo(-4,-5);ctx.lineTo(-1,0);ctx.lineTo(-4,5);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
}
function telegraphDangerSample(tg){
  if(tg?._bio489){if(tg.done||tg.life<=0||!tg.areas.some(g=>bioAreaHitV489(g,player)))return null;return {level:tg.active?1:Math.max(.32,1-tg.life/tg.max),ang:tg.a||0,color:'#ff765f'};}
  if(!tg||tg.life<=0)return null;const urgency=clamp(1-(tg.life/Math.max(.001,tg.max||tg.life)),0,1);let hit=false,ang=0,color='#ff765f';
  if(tg.kind==='bioMelee'||tg.kind==='bioSpikes'||(tg.kind==='acidBurst'&&isBioTitanV435(tg.owner))){hit=telegraphDangerContains(tg);ang=tg.a||Math.atan2(tg.y-player.y,tg.x-player.x);}
  else if(tg.kind==='counterNull'&&Array.isArray(tg.points)){for(const p of tg.points){if(Math.hypot(player.x-p.x,player.y-p.y)<(tg.r||32)+player.r+10){hit=true;ang=Math.atan2(p.y-player.y,p.x-player.x);color='#d884ff';break}}}
  else if(['charge','frostLine','runnerDash','emberCharge','frostFan'].includes(tg.kind)){const a=tg.a||0,dx=player.x-tg.x,dy=player.y-tg.y,along=dx*Math.cos(a)+dy*Math.sin(a),side=-dx*Math.sin(a)+dy*Math.cos(a),half=tg.kind==='frostLine'?(tg.w||22)*.5:tg.kind==='charge'?18:13;hit=along>-8&&along<(tg.len||230)+16&&Math.abs(side)<half+player.r+7;ang=a;color=tg.kind==='frostLine'?'#91e8ff':'#ff7a61';}
  else if(Number.isFinite(tg.x)&&Number.isFinite(tg.y)&&Number.isFinite(tg.r)){hit=Math.hypot(player.x-tg.x,player.y-tg.y)<tg.r+player.r+10;ang=Math.atan2(tg.y-player.y,tg.x-player.x);color=(tg.kind==='acidBurst'||tg.kind==='omegaPool'||tg.kind==='acidZone'||tg.kind==='spitPool')?'#7dff68':(tg.kind==='nullZone'||tg.kind==='counterExecution')?'#d884ff':'#ff765f';}
  if(!hit)return null;const active=['acidZone','nullZone'].includes(tg.kind);return {level:active?1:Math.max(.32,urgency),ang,color};
}

function attackWarningSectorV582(x,y,r,a,half,inner=0){
 if(!(r>Math.max(0,inner)&&half>0))return null;
 const p=new Path2D(),lo=a-half,hi=a+half;

 if(inner>0){p.lineTo(x+Math.cos(hi)*inner,y+Math.sin(hi)*inner);p.arc(x,y,inner,hi,lo,true);}else p.lineTo(x,y);
 p.closePath();return p;
}
function attackWarningLaneV582(x,y,a,len,half,offset=0){
 if(!(len>0&&half>0))return null;
 const p=new Path2D(),c=Math.cos(a),s=Math.sin(a);

  const px=x+c*u-s*v,py=y+s*u+c*v;i?p.lineTo(px,py):p.moveTo(px,py);
 }p.closePath();return p;
}
// Outline the swept ellipse once. The exact union used for its fill is separate,
// so intermediate collision samples never appear as circles inside the warning.
function attackWarningRushOutlineV582(points,rx,ry,offsetY){
 const q=[];for(const p of points){const v={x:p.x/rx,y:p.y/ry},last=q[q.length-1];if(!last||Math.hypot(v.x-last.x,v.y-last.y)>1e-6)q.push(v);}
 if(q.length<2)return q.length?attackWarningCircleV582(points[0].x,points[0].y+offsetY,rx,ry):new Path2D();
 const p=new Path2D(),angle=(u,v)=>Math.atan2(v.y-u.y,v.x-u.x);
 function side(list,first){
  let a=angle(list[0],list[1]),x=list[0].x-Math.sin(a),y=list[0].y+Math.cos(a);first?p.moveTo(x,y):p.lineTo(x,y);
  for(let i=1;i<list.length-1;i++){
   const v=list[i],next=angle(v,list[i+1]),turn=Math.atan2(Math.sin(next-a),Math.cos(next-a));
   if(turn<-.00001){p.lineTo(v.x-Math.sin(a),v.y+Math.cos(a));p.arc(v.x,v.y,1,a+Math.PI/2,a+Math.PI/2+turn,true);}
   else{const divisor=Math.max(.01,1+Math.cos(turn));p.lineTo(v.x-(Math.sin(a)+Math.sin(next))/divisor,v.y+(Math.cos(a)+Math.cos(next))/divisor);}
   a=next;
  }
  const end=list[list.length-1];p.lineTo(end.x-Math.sin(a),end.y+Math.cos(a));p.arc(end.x,end.y,1,a+Math.PI/2,a-Math.PI/2,true);
 }
 side(q,true);side(q.slice().reverse(),false);p.closePath();
 const world=new Path2D();world.addPath(p,new DOMMatrix([rx,0,0,ry,0,offsetY]));return world;
}

// One upper-left key light; cached alpha silhouettes, never oval placeholders.
const shadowMasksV421=new WeakMap();
const playerShadowSurfaceV421=document.createElement('canvas');
const playerShadowFadeV421=document.createElement('canvas');
function shadowMaskV421(im){
 if(im?._frozenShadowMaskV518)return im._frozenShadowMaskV518;
 let m=shadowMasksV421.get(im);if(m)return m;
 if(!im?.naturalWidth)return null;
 const crop=im._spriteCrop||[0,0,im.naturalWidth,im.naturalHeight],res=Math.min(256,Math.max(crop[2],crop[3]));
 const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(res*crop[2]/Math.max(crop[2],crop[3]))),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(res*crop[3]/Math.max(crop[2],crop[3]))),pad=8;
 const make=()=>{const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=w+pad*2;c.height=h+pad*2;return c;};
 const hard=make(),c=hard.getContext('2d');c.drawImage(im._approvedSurfaceV516||im,...crop,pad,pad,w,h);c.globalCompositeOperation='source-in';c.fillStyle='#02050a';c.fillRect(0,0,hard.width,hard.height);
 const soft=make(),sc=soft.getContext('2d');sc.filter='blur(2px)';sc.drawImage(hard,0,0);sc.filter='none';
 const cast=make(),cc=cast.getContext('2d');cc.drawImage(soft,0,0);cc.globalCompositeOperation='destination-in';
 const g=cc.createLinearGradient(0,pad,0,pad+h);g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(0,0,0,.30)');g.addColorStop(.55,'rgba(0,0,0,.62)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,0,0,.90)');cc.fillStyle=g;cc.fillRect(0,0,cast.width,cast.height);
 const contact=make(),ac=contact.getContext('2d');ac.drawImage(soft,0,0);ac.globalCompositeOperation='destination-in';
 const ag=ac.createLinearGradient(0,pad,0,pad+h);ag.addColorStop(0,'transparent');ag.addColorStop(.60,'transparent');ag.addColorStop(.85,'rgba(0,0,0,.6)');ag.addColorStop(1,'black');ac.fillStyle=ag;ac.fillRect(0,0,contact.width,contact.height);
 m={soft,cast,contact,hard,w,h,pad};shadowMasksV421.set(im,m);return m;
}
function maskPartV421(c,im,x,y,size,rot=0,sx=1,sy=1){
 const m=shadowMaskV421(im);if(!m)return;
 const s=size/Math.max(m.w,m.h);c.save();c.translate(x,y);c.rotate(rot);c.scale(sx,sy);
 c.drawImage(m.soft,(-m.w/2-m.pad)*s,(-m.h/2-m.pad)*s,(m.w+2*m.pad)*s,(m.h+2*m.pad)*s);c.restore();
}
function castSpriteShadowV421(im,x,groundY,size,rot=0,sx=1,sy=1,opacity=.58){
 const m=shadowMaskV421(im);if(!m)return;
 const scale=size/Math.max(m.w,m.h),bottom=m.h*scale*Math.abs(sy)*.47;
 ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='multiply';ctx.globalAlpha=opacity;ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;ctx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';
 ctx.translate(x,groundY);ctx.transform(1,0,-.40,-.23,.40*bottom,.23*bottom);ctx.rotate(rot);ctx.scale(sx,sy);
 ctx.drawImage(m.soft,(-m.w/2-m.pad)*scale,(-m.h/2-m.pad)*scale,(m.w+2*m.pad)*scale,(m.h+2*m.pad)*scale);ctx.restore();
 // Preserve the actual alpha of the lower legs/base at the attachment point.
 ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='multiply';ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(.65,opacity);ctx.translate(x,groundY-bottom);ctx.rotate(rot);ctx.scale(sx,sy);

}

const enemyShadowCompositeV425=new WeakMap();
function enemyShadowSpriteV425(im){
